IF Not EXISTS (SELECT * FROM dbo.sysobjects WHERE id = OBJECT_ID(N'[sp_Acc_Product_SelectAll]') AND OBJECTPROPERTY(id, N'IsProcedure') = 1)
	exec('Create PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_Acc_Product_SelectAll] AS BEGIN	print 111 	end')
GO

ALTER PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_Acc_Product_SelectAll]
	@SearchString nvarchar(max),--sample data => %name1 %name2 %name3% name4%
	@SearchOption smallint,
	@Groups int,
	@guid uniqueidentifier=null,
	@Status bit = null,
	@StartWord nvarchar(50)='',--sample data=>'word%' filter just on name
	@EndWord nvarchar(50)='',--SampleData=>'%word' filter just on name
	@depotGuid uniqueidentifier=null,
	@isTax bit=null,
	@masterGuid uniqueidentifier=null,
	@isLoadJustParent bit='true',
	@maxCount int=null,
	@CreationDate datetime=null,
	@HasDiscount bit=null,
	@TaxClassGuid uniqueidentifier=null,
	@CashierAccessGroups int=-1,
	@IsSpecial bit=null
AS
BEGIN
	set @maxCount=ISNULL(@maxCount,9999999)
	declare @SearchStringCount smallint
	if (LTrim(isnull(@SearchString,''))<>'')
		SELECT @SearchStringCount=Count(1) 
		FROM STRING_SPLIT(@SearchString,' ')

	DECLARE @PrdGuid TABLE 
	(
		PrdGuid	uniqueIdentifier NOT NULL
		PRIMARY KEY (PrdGuid)
	); 

	if (not @guid is null)
	begin
		set @SearchOption=10
		insert into @PrdGuid(PrdGuid) values(@guid)
	end
	else if (isnull(@SearchString,'')='')
	begin
		insert into @PrdGuid
		select Guid
		from Acc_Product
	end
	else if (@SearchOption=0)--All
	begin
		insert into @PrdGuid
		SELECT ixp.EntityGuid 
		FROM [Acc_IxProductAllFields] as ixp inner join Acc_FullTextWords  as ftw 
			on ftw.Id=ixp.WordId inner join 
			(
				SELECT value FROM STRING_SPLIT(@SearchString,' ')
			) as searchStringTbl on ftw.word like searchStringTbl.value 
		group by ixp.EntityGuid
		having count(distinct (convert(nvarchar(50),ixp.EntityGuid)+searchStringTbl.value))=@SearchStringCount
	end
	else if (@SearchOption=1)--Code Or Name
	begin
		insert into @PrdGuid
		SELECT ixp.EntityGuid 
		FROM [dbo].[Acc_IxProductNameAndCodeField] as ixp inner join Acc_FullTextWords  as ftw 
			on ftw.Id=ixp.WordId inner join 
			(
				SELECT value FROM STRING_SPLIT(@SearchString,' ')
			) as searchStringTbl on ftw.word like searchStringTbl.value 
		group by ixp.EntityGuid
		having count(distinct (convert(nvarchar(50),ixp.EntityGuid)+searchStringTbl.value))=@SearchStringCount
	end
	else if (@SearchOption=2)--Just Name
	begin
		insert into @PrdGuid
		SELECT ixp.EntityGuid 
		FROM [dbo].[Acc_IxProductNameField] as ixp inner join Acc_FullTextWords  as ftw 
			on ftw.Id=ixp.WordId inner join 
			(
				SELECT value FROM STRING_SPLIT(@SearchString,' ')
			) as searchStringTbl on ftw.word like searchStringTbl.value 
		group by ixp.EntityGuid
		having count(distinct (convert(nvarchar(50),ixp.EntityGuid)+searchStringTbl.value))=@SearchStringCount
	end
	else if (@SearchOption=3)--Just Code
	begin
		insert into @PrdGuid
		SELECT ixp.EntityGuid 
		FROM [dbo].[Acc_IxProductCodeField] as ixp inner join Acc_FullTextWords  as ftw 
			on ftw.Id=ixp.WordId inner join 
			(
				SELECT value FROM STRING_SPLIT(@SearchString,' ')
			) as searchStringTbl on ftw.word like searchStringTbl.value 
		group by ixp.EntityGuid
		having count(distinct (convert(nvarchar(50),ixp.EntityGuid)+searchStringTbl.value))=@SearchStringCount
	end

	insert into @PrdGuid
		select	Acc_Barcode.PrdGuid
		from	Acc_Barcode
		inner join 
			(
				SELECT value FROM STRING_SPLIT(@SearchString,' ')
			) as searchStringTbl on  BarCode like searchStringTbl.value
			group by Acc_Barcode.PrdGuid
			having count(distinct (convert(nvarchar(50),Acc_Barcode.PrdGuid)+searchStringTbl.value))=@SearchStringCount
	except
		select PrdGuid  from  @PrdGuid
	
	if (@Groups>0)
		delete @PrdGuid
		from 
		(
			select PrdGuid as Guid
			from @PrdGuid as x
			except
			select Acc_Product.Guid  as Guid
			from	Acc_Product 
			inner join Acc_ProductGroupRelation on Acc_ProductGroupRelation.ProductGuid=Acc_Product.Guid
			inner join StimulQuery on StimulQuery.Guid=Acc_ProductGroupRelation.GroupGuid and StimulQuery.RefrencedId=@Groups
		) as deleteItes 
		inner join @PrdGuid as allGuids on allGuids.PrdGuid = deleteItes.Guid

	if(@CashierAccessGroups>=0)
		delete @PrdGuid
			from 
			(
				select PrdGuid as Guid
				from @PrdGuid as x
				except
				select Acc_Product.Guid  as Guid
				from	Acc_Product 
				inner join Acc_ProductGroupRelation on Acc_ProductGroupRelation.ProductGuid=Acc_Product.Guid
				inner join StimulQuery on StimulQuery.Guid=Acc_ProductGroupRelation.GroupGuid and StimulQuery.RefrencedId=@CashierAccessGroups
			) as deleteItes 
			inner join @PrdGuid as allGuids on allGuids.PrdGuid = deleteItes.Guid
	

	SELECT    *
		, pivot_table.[1] As Sell1
		, pivot_table.[2] As Sell2
	FROM   
	(
		SELECT	  top(@maxCount) Acc_Product.Guid			 
				, Acc_Product.Modified As Modified
				, Acc_Product.IdProduct As IdProduct
				, Acc_Product.Code As Code
				, Acc_Product.Description As Description
				, Acc_Product.DiscountPercent As DiscountPercent
				, Acc_Product.IsTax			  
				, Acc_Product.Tags As Tags
				, Acc_Product.Type As Type
				, Acc_Product.Name As Name
				, Acc_Product.Pic As Pic
				, Acc_Product.Special
				, Acc_Product.Status	
				, Acc_Product.V2Qt As V2Qt
				, Acc_Product.GuidVahed As GuidVahed
				, Acc_Product.V2Guid As V2Guid
				, Acc_Product.ImageList As ImageList
				, ISNULL(prdAnbar.OrderPoint,0) As OrderPoint
				, CAST(ISNULL(prdAnbar.AmountSum,0) As real) As Amount
				, Acc_ProductVahed.Name as VahedName
				, Acc_Product.ServerStatus as ServerStatus
				, Acc_Product.Kind
				, Acc_Product.MasterGuid
				, Acc_Product.Weight
				, Acc_Product.TaxGuid
				, ISNULL(Acc_ProductGroup.Name,'') as GroupName
				, ISNULL(Acc_ProductGroup.ShortName,'') as GroupShortName
				, ISNULL(Acc_ProductPriceRelation.Price,0) As Price
				, ISNULL(Acc_ProductPriceLevel.Ordinal,0) As Ordinal
				, ISNULL(Acc_Product.NameForDisplay,'') As NameForDisplay
				, Acc_Product.DiscountStartDate
				, Acc_Product.DiscountEndDate
				, Acc_Product.DiscountPrice
				, Acc_Product.IsDependentOnCurrency
		FROM [Dbo].[Acc_Product] 
		inner join @PrdGuid as prds on prds.PrdGuid=Acc_Product.Guid and 
			  (@Status is null or Status=@Status) and
			  (ISNULL(@isTax,'false')='false' or Acc_Product.IsTax='true') and
			  (@CreationDate is null or Acc_Product.CreateDate>=@CreationDate) and
			  (isnull(@StartWord,'')='' or Name like @StartWord) and
			  (isnull(@EndWord,'')='' or Name like @EndWord) and
			  (ISNULL(@HasDiscount,'false')='false' or Acc_Product.DiscountPercent>0) and
			  (ISNULL(@TaxClassGuid,'00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000')='00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000' or Acc_Product.TaxGuid=@TaxClassGuid) and
			  (@IsSpecial is null or Special=@IsSpecial)
		left outer join 
		(
			select	sum(Amount) as AmountSum
					,PrdGuid
					,SUM(Min) As OrderPoint
			from Acc_PrdAnbarRelation
			where (@depotGuid is null or Acc_PrdAnbarRelation.AnbarGuid=@depotGuid)
			group by PrdGuid
		) as prdAnbar on Acc_Product.Guid=prdAnbar.PrdGuid
		inner join Acc_ProductVahed on Acc_ProductVahed.Guid=Acc_Product.GuidVahed
		left outer join Acc_ProductGroupRelation on Acc_ProductGroupRelation.ProductGuid=Acc_Product.Guid and Acc_ProductGroupRelation.Ordinal=1
		left outer join Acc_ProductGroup on Acc_ProductGroup.Guid=Acc_ProductGroupRelation.GroupGuid
		left outer join Acc_ProductPriceRelation on Acc_ProductPriceRelation.ProductGuid=Acc_Product.Guid
		left outer join Acc_ProductPriceLevel on Acc_ProductPriceLevel.Guid=Acc_ProductPriceRelation.PriceLevelGuid
	) p 
	PIVOT(
		Max(Price) 
		FOR Ordinal IN ([1],[2])
	) AS pivot_table
END
Go

-- ==========================================================================================
IF Not EXISTS (SELECT * FROM dbo.sysobjects WHERE id = OBJECT_ID(N'[sp_ProductsAnbarGardani_Details]') AND OBJECTPROPERTY(id, N'IsProcedure') = 1)
	exec('Create PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_ProductsAnbarGardani_Details] AS BEGIN	print 111 	end')
GO

ALTER Procedure [dbo].[sp_ProductsAnbarGardani_Details]
	@MasterGuid uniqueidentifier=null,
	@DateM1 Datetime,
	@Type smallint=2 --Movaghat
As
Begin
	declare @TempAnbarRelation Table
	(
		ProductGuid uniqueidentifier,
		DepotGuid uniqueidentifier,
		Amount decimal(18,3)
		UNIQUE CLUSTERED (ProductGuid,DepotGuid)
	)

	insert into @TempAnbarRelation
	select    Acc_Product.Guid as ProductGuid
			, Acc_Depot.Guid as DepotGuid
			, 0 As Amount
	from Acc_Product
	cross join Acc_Depot

	update @TempAnbarRelation
	set Amount=ISNULL(Acc_PrdAnbarRelation.Amount,0)
	from @TempAnbarRelation as t
	inner join Acc_PrdAnbarRelation on Acc_PrdAnbarRelation.PrdGuid=t.ProductGuid and Acc_PrdAnbarRelation.AnbarGuid=t.DepotGuid

	Declare @T Table (
			 PRDGuid uniqueidentifier
			,PRDCode nvarchar(max)
			,PRDName nvarchar(max)
			,VahedName nvarchar(max)
			,DPTGuid Uniqueidentifier
			,DPTName nvarchar(max)
			,amount Float
			,UnitPrice decimal(18,3)
			,RealAmount float
			,Kasr float
			,Ezafe float
			,ISAnbargardani bit
			,Guid uniqueidentifier null
			,LastAnbarGardaniDate datetime null
			,SecondaryBarcodeCount int null
			,FirstSanadDate datetime null
			,LastSanadDate datetime null
			,GroupName nvarchar(500) null
			 UNIQUE CLUSTERED (PRDGuid,DPTGuid)
			)
	
	insert Into @T (
					PRDGuid,
					PRDCode,
					PRDName,
					VahedName,
					DPTGuid,
					DPTName,
					amount,
					UnitPrice,
					RealAmount,
					Kasr,
					Ezafe,
					ISAnbargardani,
					GroupName
					) 
	SELECT	dbo.Acc_Product.Guid as PRDGuid,
			dbo.Acc_Product.IdProduct as PRDCode,
			dbo.Acc_Product.name as PRDName, 
			v1Tbl.Name,
			dbo.Acc_Depot.Guid as DPTGuid,
			dbo.Acc_Depot.name AS DPTName,
			0 as amount,
			0 as UnitPrice,
			0 as RealAmount,
			0 as Kasr,
			0 as Ezafe,
			'False',
			ISNULL(Acc_ProductGroup.ShortName,'')
	FROM	dbo.Acc_Product 
			inner join @TempAnbarRelation as AnbarRelation on AnbarRelation.ProductGuid=Acc_Product.Guid
			INNER JOIN dbo.Acc_Depot ON AnbarRelation.DepotGuid = dbo.Acc_Depot.Guid 
			inner join Acc_ProductVahed as v1Tbl on v1Tbl.Guid=Acc_Product.GuidVahed
			left outer join Acc_ProductGroupRelation on Acc_ProductGroupRelation.ProductGuid=Acc_Product.Guid and Acc_ProductGroupRelation.Ordinal=1
			left outer join Acc_ProductGroup on Acc_ProductGroup.Guid=Acc_ProductGroupRelation.GroupGuid
				
	-- در حالت ویرایش انبارگردانی، موجودی کالا در سطرهای قبل از انبارگردانی را از کاردکس استخراج می شود
	Create table #Z(
					PRDGuid Uniqueidentifier,
					DPTGuid uniqueidentifier,
					RemQT float,
					RemUnitPrice decimal(18,3)
					)

	insert Into #Z (
					PRDGuid,
					DPTGuid,
					RemQT,
					RemUnitPrice
					) 
	select  ProductGuid,
			DepotGuid,
			RemQT,
			RemUnitPrice
	from 
		(Select ProductGuid,
				DepotGuid,
				RemQT+AmaniCount As RemQT,
				RemUnitPrice,
				rank() over (partition By ProductGuid,DepotGuid order by datem desc,ordinal desc) as rank
			From Acc_Kardex 
			where DateM<@DateM1
		) as rankedItems
	where rankedItems.rank=1
	
	Update @T
	Set Amount=isnull(X.RemQT,0),UnitPrice=isnull(X.RemUnitPrice,0)
	From(
			Select * from #Z
		) as X
	Where [@T].[PRDGuid]=X.PRDGuid and [@T].[DPTGuid]=X.DPTGuid	
	if (@MasterGuid is not null)
		begin
			UPDATE @T 
			SET UnitPrice = X.UnitPrice,
				RealAmount=[@T].Amount-X.QTKasr+X.QTEzafe,
				Kasr=X.QTKasr,
				Ezafe=X.QTEzafe,
				Guid=x.Guid
			FROM (
					SELECT PrdGuid,DptGuid,UnitPrice,QTKasr,QTEzafe,Guid
					FROM Acc_PrdAnbarGardaniDet where MasterGuid=@MasterGuid
				 ) as X
			WHERE 
				X.PrdGuid = [@T].PRDGuid and X.DptGuid=[@T].DPTGuid

			Update @T Set ISAnbargardani='True' where RealAmount>0 or Kasr>0 or Ezafe>0
		END

	update @T
	Set LastAnbarGardaniDate=X.Date
	From
	(
		Select    Max(Acc_PrdAnbarGardani.DateM) As Date
				, Acc_PrdAnbarGardaniDet.PrdGuid
				, Acc_PrdAnbarGardaniDet.DptGuid
		from Acc_PrdAnbarGardaniDet
		inner join Acc_PrdAnbarGardani on Acc_PrdAnbarGardani.Guid=Acc_PrdAnbarGardaniDet.MasterGuid
		group by  Acc_PrdAnbarGardaniDet.DptGuid
				, Acc_PrdAnbarGardaniDet.PrdGuid
	) As X
	inner join @T As temp on temp.PRDGuid=x.PrdGuid and temp.DptGuid=x.DptGuid

	update @T
	set SecondaryBarcodeCount=Counter
	From
	(
		Select    PrdGuid
				, COUNT(*) As Counter
		from Acc_Barcode
		group by  PrdGuid
	) As X
	inner join @T As temp on temp.PRDGuid=x.PrdGuid

	if(@Type=6) --Ekhtetam
	begin
		update @T
		set   FirstSanadDate=MinDateKardex.MinDate
		from
		(
			select    MIN(DateM) As MinDate
					, ProductGuid
					, DepotGuid
			From Acc_Kardex
			group by ProductGuid,DepotGuid
		) As MinDateKardex inner join @T As t on t.PRDGuid=MinDateKardex.ProductGuid and t.DPTGuid=MinDateKardex.DepotGuid


		update @T
		set   LastSanadDate=MaxDateKardex.MaxDate
		from
		(
			select    Max(DateM) As MaxDate
					, ProductGuid
					, DepotGuid
			From Acc_Kardex
			group by ProductGuid,DepotGuid
		) As MaxDateKardex inner join @T As t on t.PRDGuid=MaxDateKardex.ProductGuid and t.DPTGuid=MaxDateKardex.DepotGuid

	end

	Drop Table #Z
	Select * from @T As T
	inner join Acc_Product on T.PRDGuid=Acc_Product.Guid
	inner join Acc_Depot on T.DPTGuid=Acc_Depot.Guid
	Where (Acc_Product.Status='true' and Acc_Depot.Status='true' and Acc_Product.Type=0)
			or T.Ezafe<>0 
			or T.Kasr<>0
	order by PRDName
End
GO

ALTER PROCEDURE [dbo].[SP_PrdAnbarGardani_Save] 
	@Guid uniqueidentifier,
	@dateM datetime,
	@dateSabt datetime,
	@number int,
	@sanadNumber int,
	@sanadSt smallint,
	@KasrDebitTafsilGuid uniqueidentifier = null,
	@KasrDebitMoeinGuid uniqueidentifier,
	@KasrCreditTafsilGuid uniqueidentifier = null,
	@KasrCreditMoeinGuid uniqueidentifier,
	@EzafeDebitTafsilGuid uniqueidentifier = null,
	@EzafeCreditTafsilGuid uniqueidentifier = null,
	@EzafeDebitMoeinGuid uniqueidentifier,
	@EzafeCreditMoeinGuid uniqueidentifier,
	@kasrSum decimal,
	@ezafeSum decimal,
	@cashierGuid uniqueidentifier,
	@desc nvarchar(max)=''
AS
BEGIN
		Declare @OKasrDebitTafsilGuid uniqueidentifier
		Declare @OKasrCreditTafsilGuid uniqueidentifier
		Declare @OEzafeDebitTafsilGuid uniqueidentifier
		Declare @OEzafeCreditTafsilGuid uniqueidentifier
		Declare @OKasrDebitMoeinGuid uniqueidentifier
		Declare @OKasrCreditMoeinGuid uniqueidentifier
		Declare @OEzafeDebitMoeinGuid uniqueidentifier
		Declare @OEzafeCreditMoeinGuid uniqueidentifier
		Declare @OKasrSum decimal(18,3)
		Declare @OEzafeSum decimal(18,3)

		Select @OKasrDebitTafsilGuid= KasrDebitTafsilGuid,
			   @OKasrCreditTafsilGuid= KasrCreditTafsilGuid,
			   @OEzafeDebitTafsilGuid= EzafeDebitTafsilGuid,
			   @OEzafeCreditTafsilGuid= EzafeCreditTafsilGuid,
			   @OKasrDebitMoeinGuid =KasrDebitMoeinGuid,
			   @OKasrCreditMoeinGuid =KasrCreditMoeinGuid,
			   @OEzafeDebitMoeinGuid =EzafeDebitMoeinGuid,
			   @OEzafeCreditMoeinGuid=EzafeCreditMoeinGuid,
			   @OKasrSum=KasrSum,
			   @OEzafeSum=EzafeSum 
		from  Acc_PrdAnbarGardani 
		where guid= @Guid

	UPDATE [dbo].[Acc_PrdAnbarGardani]
		   SET [Modified] = GETDATE()
			  ,[DateM] = @dateM
			  ,[DateSabt] = @dateSabt
			  ,[Number] = @number
			  ,[SanadNo] = @sanadNumber
			  ,[SanadSt] = @sanadSt
			  ,[KasrDebitTafsilGuid] = @KasrDebitTafsilGuid
			  ,[KasrDebitMoeinGuid] = @KasrDebitMoeinGuid
			  ,[KasrCreditTafsilGuid] = @KasrCreditTafsilGuid
			  ,[KasrCreditMoeinGuid] = @KasrCreditMoeinGuid
			  ,[EzafeDebitTafsilGuid] = @EzafeDebitTafsilGuid
			  ,[EzafeCreditTafsilGuid] = @EzafeCreditTafsilGuid
			  ,[EzafeDebitMoeinGuid] = @EzafeDebitMoeinGuid
			  ,[EzafeCreditMoeinGuid] = @EzafeCreditMoeinGuid
			  ,[KasrSum] = @KasrSum
			  ,[EzafeSum] = @EzafeSum
			  ,[UserGuid] = @cashierGuid
			  ,[Description] = @desc
		 WHERE [Guid] = @Guid
	

	if @@ROWCOUNT = 0
	begin
		INSERT INTO [dbo].[Acc_PrdAnbarGardani]
           ([Guid]
           ,[Modified]
           ,[DateM]
           ,[DateSabt]
           ,[Number]
           ,[SanadNo]
           ,[SanadSt]
           ,[KasrDebitTafsilGuid]
           ,[KasrDebitMoeinGuid]
           ,[KasrCreditTafsilGuid]
           ,[KasrCreditMoeinGuid]
           ,[EzafeDebitTafsilGuid]
           ,[EzafeCreditTafsilGuid]
           ,[EzafeDebitMoeinGuid]
           ,[EzafeCreditMoeinGuid]
           ,[KasrSum]
           ,[EzafeSum]
           ,[UserGuid]
           ,[Description])
		VALUES
           (@Guid
           ,GetDate()
           ,@dateM
           ,@dateSabt
           ,@number
           ,@sanadNumber
           ,@sanadSt
           ,@KasrDebitTafsilGuid
           ,@KasrDebitMoeinGuid
           ,@KasrCreditTafsilGuid
           ,@KasrCreditMoeinGuid
           ,@EzafeDebitTafsilGuid
           ,@EzafeCreditTafsilGuid
           ,@EzafeDebitMoeinGuid
           ,@EzafeCreditMoeinGuid
           ,@KasrSum
           ,@EzafeSum
           ,@cashierGuid
           ,@desc)
	end
End
GO

	exec('Create PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_Acc_Product_GetByCode] AS BEGIN	print 111 	end')
GO

ALTER PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_Acc_Product_GetByCode]
	@code nvarchar(100),
	@depotGuid uniqueidentifier=null,
	@status bit=null
AS
BEGIN
	SELECT    *
		, pivot_table.[1] As Sell1
		, pivot_table.[2] As Sell2
	FROM   
	(
		select    Acc_Product.Guid
				, Acc_Product.Modified
				, Acc_Product.IdProduct
				, Acc_Product.Code
				, Acc_Product.Description
				, Acc_Product.DiscountPercent
				, Acc_Product.IsTax
				, Acc_Product.Tags
				, Acc_Product.Type
				, Acc_Product.Name
				, Acc_Product.Pic
				, Acc_Product.Special
				, Acc_Product.Status
				, Acc_Product.V2Guid
				, Acc_Product.V2Qt
				, Acc_Product.GuidVahed
				, Acc_Product.ImageList
				, Acc_Product.OrderPoint
				, Acc_Product.ServerStatus
				, Acc_ProductVahed.Name As VahedName
				, Acc_Product.Kind
				, CAST(ISNULL(prdAnbar.AmountSum,0) As real) As Amount
				, Acc_Product.MasterGuid
				, Acc_Product.Weight
				, Acc_Product.TaxGuid
				, ISNULL(Acc_ProductGroup.Name,'') as GroupName
				, ISNULL(Acc_ProductGroup.ShortName,'') as GroupShortName
				, ISNULL(Acc_ProductPriceRelation.Price,0) As Price
				, ISNULL(Acc_ProductPriceLevel.Ordinal,0) As Ordinal
				, ISNULL(Acc_Product.NameForDisplay,'') As NameForDisplay
				, Acc_Product.DiscountStartDate
				, Acc_Product.DiscountEndDate
				, Acc_Product.DiscountPrice
				, Acc_Product.IsDependentOnCurrency
		From Acc_Product 
		inner join Acc_ProductVahed on Acc_Product.GuidVahed=Acc_ProductVahed.Guid 
		left outer join 
				(
					select	sum(Amount) as AmountSum
							,PrdGuid
					from Acc_PrdAnbarRelation
					where (@depotGuid is null or Acc_PrdAnbarRelation.AnbarGuid=@depotGuid)
					group by PrdGuid
				) as prdAnbar on Acc_Product.Guid=prdAnbar.PrdGuid
		left outer join Acc_ProductGroupRelation on Acc_ProductGroupRelation.ProductGuid=Acc_Product.Guid and Acc_ProductGroupRelation.Ordinal=1
		left outer join Acc_ProductGroup on Acc_ProductGroup.Guid=Acc_ProductGroupRelation.GroupGuid
		left outer join Acc_ProductPriceRelation on Acc_ProductPriceRelation.ProductGuid=Acc_Product.Guid
		left outer join Acc_ProductPriceLevel on Acc_ProductPriceLevel.Guid=Acc_ProductPriceRelation.PriceLevelGuid
		Where Acc_Product.IdProduct=@code and 
			  (@status is null or Acc_Product.Status=@status)
	) p 
	PIVOT(
		Max(Price) 
		FOR Ordinal IN ([1],[2])
	) AS pivot_table
END
GO

IF Not EXISTS (SELECT * FROM dbo.sysobjects WHERE id = OBJECT_ID(N'[sp_acc_Product_UpdateGroups]') AND OBJECTPROPERTY(id, N'IsProcedure') = 1)
	exec('Create PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_acc_Product_UpdateGroups] AS BEGIN	print 111 	end')
GO

ALTER Procedure [dbo].[sp_acc_Product_UpdateGroups]
	@RefId int
as
begin
	delete Acc_ProductGroupRelation
	from Acc_ProductGroupRelation
	inner join StimulQuery on StimulQuery.Guid=Acc_ProductGroupRelation.ProductGuid
	where StimulQuery.RefrencedId=@RefId

	INSERT INTO [dbo].[Acc_ProductGroupRelation] ([GroupGuid] ,[ProductGuid],[Ordinal])
	Select CAST(SecondKey as uniqueidentifier),Guid,Id 
	from StimulQuery
	where StimulQuery.RefrencedId=@RefId

	Update Acc_Product 
	set Modified=GETDATE(),
		ServerStatus=0
	from Acc_Product
	inner join StimulQuery on StimulQuery.Guid=Acc_Product.Guid
	where StimulQuery.RefrencedId=@RefId
end
GO

	exec('Create PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_Acc_PrdAnbarRelation_UpdateAmount] AS BEGIN	print 111 	end')
GO

ALTER PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_Acc_PrdAnbarRelation_UpdateAmount]
	@qt float,
	@prdGuid uniqueidentifier,
	@depotGuid uniqueidentifier
AS
BEGIN
	declare @masterGuid uniqueidentifier = null
	select @masterGuid=MasterGuid
	from Acc_Product
	where Guid=@prdGuid

	update Acc_PrdAnbarRelation set Amount=ROUND(Amount+@qt,3)
	where PrdGuid =@prdGuid and 
		  AnbarGuid=@depotGuid and
		  Amount<>ROUND(Amount+@qt,3)

	if(@@ROWCOUNT<=0) 
	begin
		If Not EXISTS(select Top(1) PrdGuid from Acc_PrdAnbarRelation where PrdGuid=@prdGuid and AnbarGuid=@depotGuid)
		begin
			INSERT INTO [dbo].[Acc_PrdAnbarRelation]
			   ([PrdGuid]
			   ,[AnbarGuid]
			   ,[Min]
			   ,[Amount]
			   ,[Modified])
			VALUES
			   (@prdGuid
			   ,@depotGuid
			   ,0
			   ,@qt
			   ,GETDATE())
		end
	end

	Update Acc_Product
	set Version=Version+1
	Where Guid=@prdGuid

	if(ISNULL(@masterGuid,'00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000')<>'00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000')
	begin
		update Acc_PrdAnbarRelation set Amount=ROUND(Amount+@qt,3)
		where PrdGuid =@masterGuid and 
			  AnbarGuid=@depotGuid and
			  Amount<>ROUND(Amount+@qt,3)

		if(@@ROWCOUNT<>0) return
		If Not EXISTS(select Top(1) PrdGuid from Acc_PrdAnbarRelation where PrdGuid=@masterGuid and AnbarGuid=@depotGuid)
		begin
			INSERT INTO [dbo].[Acc_PrdAnbarRelation]
			   ([PrdGuid]
			   ,[AnbarGuid]
			   ,[Min]
			   ,[Amount]
			   ,[Modified])
			VALUES
			   (@masterGuid
			   ,@depotGuid
			   ,0
			   ,@qt
			   ,GETDATE())
		end
	end
END
GO

Alter PROCEDURE sp_Acc_CashierLog_Insert
	@SanadGuid uniqueidentifier,
	@SanadType smallint,
	@Description nvarchar(MAX)='',
	@dateM datetime,
	@CashierGuid uniqueidentifier,
	@Ser_OLD nvarchar(MAX)='',
	@Ser_New nvarchar(MAX)='',
	@Action smallint
AS
BEGIN
	INSERT INTO [dbo].[Acc_CashiersLog]
           ([Guid]
           ,[Modified]
           ,[SanadGuid]
           ,[SanadType]
           ,[Title]
           ,[DateM]
           ,[CashierGuid]
           ,[Action])
     VALUES
           (NEWID()
           ,GETDATE()
           ,@SanadGuid
           ,@SanadType
           ,ISNULL(@Description,'')
           ,@dateM
           ,@CashierGuid
           ,@Action)
END

IF Not EXISTS (SELECT * FROM sys.objects WHERE object_id = OBJECT_ID(N'F_TafsilNumber_By_Guid') AND type in (N'FN', N'IF', N'TF', N'FS', N'FT'))
	exec('CREATE FUNCTION [dbo].[F_TafsilNumber_By_Guid](@TafsilGuid uniqueidentifier,@ordinal smallint) RETURNS int AS BEGIN RETURN null end')
go

ALTER FUNCTION [dbo].[F_TafsilNumber_By_Guid] 
(
	@TafsilGuid uniqueidentifier,
	@ordinal smallint
)
RETURNS NVarchar(500)
AS
BEGIN
	DECLARE @RESULT Nvarchar(500)

	SET @RESULT =(select Title + ' : ' + case when Prefix<=0 then CAST(CalcedNumber as NVarchar(500))
					   else CAST(CalcedNumber as NVarchar(500)) +'-'+CAST(Prefix as NVarchar(5))
					   end
					 from (
								select * , RANK() over(order by title,ordinal) as rank
								from Acc_TafsilNumbers
								where TafsilGuid=@TafsilGuid
						) as ranked 
					where rank=@ordinal
				 )
	RETURN @RESULT
END
Go

IF Not EXISTS (SELECT * FROM dbo.sysobjects WHERE id = OBJECT_ID(N'[sp_Kardex_GetPrdAmount]') AND OBJECTPROPERTY(id, N'IsProcedure') = 1)
	exec('Create PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_Kardex_GetPrdAmount] AS BEGIN	print 111 	end')
GO

ALTER PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_Kardex_GetPrdAmount]
	@prdGuid uniqueidentifier,
	@depotGuid uniqueidentifier,
	@date datetime,
	@KardexAffective bit=null
AS
BEGIN
	declare @amount float
	select	@amount= SUM(VorodQT)-SUM(KhorojQT) 
	from	Acc_Kardex
	where	ProductGuid=@prdGuid and
			DepotGuid=@depotGuid and
			DateM<@date and 
			SanadType<>10 and
			(@KardexAffective is null or KardexAffective=@KardexAffective)
	
	select ISNULL(@amount,0)
	return @amount
END
GO

ALTER PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_products_PointOrder] 
AS
BEGIN
	SELECT     Acc_Product.Guid
			 , Acc_Product.IdProduct
			 , Acc_Product.name
			 , Acc_Product.description
			 , Acc_Product.pic
			 , Acc_Product.Modified
			 , Acc_Product.Height
			 , Acc_Product.Width
			 , Acc_Product.Weight
			 , Acc_Product.GuidVahed
			 , Acc_Depot.name AS AnbarName
			 , Acc_Depot.description AS AnbarDescription
			 , Acc_ProductVahed.name AS VahedName
			 , Acc_PrdAnbarRelation.Min
			 , Acc_PrdAnbarRelation.AnbarGuid
			 , Acc_Product.Status
			 , round(Acc_PrdAnbarRelation.Amount,4) as amount
			 , ISNULL(Acc_ProductGroup.Name,'') As GroupName
			 , ISNULL(Acc_ProductGroup.Guid,'00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000') As ProductGroupGuid
	FROM Acc_Product
	inner join Acc_PrdAnbarRelation ON Acc_Product.Guid = Acc_PrdAnbarRelation.PrdGuid
	inner join Acc_Depot ON Acc_PrdAnbarRelation.AnbarGuid = Acc_Depot.Guid 
	inner join Acc_ProductVahed ON Acc_Product.GuidVahed = Acc_ProductVahed.Guid
	left outer join Acc_ProductGroupRelation on Acc_ProductGroupRelation.ProductGuid=Acc_Product.Guid and Acc_ProductGroupRelation.Ordinal=1
	left outer join Acc_ProductGroup on Acc_ProductGroupRelation.GroupGuid=Acc_ProductGroup.Guid
	where Acc_Product.Status='true' and
		  Acc_PrdAnbarRelation.Min > Acc_PrdAnbarRelation.Amount
	order by Acc_Product.Name,Acc_Depot.Name
END
GO

update Acc_Tafsil
	Set TafsilGroupGuid=x.TafsilGroupGuid,
		Modified=GETDATE(),
		ServerStatus=0
	From Acc_Tafsil
	inner join (
									select	
										Acc_CustomerGroup.GUID as Guid,
										Acc_CustomerGroup.TafsilGroupGuid as TafsilGroupGuid,
										Acc_Tafsil.Guid As TafsilGuid
									from Acc_CustomerGroup 
										 inner join Acc_Customer on Acc_Customer.GroupGuid=Acc_CustomerGroup.Guid
										 inner join Acc_Tafsil on Acc_Customer.Guid=Acc_Tafsil.Guid
									where Acc_CustomerGroup.TafsilGroupGuid<>Acc_Tafsil.TafsilGroupGuid
							) as x
					on Acc_Tafsil.Guid=x.TafsilGuid
GO

IF Not EXISTS (SELECT * FROM dbo.sysobjects WHERE id = OBJECT_ID(N'[sp_Acc_Order_GetSellMoghayerat]') AND OBJECTPROPERTY(id, N'IsProcedure') = 1)
	exec('Create Procedure [dbo].[sp_Acc_Order_GetSellMoghayerat] As Begin print 11 end')
GO

Alter PROCEDURE sp_Acc_Order_GetSellMoghayerat
AS
BEGIN
	select	Acc_Product.Name as ProductName,
		Acc_Tafsil.name as TafsilName,
		Acc_Tafsil.Guid As TafsilGuid,
		sales.sum as SellSum,
		sales.count as SellCount,
		sales.sumQt as SellQuantity,
		sales.minDate as FirstSellDate,
		sales.minDate as LastSellDate,
		backSales.sum as BackSellSum,
		backSales.count as BackSellCount,
		backSales.sumQt as BackSellQuantity,
		backSales.minDate as FirstBackSell,
		backSales.minDate as LastBackSell
from 
(
	select ProductGuid,TafsilGuid,Sum(Quantity) as sumQt,sum(RowSum) as sum,count(1) as count,min(Date) as minDate,max(date) as maxDate
	from Acc_Order inner join Acc_OrderDetail on Acc_Order.Guid=Acc_OrderDetail.MasterGuid
	where FactorType=8 
	group by ProductGuid,TafsilGuid
) as backSales
 left join 
(
	select ProductGuid,TafsilGuid,Sum(Quantity) as sumQt,sum(RowSum) as sum,count(1) as count,min(Date) as minDate,max(date) as maxDate
	from Acc_Order inner join Acc_OrderDetail on Acc_Order.Guid=Acc_OrderDetail.MasterGuid
	where FactorType=3 
	group by ProductGuid,TafsilGuid
) as sales on 
	sales.productGuid=backSales.ProductGuid and
	sales.TafsilGuid=backSales.TafsilGuid 
	inner join
	Acc_Tafsil on (Acc_Tafsil.Guid=Sales.TafsilGuid or Acc_Tafsil.Guid=backSales.TafsilGuid) inner join Acc_Product
	on (Acc_Product.Guid=backSales.ProductGuid or Acc_Product.Guid=Sales.ProductGuid)
	where backSales.sumQt>isnull(sales.sum,0) or backSales.sumQt>isnull(sales.sumQt,0) --and backSales.maxDate>'2022/03/01'
	order by backSales.minDate
END
Go

IF Not EXISTS (SELECT * FROM sys.objects WHERE object_id = OBJECT_ID(N'[dbo].[BeforeAccountNew]') AND type in (N'FN', N'IF', N'TF', N'FS', N'FT'))
	exec('CREATE FUNCTION [dbo].[BeforeAccountNew](@AsGuid uniqueidentifier, @AsType int) RETURNS Decimal(18,3) AS BEGIN RETURN null end')
go


ALTER FUNCTION [dbo].[BeforeAccountNew] 
(
	@AsGuid uniqueidentifier, 
	@AsType int
)
RETURNS Decimal(18,3)
AS
BEGIN
	DECLARE @Result Decimal(18,3)
	DECLARE @SanadDate DATETIME
	DECLARE @TafsilGuid Uniqueidentifier
	declare @Source smallint

	select @Source=CAST(Value as smallint)
	from Acc_LocalSetting
	where [Key]='Source'

	IF (@AsType = 7 or @AsType = 8 or @AsType = 4 or @AsType = 3 or @AsType = 26 or @AsType = 27)
			BEGIN
				SELECT	@TafsilGuid=[TafsilGuid] ,
						@SanadDate=Date,
						@Result=BeforeAccount
				FROM Acc_Order 
				WHERE Guid=@AsGuid
			END
		ELSE IF (@AsType =2)--Pardakht
			BEGIN
				SELECT	@TafsilGuid=[TafsilGuid] ,
						@SanadDate=Date,
						@Result=BeforeAccount
				FROM acc_Pardakht 
				WHERE Guid=@AsGuid
			END
		ELSE IF (@AsType =1)--Daryaft 
			BEGIN
				SELECT	@TafsilGuid=[TafsilGuid] ,
						@SanadDate=Date,
						@Result=BeforeAccount
				FROM acc_Reception 
				WHERE Guid=@AsGuid
			END
			
	if (@Source=1) --Accounting
	begin
		set @Result = isnull((	SELECT sum(isnull([Credit],0)-isnull([Debit],0)) as Mandeh
								FROM Acc_ViewSanadDetail
								where TafsilGuid=@TafsilGuid and 
									  DateM<@SanadDate And 
									  AsGuid<>@AsGuid
							),0)
		return @Result
	end

	RETURN @Result
END
GO

ALTER PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_Acc_ReceptionCheck_ForceUpdateChequeStatus]
	@ChequeGuid uniqueidentifier,
	@Status smallint,
	@DebitTafsilGuid uniqueidentifier=null
AS
BEGIN
	Update Acc_ReceptionCheck
	set Status = @Status,
		serverStatus=0,
		Modified=GetDate()
	Where Guid = @ChequeGuid

	if ISNULL(@DebitTafsilGuid,'00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000')<>'00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000'
		Update Acc_ReceptionCheck
		set DebitTafsilGuid = @DebitTafsilGuid,
			serverStatus=0,
			Modified=GetDate()
		Where Guid = @ChequeGuid
END
GO

IF not EXISTS (SELECT * FROM sys.views WHERE object_id = OBJECT_ID(N'[dbo].[ViewPrdSerial]'))
	exec('create VIEW [dbo].[ViewPrdSerial] as select * from Acc_Product')
GO

ALTER VIEW [dbo].[ViewPrdSerial]
AS
	select    Acc_PrdSerial.Guid
			, Acc_PrdSerial.Serial
			, Acc_PrdSerial.GarrantyCode
			, Acc_PrdSerial.StartDate
			, Acc_PrdSerial.EndDate
			, Acc_PrdSerial.ProductGuid
			, Count(case when Acc_PrdSerialDetail.IsVorod = 'true' then 1 else null end) As VorodCount
			, Count(case when Acc_PrdSerialDetail.IsVorod = 'false' then 1 else null end) As KhorojCount
			, Case when
					(
					  ISNULL(Count(case when ISNULL(Acc_PrdSerialDetail.IsVorod,'true') = 'true' then 1 else null end),0)
					- ISNULL(Count(case when ISNULL(Acc_PrdSerialDetail.IsVorod,'true') = 'false' then 1 else null end),0)
					) > 0 
			  then '1'
			  else '0'
			  end As IsMojod
			, MAX(Acc_PrdSerialDetail.Ordinal) As Ordinal 
			, Acc_PrdSerial.TechnicalInfo1
			, Acc_PrdSerial.TechnicalInfo2
			, Acc_PrdSerial.TechnicalInfo3
			, Acc_PrdSerial.Status
			, Acc_PrdSerial.ServerStatus
		from Acc_PrdSerial
		left outer join Acc_PrdSerialDetail on Acc_PrdSerialDetail.MasterGuid=Acc_PrdSerial.Guid
		left outer join
		(
			Select status,Acc_OrderDetail.Guid
			from Acc_Order
			inner join Acc_OrderDetail on Acc_OrderDetail.MasterGuid=Acc_Order.Guid
		) As OrderStatus on Acc_PrdSerialDetail.DetailGuid=OrderStatus.Guid
		where (Acc_PrdSerial.IsTransfer='true' or Acc_PrdSerialDetail.AsType in (3,4,7,8,20,21,44,45)) and
			  (Acc_PrdSerial.IsTransfer='true' or OrderStatus.Status='true') and 
			  Acc_PrdSerial.Status='true'
		Group by  Acc_PrdSerial.Guid
				, Acc_PrdSerial.Serial
				, Acc_PrdSerial.GarrantyCode
				, Acc_PrdSerial.StartDate
				, Acc_PrdSerial.EndDate
				, Acc_PrdSerial.ProductGuid
				, Acc_PrdSerial.TechnicalInfo1
				, Acc_PrdSerial.TechnicalInfo2
				, Acc_PrdSerial.TechnicalInfo3
				, Acc_PrdSerial.Status
				, Acc_PrdSerial.ServerStatus

		union All

		select    Acc_PrdSerial.Guid
				, Acc_PrdSerial.Serial
				, Acc_PrdSerial.GarrantyCode
				, Acc_PrdSerial.StartDate
				, Acc_PrdSerial.EndDate
				, Acc_PrdSerial.ProductGuid
				, Count(case when x.IsVorod = 'true' then 1 else null end) As VorodCount
				, Count(case when x.IsVorod = 'false' then 1 else null end) As KhorojCount
				, x.IsVorod As IsMojod
				, Max(x.Ordinal) As Ordinal
				, Acc_PrdSerial.TechnicalInfo1
				, Acc_PrdSerial.TechnicalInfo2
				, Acc_PrdSerial.TechnicalInfo3
				, Acc_PrdSerial.Status
				, Acc_PrdSerial.ServerStatus
			from Acc_PrdSerial
			inner join 
				(
					Select MasterGuid,AsType,IsVorod,Ordinal
					,RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY MasterGuid order by Ordinal desc) as rank
					from Acc_PrdSerialDetail
				) As x on x.MasterGuid=Acc_PrdSerial.Guid
			where (x.AsType = 24 or x.AsType = 44 or x.AsType = 45) and x.rank=1 and Acc_PrdSerial.Status='true'
			Group by  Acc_PrdSerial.Guid
				, Acc_PrdSerial.Serial
				, Acc_PrdSerial.GarrantyCode
				, Acc_PrdSerial.StartDate
				, Acc_PrdSerial.EndDate
				, Acc_PrdSerial.ProductGuid
				, x.IsVorod
				, Acc_PrdSerial.TechnicalInfo1
				, Acc_PrdSerial.TechnicalInfo2
				, Acc_PrdSerial.TechnicalInfo3
				, Acc_PrdSerial.Status
				, Acc_PrdSerial.ServerStatus
GO

IF Not EXISTS (SELECT * FROM dbo.sysobjects WHERE id = OBJECT_ID(N'[sp_Acc_PrdSerial_GetAll]') AND OBJECTPROPERTY(id, N'IsProcedure') = 1)
	exec('Create Procedure [dbo].[sp_Acc_PrdSerial_GetAll] As Begin print 11 end')
GO

ALTER PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_Acc_PrdSerial_GetAll]
	@productGuid uniqueidentifier=null,
	@serial nvarchar(100)='',
	@asType smallint=null,
	@detailGuid uniqueidentifier=null,
	@DateM datetime=null
AS
BEGIN
	Select    ViewPrdSerial.Guid
			, ViewPrdSerial.Serial
			, ViewPrdSerial.GarrantyCode
			, ViewPrdSerial.StartDate
			, ViewPrdSerial.EndDate
			, ViewPrdSerial.ProductGuid
			, SUM(ViewPrdSerial.VorodCount) As VorodCount
			, SUM(ViewPrdSerial.KhorojCount) As KhorojCount
			, 1 ^ count(distinct nullif(ViewPrdSerial.IsMojod, 1)) As IsMojod
			, MAX(ViewPrdSerial.Ordinal) As Ordinal
			, ISNULL(ViewPrdSerial.TechnicalInfo1,'') as TechnicalInfo1
			, ISNULL(ViewPrdSerial.TechnicalInfo2,'') as TechnicalInfo2
			, ISNULL(ViewPrdSerial.TechnicalInfo3,'') as TechnicalInfo3
	from ViewPrdSerial
	left outer join 
	(
		Select	  MasterGuid
				, Max(AsType) as AsType
				, MAX(DetailGuid) As DetailGuid 
				, MAX(Ordinal) As Ordinal
				, MAX(SanadDate) As SanadDate
		from Acc_PrdSerialDetail
		Group by  MasterGuid
	) As Details on Details.MasterGuid=ViewPrdSerial.Guid
	Where (@productGuid is null or ProductGuid=@productGuid) and
		  (ISNULL(@serial,'')='' or Serial=@serial) and
		  (ISNULL(@asType,0)=0 or Details.AsType=@asType) and
		  (@detailGuid is null or Details.DetailGuid=@detailGuid) and
		  (@DateM is null or Details.SanadDate<@DateM)
	group by  Serial
			, Guid
			, GarrantyCode
			, StartDate
			, EndDate
			, ProductGuid
			, TechnicalInfo1
			, TechnicalInfo2
			, TechnicalInfo3
	Order by Ordinal
END
GO

ALTER PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_Acc_TozieList_GetAll]
AS
BEGIN
	Select    Acc_TozieList.Guid As Guid
			, Acc_TozieList.Number As Number
			, Acc_TozieList.DateM As DateM
			, Acc_TozieList.DriverName As DriverName
			, Acc_TozieList.DriverMobile1 As DriverMobile1
			, Acc_TozieList.DriverMobile2 As DriverMobile2
			, Acc_TozieList.CarNumber As CarNumber
			, Acc_TozieList.Description As Description
			, Acc_TozieList.CashierGuid As CashierGuid
			, Acc_Cashier.Name As CashierName
			, COUNT(OrderSell.Guid) As SellCount
			, Sum(OrderSell.Sum) As SellSum
			, COUNT(OrderBSell.Guid) As BackSellCount
			, Sum(OrderBSell.Sum) As BackSellSum
			, COUNT(Acc_Reception.Guid) As ReceptionCount
			, Sum(Acc_Reception.Sum) As ReceptionSum
	From Acc_TozieList
	inner join Acc_Cashier on Acc_TozieList.CashierGuid=Acc_Cashier.Guid
	inner join Acc_TozieListDetails on Acc_TozieListDetails.MasterGuid=Acc_TozieList.Guid
	left outer join Acc_Order As OrderSell on Acc_TozieListDetails.AsType=3 and Acc_TozieListDetails.AsGuid=OrderSell.Guid
	left outer join Acc_Order As OrderBSell on Acc_TozieListDetails.AsType=8 and Acc_TozieListDetails.AsGuid=OrderBSell.Guid
	left outer join Acc_Reception on Acc_TozieListDetails.AsType=1 and Acc_TozieListDetails.AsGuid=Acc_Reception.Guid
	group by Acc_TozieList.Guid
			, Acc_TozieList.Number
			, Acc_TozieList.DateM
			, Acc_TozieList.DriverName
			, Acc_TozieList.DriverMobile1
			, Acc_TozieList.DriverMobile2
			, Acc_TozieList.CarNumber
			, Acc_TozieList.Description
			, Acc_TozieList.CashierGuid
			, Acc_Cashier.Name
END
GO

ALTER PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_Acc_Sanad_Save]
  @SanadNo int,
  @SanadSt smallint,
  @Date datetime,
  @Desc nvarchar(max),
  @Type smallint,
  @Guid uniqueidentifier,
  @CashierGuid uniqueidentifier,
  @StatusId smallint
AS
BEGIN
  UPDATE [dbo].[Acc_Sanad]
  SET   [SanadNo] = @SanadNo
       ,[SanadST] = @SanadSt
       ,[DateM] = @Date
       ,[Description] = @Desc
       ,[SanadType] = @Type
	   ,[StatusId]=@StatusId
  WHERE [Guid]=@Guid

  if @@ROWCOUNT=0
  begin
	INSERT INTO [dbo].[Acc_Sanad]
           ([Guid]
           ,[DateM]
           ,[Description]
           ,[SanadNo]
           ,[SanadSt]
           ,[SanadType]
           ,[UserGuid]
           ,[Modified]
           ,[Status]
           ,[Source]
		   ,[StatusId])
     VALUES
           (@Guid
           ,@Date
           ,@Desc
           ,@SanadNo
           ,@SanadSt
           ,@Type
           ,@CashierGuid
           ,GETDATE()
           ,'true'
           ,1
		   ,@StatusId)
  end
END
Go

IF Not EXISTS (SELECT * FROM dbo.sysobjects WHERE id = OBJECT_ID(N'[SP_Acc_Sanad_Update_50_Manual]') AND OBJECTPROPERTY(id, N'IsProcedure') = 1)
	exec('Create Procedure [dbo].[SP_Acc_Sanad_Update_50_Manual] As Begin print 11 end')
GO

ALTER PROCEDURE [dbo].[SP_Acc_Sanad_Update_50_Manual] 
	@ModifiedMode Smallint, --Insert=0 --Update=1 --Delete=2
	@Guid uniqueIdentifier,
	@SanadNO int,	
	@BulkQuery bit='false',
	@Type smallint=50,
	@CashierGuid uniqueidentifier=Null,
	@OLDSanadNO int=0,
	@OLDDateSH nvarchar=''
AS
BEGIN

	Declare @DateM DateTime

	Declare @AutoDesc nvarchar(max)
	Declare @DateBargeSH varchar(12)
	Declare @DateSanadSH varchar(12)
	Declare @DateMNow Datetime
	Declare @DateSHNow varchar(12)
	Set @DateMNow=getdate()
	Set @DateSHNow= (Select [dbo].[Civil2Persian] (getdate()))

	SET NOCOUNT ON;
	if (isnull(@SanadNO,0)=0)
	begin
		select @SanadNO=SanadNO,@DateM=DateM
		from Acc_Sanad
		where Guid=@Guid
	end 
	declare @AsGuid uniqueidentifier
	Select @DateM=DateM,@DateSanadSH=dbo.Civil2persian(DateM) from Acc_Sanad where Guid=@Guid
		
	if (isnull(@SanadNO,0)=0)
	begin
		set @SanadNO=(select top 1 max(sanadNO) from Acc_ViewSanadDetail where AsGuid=@Guid and ASType=@Type)
		if (isnull(@SanadNO,0)=0)
		begin
			--Insert
			set @SanadNO=(select max(sanadNO) from Acc_ViewSanadDetail)
			set @SanadNO=isnull(@SanadNO,0)+1
		end
	end
	
	update Acc_Sanad 
	set SanadNO=@SanadNO 
	where Guid=@Guid

	if(@ModifiedMode in (1)) and ((@OLDSanadNO>0) and (@SanadNO<>@OLDSanadNO)) or ((@OLDDateSH<>'') and (@OLDDateSH<>@DateSanadSH)) --Update 
			-- اگر شماره سند از داخل فرم سند تغییر کند -شماره سند را برای اتومات ها به روز کن
			-- اگر تاریخ از داخل سند تغییر کرد تاریخ را برای برگه ها به روز کن و لاگ را ثبت کن
		Begin
			Update Acc_ViewSanadDetail 
			Set SanadNO=@SanadNO,DateM=@DateM 
			where SanadNO=@OLDSanadNO 
	
			--Declare @AsGuid UniqueIdentifier
			Declare @ASType smallint
			declare cr_S cursor local for 
				SELECT	AsGuid,
						[ASType]
				FROM Acc_ViewSanadDetail
				where SanadNO=@SanadNO
			open cr_S
	
			fetch next from cr_S into @AsGuid,@ASType
			while (@@fetch_status=0)
			begin
				IF (@ASType=1)
					Begin
						select @AsGuid=Guid from Acc_Reception where Guid=@AsGuid
						Update [Acc_Reception] set SanadNO=@SanadNO where Guid=@AsGuid
						Select @DateBargeSH=dbo.Civil2persian(Date) from Acc_Reception Where Guid=@AsGuid
						if (@DateBargeSH <> @DateSanadSH)
							Begin
								Set @AutoDesc=N'ویرایش تاریخ دریافت از طریق ویرایش سند از (' + @DateBargeSH +') به (' +@DateSanadSH+')'
								Update [acc_Reception] set Date=CONVERT(VARCHAR(10),@DateM,111) + ' ' +convert(char(8), Date, 108) where Guid=@AsGuid

								Update Acc_ViewSanadDetailCover 
								set Date=CONVERT(VARCHAR(10),@DateM,111) + ' ' +convert(char(8), Date, 108) 
								where AsGuid=@AsGuid And AsTypeDocument=1

								exec sp_Acc_CashierLog_Insert @AsGuid,@ASType,@AutoDesc,@DateMNow,@CashierGuid,'','',2
							End
					End
				Else if (@ASType=2)
					Begin
						select @AsGuid=Guid from Acc_Pardakht where Guid=@AsGuid
						Update [acc_Pardakht] set SanadNO=@SanadNO where Guid=@AsGuid
						Select @DateBargeSH=dbo.Civil2persian(Date) from acc_Pardakht Where Guid=@AsGuid
						if (@DateBargeSH <> @DateSanadSH)
							Begin
								Set @AutoDesc=N'ویرایش تاریخ پرداخت از طریق ویرایش سند از (' + @DateBargeSH +') به (' +@DateSanadSH+')'
								Update [Acc_Pardakht] set Date=CONVERT(VARCHAR(10),@DateM,111) + ' ' +convert(char(8), Date, 108) where Guid=@AsGuid

								Update Acc_ViewSanadDetailCover 
								set Date=CONVERT(VARCHAR(10),@DateM,111) + ' ' +convert(char(8), Date, 108) 
								where AsGuid=@AsGuid And AsTypeDocument=2

								exec sp_Acc_CashierLog_Insert @AsGuid,@ASType,@AutoDesc,@DateMNow,@CashierGuid,'','',2
							End
					End
				Else if (@ASType=3)
					Begin
						Update acc_Order set SanadNO=@SanadNO where factorType=3 and Guid=@AsGuid
						Select @DateBargeSH=dbo.Civil2persian(Date) from Acc_Order Where factorType=3 and Guid=@AsGuid 
						if (@DateBargeSH <> @DateSanadSH)
							Begin
								Set @AutoDesc=N'ویرایش تاریخ فاکتور فروش از طریق ویرایش سند از (' + @DateBargeSH +') به (' +@DateSanadSH+')'
								Update [acc_order] 
								set Date=CONVERT(VARCHAR(10),@DateM,111) + ' ' +convert(char(8), Date, 108)
								where  FactorType=3 and Guid=@AsGuid

								Update Acc_ViewSanadDetailCover 
								set Date=CONVERT(VARCHAR(10),@DateM,111) + ' ' +convert(char(8), Date, 108) 
								where AsGuid=@AsGuid And AsTypeDocument=3

								exec sp_Acc_CashierLog_Insert @AsGuid,@ASType,@AutoDesc,@DateMNow,@CashierGuid,'','',2
							End
					End
				Else if (@ASType=4)
					Begin
						Update [acc_Order] set SanadNO=@SanadNO where factorType=4 and Guid=@AsGuid
						Select @DateBargeSH=dbo.Civil2persian(Date) from [acc_order] Where factorType=4 and Guid=@AsGuid
						if (@DateBargeSH <> @DateSanadSH)
							Begin
								Set @AutoDesc=N'ویرایش تاریخ فاکتور خرید از طریق ویرایش سند از (' + @DateBargeSH +') به (' +@DateSanadSH+')'
								Update [acc_order] set Date=CONVERT(VARCHAR(10),@DateM,111) + ' ' +convert(char(8), Date, 108) where FactorType=4 and Guid=@AsGuid

								Update Acc_ViewSanadDetailCover 
								set Date=CONVERT(VARCHAR(10),@DateM,111) + ' ' +convert(char(8), Date, 108) 
								where AsGuid=@AsGuid And AsTypeDocument=4

								exec sp_Acc_CashierLog_Insert @AsGuid,@ASType,@AutoDesc,@DateMNow,@CashierGuid,'','',2
							End
					End
				Else if (@ASType=7)
					Begin
						Update [acc_Order] set SanadNO=@SanadNO where FactorType=7 and Guid=@AsGuid
						Select @DateBargeSH=dbo.Civil2persian(Date) 
						from [acc_Order] 
						Where FactorType=7 and Guid=@AsGuid
						if (@DateBargeSH <> @DateSanadSH)
							Begin
								Set @AutoDesc=N'ویرایش تاریخ فاکتور برگشت از خرید از طریق ویرایش سند از (' + @DateBargeSH +') به (' +@DateSanadSH+')'
								Update [acc_Order] set Date=CONVERT(VARCHAR(10),@DateM,111) + ' ' +convert(char(8), Date, 108) where FactorType=7 and Guid=@AsGuid

								Update Acc_ViewSanadDetailCover 
								set Date=CONVERT(VARCHAR(10),@DateM,111) + ' ' +convert(char(8), Date, 108) 
								where AsGuid=@AsGuid And AsTypeDocument=7

								exec sp_Acc_CashierLog_Insert @AsGuid,@ASType,@AutoDesc,@DateMNow,@CashierGuid,'','',2
							End
					End
				Else if (@ASType=8)
					Begin
						Update [acc_Order] set SanadNO=@SanadNO where FactorType=8 and Guid=@AsGuid
						Select @DateBargeSH=dbo.Civil2persian(Date) from [acc_Order] Where FactorType=8 and Guid=@AsGuid
						if (@DateBargeSH <> @DateSanadSH)
							Begin
								Set @AutoDesc=N'ویرایش تاریخ فاکتور برگشت از فروش از طریق ویرایش سند از (' + @DateBargeSH +') به (' +@DateSanadSH+')'
								Update [Acc_Order] set Date=CONVERT(VARCHAR(10),@DateM,111) + ' ' +convert(char(8), Date, 108) where FactorType=8 and Guid=@AsGuid

								Update Acc_ViewSanadDetailCover 
								set Date=CONVERT(VARCHAR(10),@DateM,111) + ' ' +convert(char(8), Date, 108) 
								where AsGuid=@AsGuid And AsTypeDocument=8

								exec sp_Acc_CashierLog_Insert @AsGuid,@ASType,@AutoDesc,@DateMNow,@CashierGuid,'','',2
							End
					End
				Else if (@ASType=20)
					Begin
						Update [Acc_Tolid] set SanadNO=@SanadNO where Guid=@AsGuid
						Update [AcC_Tolid] set DateM=CONVERT(VARCHAR(10),@DateM,111) + ' ' +convert(char(8), DateM, 108) where Guid=@AsGuid
						Update Acc_ViewSanadDetailCover 
								set Date=CONVERT(VARCHAR(10),@DateM,111) + ' ' +convert(char(8), Date, 108) 
								where AsGuid=@AsGuid And AsTypeDocument=20
					End
				Else if (@ASType=21)
					Begin
						Update [acc_Order] set SanadNO=@SanadNO where FactorType=21 and Guid=@AsGuid
						Update [Acc_Order] set Date=CONVERT(VARCHAR(10),@DateM,111) + ' ' +convert(char(8), Date, 108) where FactorType=21 and Guid=@AsGuid
						Update Acc_ViewSanadDetailCover 
								set Date=CONVERT(VARCHAR(10),@DateM,111) + ' ' +convert(char(8), Date, 108) 
								where AsGuid=@AsGuid And AsTypeDocument=21
					End
				Else if (@ASType=26)
					Begin
						Update [Acc_Order] set SanadNO=@SanadNO where FactorType=26 and Guid=@AsGuid
						Update [Acc_Order] set [Date]=CONVERT(VARCHAR(10),@DateM,111) + ' ' +convert(char(8), Date, 108) where FactorType=26 and Guid=@AsGuid
						Update Acc_ViewSanadDetailCover 
								set Date=CONVERT(VARCHAR(10),@DateM,111) + ' ' +convert(char(8), Date, 108) 
								where AsGuid=@AsGuid And AsTypeDocument=26
					End
				Else if (@ASType=60)
					Begin
						Update Acc_ChequeMOperation set [DateM]=@DateM where SanadNO=@SanadNO
						Update Acc_ChequeShOperation set [DateM]=@DateM where SanadNo=@SanadNO
						Update Acc_ViewSanadDetailCover 
								set Date=CONVERT(VARCHAR(10),@DateM,111) + ' ' +convert(char(8), Date, 108) 
								where AsGuid=@AsGuid And AsTypeDocument=60
					End
				fetch next from cr_S into @ASGuid,@ASType
			end	
			close cr_S
			deallocate cr_S	
		End

	
	if(@ModifiedMode = 2)--Delete
	begin
		delete from Acc_ViewSanadDetail
		where asGuid=@Guid and ASType=@Type
	end
	
	if(@ModifiedMode in (0,1))--Insert or Update
	begin
		update [dbo].[Acc_ViewSanadDetail]
        set    [DateM]=Acc_Sanad.DateM,
			   [Description]=Acc_SanadDet.[Description],
			   [AsGuid]= Acc_SanadDet.AsGuid,
			   [AsType]=@Type,
			   [Debit]=Acc_SanadDet.[Debit],
			   [Credit]=Acc_SanadDet.[Credit],
			   [SanadNo]=@SanadNO,
			   [SanadSt]=Acc_Sanad.SanadST,
			   [Qt]=Acc_SanadDet.[QT],
			   [DecimalGuid]=Acc_SanadDet.[DecimalGuid],
			   [RowDesc]=Acc_SanadDet.Description,
			   [KolGuid]=Acc_Moein.KolGuid,
			   [MoeinGuid]=Acc_SanadDet.MoeinGuid,
			   [TafsilGuid]=Acc_SanadDet.TafsilGuid,
			   [CashierGuid]=Acc_Sanad.UserGuid,
			   [Modified]=getdate(),
			   [ServerStatus]=0,
			   [DeliveryId]=0,
			   [TafsilGuid2]=Acc_SanadDet.TafsilGuid2,
			   [TafsilGuid3]=Acc_SanadDet.TafsilGuid3,
			   [TafsilGuid4]=Acc_SanadDet.TafsilGuid4
		FROM Acc_SanadDet
		inner join Acc_ViewSanadDetail on Acc_ViewSanadDetail.Guid=Acc_SanadDet.Guid
		inner join Acc_Sanad on Acc_Sanad.Guid=Acc_SanadDet.AsGuid
		inner join Acc_Moein on Acc_SanadDet.MoeinGuid=Acc_Moein.Guid
		where Acc_Sanad.Guid=@Guid 
			  and Acc_SanadDet.ASType=@Type
			  and Acc_Sanad.SanadType<>8

		if(@@ROWCOUNT<=0)
		begin
			declare @SanadType smallint
			select @SanadType=SanadType
			from Acc_Sanad
			where Guid=@Guid

			set @SanadType=ISNULL(@SanadType,0)

			if(@SanadType<>8)
			begin
				INSERT INTO [dbo].[Acc_ViewSanadDetail]
			   ([Guid]
			   ,[DateM]
			   ,[Description]
			   ,[AsGuid]
			   ,[AsType]
			   ,[Debit]
			   ,[Credit]
			   ,[SanadNo]
			   ,[SanadSt]
			   ,[Qt]
			   ,[DecimalGuid]
			   ,[RowDesc]
			   ,[KolGuid]
			   ,[MoeinGuid]
			   ,[TafsilGuid]
			   ,[CashierGuid]
			   ,[Modified]
			   ,[ServerStatus]
			   ,[DeliveryId]
			   ,[TafsilGuid2]
			   ,[TafsilGuid3]
			   ,[TafsilGuid4])
			Select    Acc_SanadDet.[GUID]
					, Acc_Sanad.DateM
					, Acc_SanadDet.[Description]
					, Acc_SanadDet.AsGuid
					, @Type as ASType
					, Acc_SanadDet.[Debit]
					, Acc_SanadDet.[Credit]
					, @SanadNO
					, Acc_Sanad.SanadST
					, Acc_SanadDet.[QT]
					, Acc_SanadDet.[DecimalGuid]
					, Acc_SanadDet.Description
					, Acc_Moein.KolGuid
					, Acc_SanadDet.MoeinGuid
					, Acc_SanadDet.TafsilGuid
					, Acc_Sanad.UserGuid
					, GETDATE()
					, 0
					, 0
					, Acc_SanadDet.TafsilGuid2
					, Acc_SanadDet.TafsilGuid3
					, Acc_SanadDet.TafsilGuid4
			FROM Acc_SanadDet 
			inner join Acc_Sanad on Acc_Sanad.Guid=Acc_SanadDet.AsGuid
			inner join Acc_Moein on Acc_SanadDet.MoeinGuid=Acc_Moein.Guid
			where Acc_Sanad.Guid=@Guid 
				  and Acc_SanadDet.ASType=@Type		
				  and Acc_Sanad.SanadType<>8
			end
		end
	end

	Select @SanadNO
	Return @SanadNO
end
Go

ALTER Procedure [dbo].[sp_Acc_ViewSanadDetail_SelectRow_SanadNO]
	@SanadNO int
As
Begin
	Select Acc_viewSanadDetail.*,isnull(Acc_SanadDet.Ordinal,0) as Ordinal
	From Acc_viewSanadDetail left outer join Acc_SanadDet on Acc_SanadDet.Guid=Acc_viewSanadDetail.Guid
	Where SanadNO=@SanadNO
End
Go

END

ALTER PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_Acc_ViewSanadDetail_Insert]
	@date datetime,
	@desc nvarchar(max)='',
	@asType smallint,
	@debit decimal(18,3)=0,
	@credit decimal(18,3)=0,
	@sanadNo int,
	@sanadSt int,
	@guid uniqueidentifier,
	@qt float=0,
	@rowDesc nvarchar(max)='',
	@moeinGuid uniqueidentifier=null,
	@tafsilGuid uniqueidentifier=null,
	@cashierGuid uniqueidentifier=null,
	@decimalGuid uniqueidentifier=null,
	@asGuid uniqueidentifier,
	@serial nvarchar(500)='',
	@TafsilGuid2 uniqueidentifier=null,
	@TafsilGuid3 uniqueidentifier=null
AS
BEGIN
	declare @kolGuid uniqueidentifier
	select @kolGuid=KolGuid from Acc_Moein where Guid=@moeinGuid
	
	UPDATE [dbo].[Acc_ViewSanadDetail]
	   SET [DateM] = @date
		  ,[Description] = @desc
		  ,[AsGuid] = @asGuid
		  ,[AsType] = @asType
		  ,[Debit] = @debit
		  ,[Credit] = @credit
		  ,[SanadNo] = @sanadNo
		  ,[SanadSt] = @SanadSt
		  ,[Qt] = @Qt
		  ,[DecimalGuid] = @DecimalGuid
		  ,[RowDesc] = @RowDesc
		  ,[KolGuid] = @KolGuid
		  ,[MoeinGuid] = @MoeinGuid
		  ,[TafsilGuid] = @TafsilGuid
		  ,[CashierGuid] = @CashierGuid
		  ,[Modified] = GETDATE()
		  ,[ServerStatus] = 0
		  ,[DeliveryId] = 0
		  ,[Serial] = @serial
		  ,[TafsilGuid2] = @TafsilGuid2
		  ,[TafsilGuid3] = @TafsilGuid3
	 WHERE Guid = @guid
	if(@@rowcount<=0)
	begin
		INSERT INTO [dbo].Acc_ViewSanadDetail
			   ([DateM]
			   ,[Description]
			   ,[ASType]
			   ,[Debit]
			   ,[Credit]
			   ,[SanadNO]
			   ,[SanadST]
			   ,[GUID]
			   ,[QT]
			   ,[DecimalGuid]
			   ,[RowDesc]
			   ,[KolGuid]
			   ,[MoeinGuid]
			   ,[TafsilGuid]
			   ,[CashierGuid]
			   ,[AsGuid]
			   ,[Modified]
			   ,[ServerStatus]
			   ,[DeliveryId]
			   ,[Serial]
			   ,[TafsilGuid2]
			   ,[TafsilGuid3])
		 VALUES
			   (@date
			   ,@desc
			   ,@asType
			   ,@debit
			   ,@credit
			   ,@sanadNo
			   ,@sanadSt
			   ,@Guid
			   ,@qt
			   ,null
			   ,@rowDesc
			   ,@kolGuid
			   ,@moeinGuid
			   ,@tafsilGuid
			   ,@cashierGuid
			   ,@asGuid
			   ,GETDATE()
			   ,0
			   ,0
			   ,@serial
			   ,@TafsilGuid2
			   ,@TafsilGuid3)
		end
END
Go

	exec('Create Procedure [dbo].[sp_Acc_Sanad_DeleteRowsByAsGuid_AsType] As Begin print 11 end')
GO

ALTER Procedure [dbo].[sp_Acc_Sanad_DeleteRowsByAsGuid_AsType]
	@AsGuid uniqueidentifier,
	@AsType smallint
As
begin
	declare @SanadNumber int
	select	@SanadNumber=SanadNO
	from Acc_ViewSanadDetail
	where ASType=@AsType and AsGuid=@AsGuid

	set @SanadNumber=ISNULL(@SanadNumber,0)
	if(@SanadNumber<=0)
		select	@SanadNumber=SanadNO
		from Acc_Sanad
		where Guid=@AsGuid

	delete from Acc_SanadDet
	where AsGuid=@AsGuid and AsType=@AsType

	update Acc_DepotReceiptDetail
	set ReferenceGuid=null
	from Acc_DepotReceiptDetail
	inner join Acc_ViewSanadDetail on Acc_ViewSanadDetail.Guid=Acc_DepotReceiptDetail.ReferenceGuid
	where ASType=@AsType and 
		  AsGuid=@AsGuid and 
		  not ReferenceGuid is null

	delete from Acc_ViewSanadDetail
	where ASType=@AsType and AsGuid=@AsGuid

	if(not exists(
		select sanadNo
		from Acc_ViewSanadDetail
		where SanadNO=@SanadNumber
			))
	begin
		delete Acc_Sanad
		where SanadNo=@SanadNumber
	end
End
Go

	exec('Create Procedure [dbo].[sp_Sanad_Auto_Filter]	As Begin print 11 end')  
GO

ALTER PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_Sanad_Auto_Filter]
	@Number_1 bigint=0,
	@Number_2 bigint=0,
	@Numbers nvarchar(max)=null,
	@Sum1 decimal=0,
	@Sum2 decimal=0,
	@CashierGuid Uniqueidentifier=null,
	@Date1 datetime=null,
	@Date2 datetime=null,
	@SanadST smallint=0,
	@Description nvarchar(max)='',
	@Debit_Min decimal=0,
	@Debit_Max decimal=0,
	@Credit_Min decimal=0,
	@Credit_Max decimal=0,
	@RowDescription nvarchar(max)='',
	@MoeinGuid uniqueidentifier=null,
	@TafsilGuid uniqueidentifier=null,
	@HesabCode1 int,
	@HesabCode2 int,
	@HesabFullCode1 bigint,
	@HesabFullCode2 bigint,
	@RefrencedId int=0,
	@StatusId int=null
AS
BEGIN
	SET NOCOUNT ON;

	Create Table #VSanadAutoTemp
	(
		SanadNO int,
		SanadST smallint,
		SanadGuid uniqueIdentifier,
		SanadType Smallint,
		DateM DateTime,
		[Description] NVarchar(Max),
		CashierGuid uniqueidentifier,
		[SUM] Decimal(19,3)
	)

	DECLARE @DetailsSearch TABLE
	(
		AsGuid uniqueIdentifier,
		ASType smallint
	)

	Declare @HTreeID int

	if(@Date1 is not null)
		Set @Date1 = convert(char(4),datepart(yy,@Date1)) + '/' + convert(char(2),datepart(mm,@Date1)) + '/' + convert(char(2),datepart(dd,@Date1)) + ' 00:00:00'
	if(@Date2 is not null)
		Set @Date2 = convert(char(4),datepart(yy,@Date2)) + '/' + convert(char(2),datepart(mm,@Date2)) + '/' + convert(char(2),datepart(dd,@Date2)) + ' 23:59:59'

	set @RowDescription=replace(isnull(@RowDescription,'%'),'%%','%') 
	if (@RowDescription='') 
		set @RowDescription='%'

	set @Description=REPLACE(isnull(@Description,'%'),'%%','%') 
	if (@Description='') 
		set @Description='%'

	set @Debit_Min = isnull(@Debit_Min ,0) 
	set @Debit_Max = isnull(@Debit_Max ,0) 
	set @Credit_Min= isnull(@Credit_Min,0) 
	Set @Credit_Max= isnull(@Credit_Max,0) 
	
	if (not @MoeinGuid is null) or (not @TafsilGuid is null) or replace(@RowDescription,'%','')<>'' or @Debit_Min<>0 or @Debit_Max<>0 or @Credit_Min<>0 or @Credit_Max<>0 or @HesabCode1<>0 or @HesabCode2<>0 or @HesabFullCode1<>0 or @HesabFullCode2<>0
	begin
		insert into @DetailsSearch
			SELECT distinct Acc_ViewSanadDetail.AsGuid, Acc_ViewSanadDetail.ASType
			FROM Acc_ViewSanadDetail INNER JOIN
								 Acc_Moein ON Acc_ViewSanadDetail.MoeinGuid = Acc_Moein.Guid
			WHERE (Acc_ViewSanadDetail.Description LIKE @RowDescription COLLATE DATABASE_DEFAULT) AND 
								(
									(Acc_ViewSanadDetail.Debit BETWEEN @Debit_Min AND @Debit_Max and Acc_ViewSanadDetail.Credit = 0 or (@debit_Min=0 and @Debit_Max=0)) or
									(Acc_ViewSanadDetail.Credit BETWEEN @Credit_Min AND @Credit_Max and Acc_ViewSanadDetail.Debit = 0 or (@credit_Min=0 and @credit_Max=0)) 			 
								) and
								(@MoeinGuid is null OR Acc_ViewSanadDetail.MoeinGuid = @MoeinGuid) and
								((@hesabCode1=0 and @HesabCode2=0)or(dbo.STRING2INT(Acc_Moein.Code) between @HesabCode1 and @HesabCode2)) 
			union

			SELECT distinct Acc_ViewSanadDetail.AsGuid, Acc_ViewSanadDetail.ASType
			FROM Acc_ViewSanadDetail INNER JOIN
								 Acc_Tafsil ON Acc_ViewSanadDetail.TafsilGuid = Acc_Tafsil.Guid
			WHERE (Acc_ViewSanadDetail.Description LIKE @RowDescription COLLATE DATABASE_DEFAULT) AND 
								(
									(Acc_ViewSanadDetail.Debit BETWEEN @Debit_Min AND @Debit_Max and Acc_ViewSanadDetail.Credit = 0 or (@debit_Min=0 and @Debit_Max=0)) or
									(Acc_ViewSanadDetail.Credit BETWEEN @Credit_Min AND @Credit_Max and Acc_ViewSanadDetail.Debit = 0 or (@credit_Min=0 and @credit_Max=0)) 			 
								) and
								(@TafsilGuid is null OR Acc_ViewSanadDetail.TafsilGuid = @TafsilGuid) AND
								((@hesabCode1=0 and @HesabCode2=0)or(dbo.STRING2INT(Acc_Tafsil.Code) between @HesabCode1 and @HesabCode2)) 
		
		INSERT INTO #VSanadAutoTemp
			SELECT	[SANADNO],
					[SANADST],
					[VSanadAuto].AsGuid,
					[SANADTYPE],
					[DATEM],
					[VSanadAuto].[DESCRIPTION],
					[USERID],
					[SUM]
			FROM	[VSanadAuto] inner join @DetailsSearch as d on (d.asGuid=[VSanadAuto].[AsGuid] and
					d.ASType=[VSanadAuto].[SANADTYPE]) 
			Where	(SanadNO between @Number_1 and @Number_2 or (@Number_1=0 and @Number_1=0) ) and
					([sum] between @Sum1 and @Sum2 or (@sum1=0 and @Sum2=0)) and
					(USERID=@CashierGuid or @CashierGuid is null) and
					(@Date1 is null or datem >= @Date1) and
					(@Date2 is null or datem <= @Date2) and
					(SanadST=@SanadST or @SanadST=0) and
					([VSanadAuto].[Description] like @Description COLLATE DATABASE_DEFAULT) and
					((ISNULL(@StatusId,0)<=0) or VSanadAuto.StatusId=@StatusId)
			order by datem,SanadNO

		INSERT INTO #VSanadAutoTemp
			SELECT	[SANADNO],
					[SANADST],
					Guid as AsGuid,
					[SANADTYPE],
					[DATEM],
					Acc_Sanad.[DESCRIPTION],
					UserGuid as [USERID],
					0 as [SUM]
			FROM	Acc_Sanad 
			Where	(@Date1 is null or datem >= @Date1) and
					(@Date2 is null or datem <= @Date2) and
					(SanadNO between @Number_1 and @Number_2 or (@Number_1=0 and @Number_1=0) ) and
					(UserGuid=@CashierGuid or @CashierGuid is null) and
					(SanadST=@SanadST or @SanadST=0) and
					((ISNULL(@StatusId,0)<=0) or Acc_Sanad.StatusId=@StatusId) and 
					Acc_Sanad.SanadType=8
			order by datem,SanadNO
	end
	else
	begin
		INSERT INTO #VSanadAutoTemp
			SELECT	[SANADNO],
					[SANADST],
					AsGuid,
					[SANADTYPE],
					[DATEM],
					[VSanadAuto].[DESCRIPTION],
					[USERID],
					[SUM]
			FROM	[VSanadAuto] 
			Where	(@Date1 is null or datem >= @Date1) and
					(@Date2 is null or datem <= @Date2) and
					(SanadNO between @Number_1 and @Number_2 or (@Number_1=0 and @Number_1=0) ) and
					([sum] between @Sum1 and @Sum2 or (@sum1=0 and @Sum2=0)) and
					(USERID=@CashierGuid or @CashierGuid is null) and
					(SanadST=@SanadST or @SanadST=0) and
					((ISNULL(@StatusId,0)<=0) or VSanadAuto.StatusId=@StatusId)
			order by datem,SanadNO

		INSERT INTO #VSanadAutoTemp
			SELECT	[SANADNO],
					[SANADST],
					Guid as AsGuid,
					[SANADTYPE],
					[DATEM],
					Acc_Sanad.[DESCRIPTION],
					UserGuid as [USERID],
					0 as [SUM]
			FROM	Acc_Sanad 
			Where	(@Date1 is null or datem >= @Date1) and
					(@Date2 is null or datem <= @Date2) and
					(SanadNO between @Number_1 and @Number_2 or (@Number_1=0 and @Number_1=0) ) and
					(UserGuid=@CashierGuid or @CashierGuid is null) and
					(SanadST=@SanadST or @SanadST=0) and
					((ISNULL(@StatusId,0)<=0) or Acc_Sanad.StatusId=@StatusId) and 
					Acc_Sanad.SanadType=8
			order by datem,SanadNO
	end	

	Select	#VSanadAutoTemp.SANADNO,
				#VSanadAutoTemp.SANADST,
				[SanadGuid],
				Acc_Sanad.SanadType,
				#VSanadAutoTemp.[DATEM],
				#VSanadAutoTemp.[DESCRIPTION],
				#VSanadAutoTemp.CashierGuid,
				#VSanadAutoTemp.[SUM],
				Acc_Sanad.ID As SanadID,
				dbo.Civil2Persian(#VSanadAutoTemp.dateM) as [DateSH],
				[Acc_Cashier].[Name] as [UserName],
				case When #VSanadAutoTemp.SanadST=2 then N'دائمی' 
					 when #VSanadAutoTemp.SanadST=1 then N'موقت' 
					 else N'ناشناخته' 
					 end as STName,	
				case When Acc_Sanad.SanadType=50 then N'عادی' 
					 when Acc_Sanad.SanadType=51 then N'افتتاحیه' 
					 when Acc_Sanad.SanadType=52 then N'اختتامیه' 
					 when Acc_Sanad.SanadType=53 then N'سود و زیانی'  
					 when Acc_Sanad.SanadType=54 then N'یادداشت' 
					 else N'اتومات' 
					 end as SanadTypeName,
				'false' as Checked,
				case when ISNULL(SanadMode.CountOfAuto,0)>0 and ISNULL(SanadMode.CountOfManual,0)=0 then 1--Automatic
					 when ISNULL(SanadMode.CountOfAuto,0)=0 and ISNULL(SanadMode.CountOfManual,0)>0 then 2--Manual
					 when ISNULL(SanadMode.CountOfAuto,0)>0 and ISNULL(SanadMode.CountOfManual,0)>0 then 3--Mixed
					 else 0 
					 end as Mode,
				ISNULL(Acc_Sanad.StatusId,0) As StatusId
		from	#VSanadAutoTemp 
				left outer join [Acc_Cashier] on [Acc_Cashier].Guid=[CashierGuid] 
				left outer join Acc_Sanad on #VSanadAutoTemp.SanadNO=Acc_Sanad.SanadNo
				left outer join 
				(
					select	sanadNo
							,SUM(IsManual) as CountOfManual
							,SUM(IsAuto) as CountOfAuto
					from (
						select	SanadNo
							,case when AsType=50 then 1 else 0 end as IsManual
							,case when AsType<>50 then 1 else 0 end as IsAuto
						from Acc_ViewSanadDetail
						group by SanadNo,AsType
					) as type
					group by SanadNo
				) as SanadMode
				on SanadMode.SanadNo=#VSanadAutoTemp.SanadNO
		Where	(#VSanadAutoTemp.[Description] like @Description COLLATE DATABASE_DEFAULT) and
				((ISNULL(@RefrencedId,0)=0 or #VSanadAutoTemp.SanadNO in (Select ID from [dbo].[StimulQuery] where RefrencedId = @RefrencedId)))
		Order by DateM,SanadNO
	Drop Table #VSanadAutoTemp
END
Go

IF Not EXISTS (SELECT * FROM dbo.sysobjects WHERE id = OBJECT_ID(N'[sp_Sanad_CountOFAutoSanadWithDiffrentDate]') AND OBJECTPROPERTY(id, N'IsProcedure') = 1)
  exec('Create Procedure [dbo].[sp_Sanad_CountOFAutoSanadWithDiffrentDate]	As Begin print 11 end')  
GO

ALTER PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_Sanad_CountOFAutoSanadWithDiffrentDate] 
	@SanadNO BigInt,
	@DateM Datetime,
	@AsGuid1 UniqueIdentifier='00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000',
	@ASType1 smallint=0,
	@AsGuid2 UniqueIdentifier='00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000',
	@ASType2 smallint=0
AS
BEGIN
	Declare @Ret int
	Declare @T table (SanadGuid UniqueIdentifier ,SanadType int )
	
	insert into @T
	Select AsGuid,ASType
	from Acc_ViewSanadDetail 
	where SanadNO=@SanadNO and dbo.civil2persian(DateM)<>dbo.civil2persian(@DateM) and ASType<>50

	delete @T
	where	(sanadGuid=@AsGuid1 and SanadType=@ASType1) or 
			(sanadGuid=@AsGuid2 and SanadType=@ASType2)

	Declare @P table (SanadGuid UniqueIdentifier ,SanadType int )
	insert into @P
	Select SanadGuid,SanadType from @T group by SanadGuid,SanadType
	
	Select @Ret=count(1) from @P
	
	Select isnull(@Ret,0)
	Return isnull(@Ret,0)
END
Go

Alter PROCEDURE sp_Acc_ReceptionCheck_Summary
	@date1 datetime=null,
	@date2 datetime=null
AS
BEGIN
	select Status,COUNT(*) As ChequeCount,Sum(Price) As ChequeSum 
	from Acc_ReceptionCheck
	where (@date1 is null or Date>=@date1) and
		  (@date2 is null or Date<=@date2)
	group by Status
END
GO

IF Not EXISTS (SELECT * FROM dbo.sysobjects WHERE id = OBJECT_ID(N'[sp_Acc_Customer_GardeshArzi]') AND OBJECTPROPERTY(id, N'IsProcedure') = 1)
  exec('Create Procedure [dbo].[sp_Acc_Customer_GardeshArzi]	As Begin print 11 end')  
GO

ALTER Procedure [dbo].[sp_Acc_Customer_GardeshArzi]
	@TafsilGuid uniqueidentifier,
	@Date1 DateTime,
	@Date2 DateTime
As
Begin
	Declare @T Table
	(
		  SanadNO int
		, Date Datetime
		, DateSH char(10)
		, DecimalName Nvarchar(200)
		, DecimalGuid uniqueidentifier
		, TafsilGuid uniqueidentifier
		, AsGuid uniqueidentifier
		, AsType smallint
		, [Description] nvarchar(max)
		, Debit float
		, Credit float
		, Mandeh float
	)
	Declare @Z Table 
	(
		  SanadNO int
		, Date Datetime
		, DateSH char(10)
		, DecimalName Nvarchar(200)
		, DecimalGuid uniqueidentifier
		, TafsilGuid uniqueidentifier
		, AsGuid uniqueidentifier
		, AsType smallint
		, [Description] nvarchar(max)
		, Debit float
		, Credit float
		, Mandeh float
	)

	--کل گردش ارزی شخص در جدول @T
	Insert into @T 
		(
			  SanadNO
			, Date
			, DateSH
			, DecimalName
			, DecimalGuid
			, TafsilGuid
			, AsGuid
			, AsType
			, [Description]
			, Debit
			, Credit
			, Mandeh
		)
Select 
		  SanadNO
		, Date
		, DateSH
		, DecimalName
		, DecimalGuid
		, TafsilGuid
		, AsGuid
		, AsType
		, Description
		, Debit
		, Credit
		, Mandeh
From(
		SELECT    Acc_Order.SanadNo
				, Acc_Order.Date
				, dbo.Civil2Persian(Acc_Order.Date) as DateSH
				, Acc_Decimal.Name as DecimalName
				, Acc_OrderDetail.DecimalGuid
				, Acc_Order.TafsilGuid
				, Acc_OrderDetail.MasterGuid as AsGuid
				, Acc_Order.FactorType as AsType
				, N'فاکتور خرید شماره('+ cast(Acc_Order.ID2 as nvarchar) + ') '+ Acc_Order.Description as Description
				, 0 as Debit
				, Round(Sum(Acc_OrderDetail.Quantity * Acc_OrderDetail.DecimalQt),3) as Credit
				, 0 as Mandeh
		FROM Acc_OrderDetail 
			 INNER JOIN Acc_Order ON Acc_OrderDetail.MasterGuid = Acc_Order.Guid 
			 INNER JOIN Acc_Decimal ON Acc_OrderDetail.DecimalGuid = Acc_Decimal.Guid 
		group by  Acc_Order.SanadNO
				, Acc_Order.Date
				, Acc_Decimal.Name
				, Acc_OrderDetail.DecimalGuid
				, Acc_Order.TafsilGuid
				, Acc_OrderDetail.MasterGuid
				, Acc_Order.description
				, Acc_Order.ID2
				, Acc_Order.FactorType
		having  isnull(Acc_OrderDetail.DecimalGuid,'00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000') <> '00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000' and
				Acc_Order.FactorType=4

		Union All
		
		SELECT    Acc_Order.SanadNo
				, Acc_Order.Date
				, dbo.Civil2Persian(Acc_Order.Date) as DateSH
				, Acc_Decimal.Name as DecimalName
				, Acc_OrderDetail.DecimalGuid
				, Acc_Order.TafsilGuid
				, Acc_OrderDetail.MasterGuid as AsGuid
				, Acc_Order.FactorType as AsType
				, N'فاکتور فروش شماره('+ cast(Acc_Order.ID2 as nvarchar) + ') '+ Acc_Order.Description as Description
				, Round(Sum(Acc_OrderDetail.Quantity * Acc_OrderDetail.DecimalQt),3) as Debit
				, 0 as Credit
				, 0 as Mandeh
		FROM Acc_OrderDetail 
			 INNER JOIN Acc_Order ON Acc_OrderDetail.MasterGuid = Acc_Order.Guid 
			 INNER JOIN Acc_Decimal ON Acc_OrderDetail.DecimalGuid = Acc_Decimal.Guid 
		group by  Acc_Order.SanadNO
				, Acc_Order.Date
				, Acc_Decimal.Name
				, Acc_OrderDetail.DecimalGuid
				, Acc_Order.TafsilGuid
				, Acc_OrderDetail.MasterGuid
				, Acc_Order.description
				, Acc_Order.ID2
				, Acc_Order.FactorType
		having  isnull(Acc_OrderDetail.DecimalGuid,'00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000') <> '00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000' and
				Acc_Order.FactorType=3

		Union All

		SELECT    Acc_Sanad.SanadNo
				, Acc_Sanad.DateM
				, dbo.Civil2Persian(Acc_Sanad.DateM) as DateSH
				, Acc_Decimal.Name as DecimalName
				, Acc_Decimal.Guid as DecimalGuid
				, Acc_SanadDet.TafsilGuid
				, Acc_SanadDet.AsGuid as AsGuid
				, 50 as AsType
				, N'سند حسابداری شماره('+ cast(Acc_Sanad.SanadNO as nvarchar) + N') بابت :'+ Acc_Sanad.Description as Description
				, Acc_SanadDet.QT as Debit
				, 0 as Credit
				, 0 as Mandeh
		FROM Acc_Sanad 
			 INNER JOIN Acc_SanadDet ON Acc_Sanad.Guid = Acc_SanadDet.AsGuid 
			 INNER JOIN Acc_Decimal ON Acc_SanadDet.DecimalGuid = Acc_Decimal.Guid
		where Acc_SanadDet.Debit<>0 

		Union All

		SELECT    Acc_Sanad.SanadNo
				, Acc_Sanad.DateM
				, dbo.Civil2Persian(Acc_Sanad.DateM) as DateSH
				, Acc_Decimal.Name as DecimalName
				, Acc_Decimal.Guid as DecimalGuid
				, Acc_SanadDet.TafsilGuid
				, Acc_SanadDet.AsGuid as AsGuid
				, 50 as AsType
				, N'سند حسابداری شماره('+ cast(Acc_Sanad.SanadNO as nvarchar) + N') بابت :'+ Acc_Sanad.Description as Description
				, 0 as Debit
				, Acc_SanadDet.QT as Credit
				, 0 as Mandeh
		FROM Acc_Sanad 
			 INNER JOIN Acc_SanadDet ON Acc_Sanad.Guid = Acc_SanadDet.AsGuid 
			 INNER JOIN Acc_Decimal ON Acc_SanadDet.DecimalGuid = Acc_Decimal.Guid
		where Acc_SanadDet.Credit<>0 
	) as X
where X.TafsilGuid=@TafsilGuid
order by X.Date
	

	--محاسبه مانده از قبل و در صورت مخالف صفر بودن اضافه کردن یک سطر به @Z 
	Declare @M float
	Set @M=round((Select isnull(Sum(Credit)-Sum(Debit),0) from @T where Date<@Date1),3)

	if (@M>0)
		begin
			insert INTO @Z
						(
							  SanadNO
							, Date
							, DateSH
							, DecimalName
							, DecimalGuid
							, TafsilGuid
							, AsGuid
							, AsType
							, [Description]
							, Debit
							, Credit
							, Mandeh
						)
			Values
					(
						  0
						, ''
						, ''
						, ''
						, '00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000'
						, '00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000'
						, '00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000'
						, 0
						, N'مانده از قبل'
						, 0
						, @M
						, @M
					)
		End
	else if (@M<0)
		begin
			insert INTO @Z
						(
							  SanadNO
							, Date
							, DateSH
							, DecimalName
							, DecimalGuid
							, TafsilGuid
							, AsGuid
							, AsType
							, [Description]
							, Debit
							, Credit
							, Mandeh
						)
			Values
					(
						  0
						, ''
						, ''
						, ''
						, '00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000'
						, '00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000'
						, '00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000'
						, 0
						, N'مانده از قبل'
						, -@M
						, 0
						, -@M
					)
		End

	-- انتقال داده های جدول @T به @Z
	Insert INTO @Z
	Select * from @T where Date Between @Date1 and @Date2

	DECLARE @Debit Float;
	DECLARE @Credit Float;
	DECLARE @Mandeh Float;
	DECLARE @AsGuid uniqueidentifier;
	DECLARE @AsType SMALLINT;

	-- محاسبه مانده از قبل در جدول @Z
	DECLARE a CURSOR
	FOR SELECT Debit,Credit,AsGuid,AsType
	FROM @Z
	order by Date

	OPEN a;
		DECLARE @r BIGINT;
		DECLARE @finish BIGINT;
		SET @finish=(SELECT ISNULL((SELECT COUNT(SanadNO) FROM @Z),0));
		SET @r=0;
		WHILE @r<>(@finish)
		BEGIN
			
			SET @r=@r+1;
			FETCH NEXT FROM a INTO @Debit,@Credit,@AsGuid,@AsType
			SET @Mandeh= (ISNULL(@Mandeh,0)-ISNULL(@Debit,0)+ISNULL(@Credit,0));
			
			Update @Z set Mandeh=Round(@Mandeh,3) 
			where AsGuid=@AsGuid and AsType=@AsType
		END
	CLOSE a;

	DEALLOCATE a;	

	Select    SanadNO
			, Date
			, DateSH
			, DecimalName
			, DecimalGuid
			, TafsilGuid
			, AsGuid
			, AsType
			, [Description]
			, Debit
			, Credit
			, Mandeh 
	from @Z
End
Go

IF Not EXISTS (SELECT * FROM sys.objects WHERE object_id = OBJECT_ID(N'Fn_TolidSummary') AND type in (N'FN', N'IF', N'TF', N'FS', N'FT'))
	exec('CREATE FUNCTION [dbo].[Fn_TolidSummary](@ProductGuid uniqueIdentifier,@DepotGuid uniqueIdentifier) RETURNS float AS BEGIN RETURN null end')
go

ALTER FUNCTION [dbo].[Fn_TolidSummary]
(
	@ProductGuid uniqueIdentifier,
	@DepotGuid uniqueIdentifier
)
RETURNS float
AS
BEGIN
	declare @ret float

	set @ret=(select top(1) ROUND(RealAmount/Acc_Tolid_Formula_Export.Quantity,3) As Amount
	from
	(
		select    Acc_Tolid_Formula_Mavad.* 
				, Acc_PrdAnbarRelation.Amount
				, ROUND(Acc_PrdAnbarRelation.Amount/Acc_Tolid_Formula_Mavad.Quantity,3) As RealAmount
		from Acc_Tolid_Formula_Mavad
		inner join Acc_Tolid_Formula on Acc_Tolid_Formula_Mavad.MasterGuid=Acc_Tolid_Formula.Guid
		inner join Acc_Tolid_Formula_Export on Acc_Tolid_Formula_Export.MasterGuid=Acc_Tolid_Formula.Guid
		inner join Acc_PrdAnbarRelation on Acc_PrdAnbarRelation.AnbarGuid=@DepotGuid and Acc_PrdAnbarRelation.PrdGuid=Acc_Tolid_Formula_Mavad.ProductGuid
		where Acc_Tolid_Formula_Export.ProductGuid=@ProductGuid and Acc_Tolid_Formula_Export.DepotGuid=@DepotGuid
	) As x
	inner join Acc_Tolid_Formula on x.MasterGuid=Acc_Tolid_Formula.Guid
	inner join Acc_Tolid_Formula_Export on Acc_Tolid_Formula_Export.MasterGuid=Acc_Tolid_Formula.Guid
	order by x.RealAmount)

	return @ret
END
GO

	exec('Create PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_TolidSummary_Details] AS BEGIN	print 111 	end')
GO

ALTER PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_TolidSummary_Details]
	@MasterGuid uniqueidentifier=null,
	@DateM datetime
AS
BEGIN
	Declare @T Table (
						 PRDGuid uniqueidentifier
						,PRDCode nvarchar(max)
						,PRDName nvarchar(max)
						,VahedName nvarchar(max)
						,DPTGuid Uniqueidentifier
						,DPTName nvarchar(max)
						,AvailableAmount Float
						,RealAmount float
						,ProducedAmount float
						,Guid uniqueidentifier null
						,TolidGuid uniqueidentifier null
						 UNIQUE CLUSTERED (PRDGuid,DPTGuid)
					)

	insert Into @T (
						 PRDGuid 
						,PRDCode
						,PRDName
						,VahedName 
						,DPTGuid 
						,DPTName 
						,AvailableAmount 
						,RealAmount 
						,ProducedAmount 
					) 
		SELECT	dbo.Acc_Product.Guid as PRDGuid,
				dbo.Acc_Product.IdProduct as PRDCode,
				dbo.Acc_Product.name as PRDName, 
				v1Tbl.Name,
				dbo.Acc_Depot.Guid as DPTGuid,
				dbo.Acc_Depot.name AS DPTName,
				0 as AvailableAmount,
				0 as RealAmount,
				0 as ProducedAmount
		FROM	dbo.Acc_Product 
				INNER JOIN dbo.Acc_PrdAnbarRelation ON dbo.Acc_Product.Guid = dbo.Acc_PrdAnbarRelation.PrdGuid 
				INNER JOIN dbo.Acc_Depot ON dbo.Acc_PrdAnbarRelation.AnbarGuid = dbo.Acc_Depot.Guid 
				inner join Acc_ProductVahed as v1Tbl on v1Tbl.Guid=Acc_Product.GuidVahed
		Where Acc_Product.Type=0

	Create table #Z(
						PRDGuid Uniqueidentifier,
						DPTGuid uniqueidentifier,
						RemQT float
					)

	insert Into #Z (
						PRDGuid,
						DPTGuid,
						RemQT
					) 
	select  ProductGuid,
			DepotGuid,
			RemQT
	from 
		(
			Select  ProductGuid,
					DepotGuid,
					RemQT,
					RemUnitPrice,
					rank() over (partition By ProductGuid,DepotGuid order by datem desc,ordinal desc) as rank
				From Acc_Kardex 
				where DateM<@DateM
		) as rankedItems
	where rankedItems.rank=1

	Update @T
		Set RealAmount=isnull(X.RemQT,0)
		From(
				Select * from #Z
			) as X
		Where [@T].[PRDGuid]=X.PRDGuid and [@T].[DPTGuid]=X.DPTGuid	

	update @T
	set AvailableAmount = dbo.Fn_TolidSummary(T.PRDGuid,T.DPTGuid)
	from @T As T
	
	Drop Table #Z

	if (@MasterGuid is not null)
			begin
				UPDATE @T 
				SET RealAmount=[@T].RealAmount,
					AvailableAmount=X.AvailableAmount,
					ProducedAmount=X.ProducedAmount,
					Guid=x.Guid,
					TolidGuid=x.TolidGuid
				FROM (
						SELECT    ProductGuid
								, DepotGuid
								, RealAmount
								, AvailableAmount
								, ProducedAmount
								, Guid
								, TolidGuid
						FROM Acc_TolidSummaryDetail where MasterGuid=@MasterGuid
					 ) as X
				WHERE X.ProductGuid = [@T].PRDGuid and X.DepotGuid=[@T].DPTGuid
			END

	Select    T.* 
			, ISNULL(Acc_ProductGroupRelation.GroupGuid,'00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000') As GuidGroup
			, ISNULL(Acc_ProductGroup.Name,'') As GroupName
	from @T As T
	inner join Acc_Product on T.PRDGuid=Acc_Product.Guid
	inner join Acc_Depot on T.DPTGuid=Acc_Depot.Guid
	left outer join Acc_ProductGroupRelation on Acc_ProductGroupRelation.ProductGuid=Acc_Product.Guid and Acc_ProductGroupRelation.Ordinal=1
	left outer join Acc_ProductGroup on Acc_ProductGroupRelation.GroupGuid=Acc_ProductGroup.Guid
	Where Acc_Product.Status='true' and 
		  Acc_Depot.Status='true' and
	      ISNULL(Acc_Product.TolidFormulaGuid,'00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000')<>'00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000' and
		  ISNULL(Acc_ProductGroupRelation.Ordinal,0)=1
	order by PRDName
END
GO

	exec('Create PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_Acc_Product_UpdateTaxPercent] AS BEGIN	print 111 	end')
GO

ALTER PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_Acc_Product_UpdateTaxPercent]
	@TaxGuid uniqueidentifier=null,
	@RefrenceId int
AS
BEGIN
	if(ISNULL(@TaxGuid,'00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000')='00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000')
	begin
		Update Acc_Product 
		set       IsTax='false'
				, Modified=GETDATE()
				, ServerStatus=0
				, TaxGuid=null
		From Acc_Product
		inner join StimulQuery on StimulQuery.Guid=Acc_Product.Guid
		where StimulQuery.RefrencedId=@RefrenceId
	end
	else
	begin
	Update Acc_Product 
		set       IsTax='true'
				, Modified=GETDATE()
				, ServerStatus=0
				, TaxGuid=@TaxGuid
		From Acc_Product
		inner join StimulQuery on StimulQuery.Guid=Acc_Product.Guid
		where StimulQuery.RefrencedId=@RefrenceId
	end
END
Go

IF Not EXISTS (SELECT * FROM dbo.sysobjects WHERE id = OBJECT_ID(N'[sp_ChequeM_Instant]') AND OBJECTPROPERTY(id, N'IsProcedure') = 1)
	exec('Create PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_ChequeM_Instant] AS BEGIN	print 111 	end')
GO

ALTER PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_ChequeM_Instant]
	@DateM datetime
AS
BEGIN
	select Acc_ReceptionCheck.Guid
		 , case When Acc_ReceptionCheck.Status=6 then 6
		   else Operations.NewState
		   end as NewState
		 , Operations.OpGuid
	from Acc_ReceptionCheck
	left outer join Acc_Reception on Acc_Reception.Guid=Acc_ReceptionCheck.MasterGuid
	left outer join 
			(
				Select * 
				from
				(
					select Guid As OpGuid
					, NewState 
					, GuidCheque
					, RANK () OVER ( 
						PARTITION BY GuidCheque
						ORDER BY Ordinal DESC
							) as rank  
					from Acc_ChequeMOperation
					where DateM<=@DateM
				) As x Where x.rank=1
			) As Operations on Operations.GuidCheque=Acc_ReceptionCheck.Guid
	where ((Acc_ReceptionCheck.Aval2re='false' and Acc_Reception.Date<=@DateM) or
		  (Acc_ReceptionCheck.Aval2re='true' and Acc_ReceptionCheck.A2re_ReceptionDate<=@DateM))
END
GO

IF Not EXISTS (SELECT * FROM dbo.sysobjects WHERE id = OBJECT_ID(N'[sp_ChequeSh_Instant]') AND OBJECTPROPERTY(id, N'IsProcedure') = 1)
	exec('Create PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_ChequeSh_Instant] AS BEGIN	print 111 	end')
GO

Alter PROCEDURE sp_ChequeSh_Instant
	@DateM datetime
AS
BEGIN
	select Acc_PardakhtCheckSh.Guid,Operations.NewState,Operations.OpGuid
	from Acc_PardakhtCheckSh
	left outer join Acc_Pardakht on Acc_Pardakht.Guid=Acc_PardakhtCheckSh.MasterGuid
	left outer join 
			(
				Select * 
				from
				(
					select Guid As OpGuid
					, NewState
					, ChequeGuid
					, RANK () OVER ( 
						PARTITION BY ChequeGuid
						ORDER BY Ordinal DESC
							) as rank  
					from Acc_ChequeShOperation
					where DateM<=@DateM
				) As x Where x.rank=1
			) As Operations on Operations.ChequeGuid=Acc_PardakhtCheckSh.Guid
	where ((Acc_PardakhtCheckSh.Aval2re='false' and Acc_Pardakht.Date<=@DateM) or
		  (Acc_PardakhtCheckSh.Aval2re='true' and Acc_PardakhtCheckSh.PardakhtDate<=@DateM))
END
GO
