	exec('Create PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_Acc_TTMS_GetAll] AS BEGIN	print 111 	end')
GO

ALTER PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_Acc_TTMS_GetAll]
AS
BEGIN
	Select    Acc_TTMS.Guid As Guid
			, Acc_TTMS.DateM As DateM
			, Acc_TTMS.FilePath As FilePath
			, Acc_TTMS.Priod As Priod
			, Acc_TTMS.Year As Year
			, Acc_TTMS.State As State
			, COUNT(OrderSell.Guid) As SellCount
			, Sum(OrderSell.Sum) As SellSum
			, COUNT(OrderBSell.Guid) As BackSellCount
			, Sum(OrderBSell.Sum) As BackSellSum
			, COUNT(OrderBuy.Guid) As BuyCount
			, Sum(OrderBuy.Sum) As BuySum
			, COUNT(OrderBBuy.Guid) As BackBuyCount
			, Sum(OrderBBuy.Sum) As BackBuySum
	From Acc_TTMS
	inner join Acc_TTMSDetail on Acc_TTMSDetail.MasterGuid=Acc_TTMS.Guid
	left outer join Acc_Order As OrderSell on Acc_TTMSDetail.AsType=3 and Acc_TTMSDetail.AsGuid=OrderSell.Guid
	left outer join Acc_Order As OrderBSell on Acc_TTMSDetail.AsType=8 and Acc_TTMSDetail.AsGuid=OrderBSell.Guid
	left outer join Acc_Order As OrderBuy on Acc_TTMSDetail.AsType=4 and Acc_TTMSDetail.AsGuid=OrderBuy.Guid
	left outer join Acc_Order As OrderBBuy on Acc_TTMSDetail.AsType=7 and Acc_TTMSDetail.AsGuid=OrderBBuy.Guid
	group by  Acc_TTMS.Guid
			, Acc_TTMS.DateM
			, Acc_TTMS.FilePath
			, Acc_TTMS.Priod
			, Acc_TTMS.Year
			, Acc_TTMS.State
END
Go

IF Not EXISTS (SELECT * FROM dbo.sysobjects WHERE id = OBJECT_ID(N'[sp_AccountTree]') AND OBJECTPROPERTY(id, N'IsProcedure') = 1)
	exec('Create PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_AccountTree] AS BEGIN	print 111 	end')
GO

ALTER PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_AccountTree]
AS
BEGIN
	select    Guid
		, NEWID() As MasterGuid
		, Name
		, Case  When Guid= 'ae8ae7f6-1c10-41c8-8e49-2f828a0eb78f' Then 1
				When Guid= '72186d97-5a74-490d-a379-ff45948cb4a2' Then 2
				When Guid= 'e14b72ae-b29d-402c-b710-bebad6f39970' Then 3
				When Guid= 'ed6566ab-12a0-46ec-b1f4-409446798b8f' Then 4
				When Guid= '048190e8-f37b-464b-a15a-4d035195b2f4' Then 5
		  else 0
		  end As Code
		, 0 As Level
		, 0 As Account
	from Acc_HesabType

	union All 

	select    Guid
			, TypeGuid As MasterGuid
			, Name
			, Code
			, 1 As Level
			, 0 As Account
	from Acc_HesabGroup
	where Status='true'

	union All 

	select    Guid
			, GroupGuid As MasterGuid
			, Name
			, Code
			, 2 As Level
			, Account
	from Acc_Kol
	where Status='true'

	union All 

	select    Guid
			, KolGuid As MasterGuid
			, Name
			, Code
			, 3 As Level
			, Account
	from Acc_Moein
	where Status='true'

	union All

	select    Acc_Tafsil.Guid As Guid
			, Acc_Moein.Guid As MasterGuid
			, ISNULL(Acc_Tafsil.Name,'') As Name
			, MAX(ISNULL(Acc_Tafsil.Code,0)) As Code
			, 4 As Level
			, ISNULL(SUM(Acc_Accounts.Account),0) As Account
	from Acc_Tafsil
	inner join Acc_TafsilGroup on Acc_TafsilGroup.Guid=Acc_Tafsil.TafsilGroupGuid
	inner join Acc_MoeinTafsilLevelRelations on Acc_MoeinTafsilLevelRelations.TafsilGroupGuid=Acc_TafsilGroup.Guid
	inner join Acc_Moein on Acc_MoeinTafsilLevelRelations.MoeinGuid=Acc_Moein.Guid
	left outer join Acc_Accounts on Acc_Accounts.MoeinGuid=Acc_Moein.Guid and Acc_Tafsil.Guid=Acc_Accounts.TafsilGuid
	where Acc_Tafsil.Status='true'
	group by Acc_Moein.Guid,Acc_Tafsil.Guid,Acc_Tafsil.Name

	union all 

	Select    Acc_TafsilGroup.Guid As Guid
			, Acc_Moein.Guid As MasterGuid
			, ISNULL(Acc_TafsilGroup.Name,'') As Name
			, 0 As Code
			, 5 As Level
			, 0 As Account
	From Acc_TafsilGroup 
	inner join Acc_MoeinTafsilLevelRelations on Acc_MoeinTafsilLevelRelations.TafsilGroupGuid=Acc_TafsilGroup.Guid
	inner join Acc_Moein on Acc_MoeinTafsilLevelRelations.MoeinGuid=Acc_Moein.Guid
	where Acc_TafsilGroup.Status='true'
	group by Acc_Moein.Guid,Acc_TafsilGroup.Guid,Acc_TafsilGroup.Name
END
GO

GO

ALTER PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_Acc_Resid_GetAllCompact]
	@RefId int=null,
	@DateFrom dateTime=null,
	@DateTo dateTime=null
AS
BEGIN
	select    Acc_resid.*
			, ISNULL(Acc_ProductVahed.Name,'') As VahedName
			, ISNULL(Acc_Reception.Sum,0) As Beyane
			, Case when ISNULL(Acc_SanadPrint.Name,'')<>'' then ISNULL(Acc_Tafsil.Name,'') + ' ('+ ISNULL(Acc_SanadPrint.Name,'') + ')'
				   else ISNULL(Acc_Tafsil.Name,'')
				   end As TafsilName
			, ISNULL(Acc_Product.Name,'') As PrdName
			, ISNULL(Acc_SanadStatusReport.Name,'') As StateDisplay
			, ISNULL(Acc_SanadStatusReport.Guid,'00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000') As StatusGuid
	from Acc_resid
	left outer join Acc_ProductVahed on Acc_ProductVahed.Guid=Acc_Resid.GuidVahed
	left outer join Acc_SanadStatusReport on Acc_SanadStatusReport.Id=Acc_Resid.StatusId
	left outer join Acc_Reception on Acc_Reception.Guid=Acc_Resid.ReceptionGuid
	left outer join Acc_Tafsil on Acc_Tafsil.Guid=Acc_Resid.TafsilGuid
	left outer join Acc_Product on Acc_Product.Guid=Acc_Resid.PrdGuid
	left outer join Acc_SanadPrint on Acc_SanadPrint.AsGuid=Acc_Resid.Guid and Acc_SanadPrint.AsType=203
	Where (@DateFrom is null or Acc_Resid.Date1>=@DateFrom) and
		  (@DateTo is null or Acc_Resid.Date1<=@DateTo) and
		  (@RefId is null or Acc_Resid.StatusId in (select Id from StimulQuery where RefrencedId=@RefId))
END
GO

IF Not EXISTS (SELECT * FROM dbo.sysobjects WHERE id = OBJECT_ID(N'[Acc_Reception_UpdateDateMoaser]') AND OBJECTPROPERTY(id, N'IsProcedure') = 1)
	exec('Create PROCEDURE [dbo].[Acc_Reception_UpdateDateMoaser] AS BEGIN	print 111 	end')
GO

ALTER PROCEDURE [dbo].[Acc_Reception_UpdateDateMoaser]
	@Guid uniqueidentifier=null
AS
BEGIN
	update Acc_Reception 
	set DateMoaser=temp.AffectiveDay
	from 
	(
		select		dateadd(day,round(
									SUM(Price*DATEDIFF(DAY, ReceptionDate,AffectiveDate))/SUM(Price)
									,0
								 ),ReceptionDate) AS AffectiveDay
				,ReceptionDate
				,ReceptionGuid
		from 
		(
			select	  Acc_Reception.Guid as ReceptionGuid
					, cast(Acc_ReceptionCheck.Date as date) as AffectiveDate
					, cast(Acc_Reception.Date as date) as ReceptionDate
					, Acc_ReceptionCheck.Price
			from Acc_Reception
			inner join Acc_ReceptionCheck on Acc_ReceptionCheck.MasterGuid=Acc_Reception.Guid
			where ((ISNULL(@Guid,'00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000')='00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000') or Acc_Reception.Guid=@Guid)

			union all

			select	  Acc_Reception.Guid as ReceptionGuid
					, cast(Acc_ReceptionHavale.Date as date) as AffectiveDate
					, cast(Acc_Reception.Date as date) as ReceptionDate
					, Acc_ReceptionHavale.Price
			from Acc_Reception
			inner join Acc_ReceptionHavale on Acc_ReceptionHavale.MasterGuid=Acc_Reception.Guid
			where ((ISNULL(@Guid,'00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000')='00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000') or Acc_Reception.Guid=@Guid)

			union all

			select	  Acc_Reception.Guid as ReceptionGuid
					, cast(Acc_ReceptionNaghd.DateM as date) as AffectiveDate
					, cast(Acc_Reception.Date as date) as ReceptionDate
					, Acc_ReceptionNaghd.Price
			from Acc_Reception
			inner join Acc_ReceptionNaghd on Acc_ReceptionNaghd.MasterGuid=Acc_Reception.Guid
			where ((ISNULL(@Guid,'00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000')='00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000') or Acc_Reception.Guid=@Guid)

			union all

			select    Acc_SanadDet.AsGuid as ReceptionGuid
					, cast(acc_reception.Date as date) as AffectiveDate
					, cast(Acc_Reception.Date as date) as ReceptionDate
					, Acc_SanadDet.Debit as Price
			from acc_reception 
			inner join Acc_SanadDet on Acc_Reception.Guid=Acc_SanadDet.AsGuid and Acc_SanadDet.AsType=1
			where Acc_SanadDet.Debit>0 and Acc_Reception.TafsilGuid<>Acc_SanadDet.TafsilGuid
					and ((ISNULL(@Guid,'00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000')='00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000') or Acc_Reception.Guid=@Guid)
			union all

			select    Acc_SanadDet.AsGuid as ReceptionGuid
					, cast(acc_reception.Date as date) as AffectiveDate
					, cast(Acc_Reception.Date as date) as ReceptionDate
					, -Acc_SanadDet.Credit as Price
			from acc_reception 
			inner join Acc_SanadDet on Acc_Reception.Guid=Acc_SanadDet.AsGuid and Acc_SanadDet.AsType=1
			where Acc_SanadDet.Credit>0 and Acc_Reception.TafsilGuid<>Acc_SanadDet.TafsilGuid 
					and ((ISNULL(@Guid,'00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000')='00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000') or Acc_Reception.Guid=@Guid)
		) as ReceptionAffectiveDate
		group by ReceptionGuid,ReceptionDate
		having SUM(Price)>0
	) as temp 
	inner join Acc_Reception on temp.ReceptionGuid = Acc_Reception.Guid
	where (ISNULL(@Guid,'00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000')='00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000' and (DateMoaser is null or DateMoaser<>temp.AffectiveDay))
		  or Guid=@Guid
END
GO

GO

Alter PROCEDURE sp_Acc_TableRelationShip_GetAllModel
	@SanadGuid uniqueidentifier,
	@SanadType smallint
AS
BEGIN
	select *
	from
	(
			select    Acc_TableRelationShip.*
					, Acc_Reception.Date As DateM
					, Acc_Tafsil.Guid As TafsilGuid
					, Acc_Tafsil.Name As TafsilName
					, Acc_Reception.Id2
					, Acc_Reception.Description
					, Acc_Reception.Sum
			from Acc_TableRelationShip
			inner join Acc_Reception on Acc_Reception.Guid=Acc_TableRelationShip.RelatedSanadGuid and Acc_TableRelationShip.RelatedSanadType=1
			inner join Acc_Tafsil on Acc_Reception.TafsilGuid=Acc_Tafsil.Guid

			union All

			select    Acc_TableRelationShip.*
					, Acc_Pardakht.Date As DateM
					, Acc_Tafsil.Guid As TafsilGuid
					, Acc_Tafsil.Name As TafsilName
					, Acc_Pardakht.Id2
					, Acc_Pardakht.Description
					, Acc_Pardakht.Sum
			from Acc_TableRelationShip
			inner join Acc_Pardakht on Acc_Pardakht.Guid=Acc_TableRelationShip.RelatedSanadGuid and Acc_TableRelationShip.RelatedSanadType=2
			inner join Acc_Tafsil on Acc_Pardakht.TafsilGuid=Acc_Tafsil.Guid

			union All

			select    Acc_TableRelationShip.*
					, Acc_Order.Date As DateM
					, Acc_Tafsil.Guid As TafsilGuid
					, Acc_Tafsil.Name As TafsilName
					, Acc_Order.Id2
					, Acc_Order.Description
					, Acc_Order.TotalSum As Sum
			from Acc_TableRelationShip
			inner join Acc_Order on Acc_Order.Guid=Acc_TableRelationShip.RelatedSanadGuid and Acc_TableRelationShip.RelatedSanadType=Acc_Order.FactorType
			inner join Acc_Tafsil on Acc_Order.TafsilGuid=Acc_Tafsil.Guid
	) As TempAttachment
	where TempAttachment.SanadGuid=@SanadGuid and TempAttachment.SanadType=@SanadType
END
Go

IF Not EXISTS (SELECT * FROM dbo.sysobjects WHERE id = OBJECT_ID(N'[Acc_Order_UpdateDateMoaser]') AND OBJECTPROPERTY(id, N'IsProcedure') = 1)
	exec('Create PROCEDURE [dbo].[Acc_Order_UpdateDateMoaser] AS BEGIN	print 111 	end')
GO

ALTER PROCEDURE [dbo].[Acc_Order_UpdateDateMoaser]
	@Guid uniqueidentifier=null
AS
BEGIN
	update Acc_Order
	set TasvieDateMoaser=Temp.AffectiveDay
	from
	(
		select dateadd(day,round(
										SUM(ReceptionSum*DATEDIFF(DAY, OrderDate,AffectiveDate))/SUM(ReceptionSum)
										,0
									 ),OrderDate) AS AffectiveDay
			 ,OrderDate
			 ,OrderGuid
		from
		(
			select    Acc_Order.Guid As OrderGuid
					, CAST(Acc_Reception.DateMoaser as date) As AffectiveDate
					, CAST(Acc_Order.Date as date) As OrderDate
					, OrderRelation.Price as ReceptionSum
			from Acc_Order
			inner join Acc_TableRelationShip As OrderRelation on OrderRelation.SanadGuid=Acc_Order.Guid and OrderRelation.SanadType=Acc_Order.FactorType
			inner join Acc_Reception on Acc_Reception.Guid=OrderRelation.RelatedSanadGuid and OrderRelation.RelatedSanadType=1
			where ISNULL(@Guid,'00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000')='00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000' or Acc_Order.Guid=@Guid
		) As OrderActiveDate
		group by OrderGuid,OrderDate
		having SUM(ReceptionSum)>0
	) As Temp
	Right outer join Acc_Order on Acc_Order.Guid=Temp.OrderGuid
	where (ISNULL(@Guid,'00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000')='00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000' and (TasvieDateMoaser is null or TasvieDateMoaser<>temp.AffectiveDay))
		  or Guid=@Guid
END
Go

IF Not EXISTS (SELECT * FROM dbo.sysobjects WHERE id = OBJECT_ID(N'[sp_acc_order_GetAttachmentSum]') AND OBJECTPROPERTY(id, N'IsProcedure') = 1)
	exec('Create PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_acc_order_GetAttachmentSum] AS BEGIN	print 111 	end')
GO

ALTER PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_acc_order_GetAttachmentSum]
	@refId int
AS
BEGIN
	select    Acc_Order.Guid
			, Sum(Acc_TableRelationShip.Price) As AttachmentSum
	from Acc_Order
	inner join StimulQuery on StimulQuery.Guid=Acc_Order.Guid
	inner join Acc_TableRelationShip on Acc_TableRelationShip.SanadGuid=Acc_Order.Guid and Acc_TableRelationShip.SanadType=Acc_Order.FactorType
	where StimulQuery.RefrencedId=@refId and Acc_TableRelationShip.RelatedSanadGuid<>'00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000'
	group by Acc_Order.Guid

	union all

	select    Acc_Order.Guid
			, Sum(Acc_TableRelationShip.Price) As AttachmentSum
	from Acc_Order
	inner join StimulQuery on StimulQuery.Guid=Acc_Order.Guid
	inner join Acc_TableRelationShip on Acc_TableRelationShip.RelatedSanadGuid=Acc_Order.Guid and Acc_TableRelationShip.RelatedSanadType=Acc_Order.FactorType
	where StimulQuery.RefrencedId=@refId and Acc_TableRelationShip.SanadGuid<>'00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000'
	group by Acc_Order.Guid
END
GO

IF not EXISTS (SELECT * FROM sys.views WHERE object_id = OBJECT_ID(N'[dbo].[VCouponUsages]'))
	exec('create VIEW [dbo].[VCouponUsages] as select * from Acc_Product')
go

ALTER VIEW [dbo].[VCouponUsages]
AS
SELECT    dbo.Acc_OrderDetail.MasterGuid AS OrderGuid
		, dbo.Acc_Order.TafsilGuid
		, Acc_CouponDiscountDetail.MasterGuid as CouponGuid
FROM dbo.Acc_OrderDetail 
INNER JOIN dbo.Acc_Order ON dbo.Acc_Order.Guid = dbo.Acc_OrderDetail.MasterGuid 
inner join Acc_CouponDiscountDetail on Acc_OrderDetail.DicountCouponDetailGuid=Acc_CouponDiscountDetail.Guid
WHERE dbo.Acc_OrderDetail.DicountCouponDetailGuid IS NOT NULL 
	  AND Acc_Order.FactorType = 3
GROUP BY  dbo.Acc_OrderDetail.MasterGuid
		, dbo.Acc_Order.TafsilGuid
		, Acc_CouponDiscountDetail.MasterGuid 
GO

-- ==========================================================================================
IF Not EXISTS (SELECT * FROM sys.objects WHERE object_id = OBJECT_ID(N'[dbo].[fn_CheckOrderSum]') AND type in (N'FN', N'IF', N'TF', N'FS', N'FT'))
	exec('CREATE FUNCTION [dbo].[fn_CheckOrderSum](@DayCount int,@TafsilGuid uniqueidentifier,@StartPrice decimal(18,3),@EndPrice decimal(18,3),@OrderDate datetime) RETURNS bit AS BEGIN RETURN null end')
go

ALTER FUNCTION [dbo].[fn_CheckOrderSum]
(
	@DayCount int,
	@TafsilGuid uniqueidentifier,
	@StartPrice decimal(18,3),
	@EndPrice decimal(18,3),
	@OrderDate datetime
)
RETURNS bit
AS
BEGIN
	set @DayCount=-@DayCount

	declare @SellSum decimal(18,3)
	declare @BackSellSum decimal(18,3)
	declare @TotalSum decimal(18,3)

	select @SellSum=Sum(TotalSum)
	from Acc_Order
	where TafsilGuid=@TafsilGuid and
		  Date>=DATEADD(DAY,@DayCount,GETDATE()) and 
		  FactorType=3 and
		  Status='true'

	select @BackSellSum=Sum(TotalSum)
	from Acc_Order
	where TafsilGuid=@TafsilGuid and
		  Date>=DATEADD(DAY,@DayCount,GETDATE()) and 
		  FactorType=8 and
		  Status='true'

	set @TotalSum = ISNULL(@SellSum,0)-ISNULL(@BackSellSum,0)
	if((@StartPrice=0 or @TotalSum>=@StartPrice) and (@EndPrice=0 or @TotalSum<=@EndPrice))
		return 'true'
	return 'false'
END
GO

--====================================================================================================================
IF Not EXISTS (SELECT * FROM dbo.sysobjects WHERE id = OBJECT_ID(N'[Acc_Coupon_SelectContainsForOrder]') AND OBJECTPROPERTY(id, N'IsProcedure') = 1)
	exec('Create PROCEDURE [dbo].[Acc_Coupon_SelectContainsForOrder] AS BEGIN	print 111 	end')
GO

ALTER PROCEDURE [dbo].[Acc_Coupon_SelectContainsForOrder]
	@TafsilGuid uniqueidentifier,
	@OrderDate datetime,
	@OrderTotalSum decimal(18,3),
	@RefrenceId int,
	@SpecificCouponGuid uniqueidentifier = null,
	@SanadPrintNumber bigint=0
AS
BEGIN
	declare @CustomerGroupGuid uniqueidentifier

	select @CustomerGroupGuid=GroupGuid
	from Acc_Customer
	where Guid=@TafsilGuid

	--تعداد استفاده کوپن برای این مشتری و همه مشتریان
	--به جهت اعتبار سنجی برای تعداد استفاده
	declare @CouponUsages Table
	(
		CouponGuid uniqueidentifier,
		UseCountForTafsil int,
		UseCountForAll int
	)

	--همه کوپن های استفاده شده توسط کلیه مشتریان
	insert into @CouponUsages
	(	CouponGuid,
		UseCountForTafsil,
		UseCountForAll
	)
	SELECT	CouponGuid
			,0 as UseCountForTafsil
			,count(*)
	FROM [dbo].[VCouponUsages]
	group by CouponGuid 

	--بروزرسانی کوپن های استفاده شده توسط مشتری فاکتور جاری
	update @CouponUsages
	set UseCountForTafsil=tafsilUsages.UseCountForTafsil
	from @CouponUsages as usages 
	inner join 
	(
		select CouponGuid,count(*) as UseCountForTafsil
		from VCouponUsages
		where TafsilGuid=@TafsilGuid 
		group by CouponGuid 
	) as tafsilUsages on tafsilUsages.CouponGuid=usages.CouponGuid

	--کوپن هایی که از لحاظ تاریخ و مبلغ، مجاز به اسفاده هستند
	declare @validCoupons Table
	(
		CouponGuid uniqueidentifier
	)

	--افزودن کوپن های مجاز
	insert into @validCoupons
	select	distinct Acc_CouponDiscount.Guid as CouponGuid 
	from	Acc_CouponDiscount 
	inner join Acc_CouponDiscountDetail on Acc_CouponDiscountDetail.MasterGuid=Acc_CouponDiscount .Guid
	left outer join @CouponUsages as usages on usages.CouponGuid=Acc_CouponDiscount.Guid 
	where	Acc_CouponDiscount.Status='true' and 
			Acc_CouponDiscount.Type=0 and 
			(ISNULL(@SpecificCouponGuid,'00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000')<>'00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000' or Acc_CouponDiscount.Mode=0 or Acc_CouponDiscount.Mode=4) and 
			(ISNULL(@SpecificCouponGuid,'00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000')<>'00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000' or Acc_CouponDiscount.DateFrom<=@OrderDate) and 
			(ISNULL(@SpecificCouponGuid,'00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000')<>'00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000' or Acc_CouponDiscount.DateTo>=@OrderDate) and 
			Acc_CouponDiscount.CountCouponUse>isnull(usages.UseCountForAll,0) and 
			Acc_CouponDiscount.CountCouponUseForUser>isnull(usages.UseCountForTafsil,0) and
			(Acc_CouponDiscountDetail.MinBuy=0 or Acc_CouponDiscountDetail.MinBuy<=@OrderTotalSum) and
			(Acc_CouponDiscountDetail.MaxBuy=0 or Acc_CouponDiscountDetail.MaxBuy>=@OrderTotalSum) and
			(ISNULL(@SpecificCouponGuid,'00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000')='00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000' or Acc_CouponDiscount.Guid=@SpecificCouponGuid)

	--افزودن پلکان های مجاز
	insert into @validCoupons
	select	distinct Acc_CouponDiscount.Guid as CouponGuid 
	from	Acc_CouponDiscount 
	inner join Acc_CouponDiscountDetail on Acc_CouponDiscountDetail.MasterGuid=Acc_CouponDiscount .Guid
	left outer join @CouponUsages as usages on usages.CouponGuid=Acc_CouponDiscount.Guid
	where	Acc_CouponDiscount.Status='true' and 
			Acc_CouponDiscount.Type=1 and 
			(Acc_CouponDiscount.Mode=0 or Acc_CouponDiscount.Mode=4) and 
			Acc_CouponDiscount.DateFrom<=@OrderDate and 
			Acc_CouponDiscount.DateTo>=@OrderDate and 
			Acc_CouponDiscount.CountCouponUse>isnull(usages.UseCountForAll,0) and 
			Acc_CouponDiscount.CountCouponUseForUser>isnull(usages.UseCountForTafsil,0) and
			dbo.fn_CheckOrderSum(Acc_CouponDiscountDetail.GardeshAffectiveDays,@TafsilGuid,Acc_CouponDiscountDetail.GardeshStartPrice,Acc_CouponDiscountDetail.GardeshEndPrice,@OrderDate)='true' and
			(Acc_CouponDiscountDetail.MinBuy=0 or Acc_CouponDiscountDetail.MinBuy<=@OrderTotalSum) and
			(Acc_CouponDiscountDetail.MaxBuy=0 or Acc_CouponDiscountDetail.MaxBuy>=@OrderTotalSum)

	--اشخاصی که مشمول کوپن نمیشوند
	delete from @validCoupons
	where CouponGuid in
	(
		select	Acc_CouponDiscount.Guid As CouponGuid
		from	Acc_CouponDiscountEntityRelation
		inner join Acc_CouponDiscountDetail on Acc_CouponDiscountDetail.Guid=Acc_CouponDiscountEntityRelation.CouponDetailGuid
		inner join Acc_CouponDiscount on Acc_CouponDiscount.Guid=Acc_CouponDiscountDetail.MasterGuid
		where Acc_CouponDiscountDetail.CustomerContains='false' and
			  Acc_CouponDiscountEntityRelation.EntityType = 4 --Customer

		union all

		select	Acc_CouponDiscount.Guid As CouponGuid
		from	Acc_CouponDiscountEntityRelation
		inner join Acc_CouponDiscountDetail on Acc_CouponDiscountDetail.Guid=Acc_CouponDiscountEntityRelation.CouponDetailGuid
		inner join Acc_CouponDiscount on Acc_CouponDiscount.Guid=Acc_CouponDiscountDetail.MasterGuid
		where Acc_CouponDiscountDetail.CustomerContains='false' and
			  Acc_CouponDiscountEntityRelation.EntityType = 5 --CustomerGroup

		except

		select Acc_CouponDiscount.Guid As CouponGuid
		from 
		(
			--کل کوپن هایی که برای شامل شدن مشتری فیلتر اعمال کرده
			select	distinct Acc_CouponDiscountDetail.Guid As DiscountCouponDetailGuid
			from	Acc_CouponDiscountEntityRelation
			inner join Acc_CouponDiscountDetail on Acc_CouponDiscountDetail.Guid=Acc_CouponDiscountEntityRelation.CouponDetailGuid
			where Acc_CouponDiscountDetail.CustomerContains='true' and
				  Acc_CouponDiscountEntityRelation.EntityType = 4 --Customer

			union all

			select	Acc_CouponDiscountDetail.Guid As DiscountCouponDetailGuid
			from	Acc_CouponDiscountEntityRelation
			inner join Acc_CouponDiscountDetail on Acc_CouponDiscountDetail.Guid=Acc_CouponDiscountEntityRelation.CouponDetailGuid
			where Acc_CouponDiscountDetail.CustomerContains='true' and
				  Acc_CouponDiscountEntityRelation.EntityType = 5 --CustomerGroup
	
			except 
			--تفاضل مجموعه ها  
			--حاصل تفاضل کوپن هایی هست که برای مشمول شدن مشتری فیلتر دارند و این شخص در این فیلتر وجود ندارد
			--یعنی کوپن هایی که این این شخص مشمول شون نمیشه

			select	distinct Acc_CouponDiscountDetail.Guid As DiscountCouponDetailGuid
			from	Acc_CouponDiscountEntityRelation
			inner join Acc_CouponDiscountDetail on Acc_CouponDiscountDetail.Guid=Acc_CouponDiscountEntityRelation.CouponDetailGuid
			where Acc_CouponDiscountDetail.CustomerContains='true' and
				  Acc_CouponDiscountEntityRelation.EntityType = 4 and --Customer
				  Acc_CouponDiscountEntityRelation.EntityGuid = @TafsilGuid

			union all

			select	Acc_CouponDiscountDetail.Guid As DiscountCouponDetailGuid
			from	Acc_CouponDiscountEntityRelation
			inner join Acc_CouponDiscountDetail on Acc_CouponDiscountDetail.Guid=Acc_CouponDiscountEntityRelation.CouponDetailGuid
			where Acc_CouponDiscountDetail.CustomerContains='true' and
				  Acc_CouponDiscountEntityRelation.EntityType = 5 and --CustomerGroup
				  Acc_CouponDiscountEntityRelation.EntityGuid=@CustomerGroupGuid
		) as temp 
		inner join Acc_CouponDiscountDetail on Acc_CouponDiscountDetail.Guid=temp.DiscountCouponDetailGuid
		inner join Acc_CouponDiscount on Acc_CouponDiscount.Guid=Acc_CouponDiscountDetail.MasterGuid
	)

	--اشخاصی که مشمول کوپن نمیشوند
	delete from @validCoupons
	where CouponGuid in
	(
		select Acc_CouponDiscount.Guid As CouponGuid
		from 
		(
			--کل کوپن هایی که برای شامل شدن مشتری فیلتر اعمال کرده
			select	distinct Acc_CouponDiscountDetail.Guid As DiscountCouponDetailGuid
			from	Acc_CouponDiscountEntityRelation
			inner join Acc_CouponDiscountDetail on Acc_CouponDiscountDetail.Guid=Acc_CouponDiscountEntityRelation.CouponDetailGuid
			where Acc_CouponDiscountDetail.CustomerContains='true' and
				  Acc_CouponDiscountEntityRelation.EntityType = 4 --Customer

			union all

			select	Acc_CouponDiscountDetail.Guid As DiscountCouponDetailGuid
			from	Acc_CouponDiscountEntityRelation
			inner join Acc_CouponDiscountDetail on Acc_CouponDiscountDetail.Guid=Acc_CouponDiscountEntityRelation.CouponDetailGuid
			where Acc_CouponDiscountDetail.CustomerContains='true' and
				  Acc_CouponDiscountEntityRelation.EntityType = 5 --CustomerGroup


			except

			select	distinct Acc_CouponDiscountDetail.Guid As DiscountCouponDetailGuid
			from	Acc_CouponDiscountEntityRelation
			inner join Acc_CouponDiscountDetail on Acc_CouponDiscountDetail.Guid=Acc_CouponDiscountEntityRelation.CouponDetailGuid
			where Acc_CouponDiscountDetail.CustomerContains='true' and
				  Acc_CouponDiscountEntityRelation.EntityType = 4 and --Customer
				  Acc_CouponDiscountEntityRelation.EntityGuid = @TafsilGuid

			union all

			select	Acc_CouponDiscountDetail.Guid As DiscountCouponDetailGuid
			from	Acc_CouponDiscountEntityRelation
			inner join Acc_CouponDiscountDetail on Acc_CouponDiscountDetail.Guid=Acc_CouponDiscountEntityRelation.CouponDetailGuid
			where Acc_CouponDiscountDetail.CustomerContains='true' and
				  Acc_CouponDiscountEntityRelation.EntityType = 5 and --CustomerGroup
				  Acc_CouponDiscountEntityRelation.EntityGuid=@CustomerGroupGuid

		) as temp 
		inner join Acc_CouponDiscountDetail on Acc_CouponDiscountDetail.Guid=temp.DiscountCouponDetailGuid
		inner join Acc_CouponDiscount on Acc_CouponDiscount.Guid=Acc_CouponDiscountDetail.MasterGuid

		except
		
		select	Acc_CouponDiscount.Guid As CouponGuid
		from	Acc_CouponDiscountEntityRelation
		inner join Acc_CouponDiscountDetail on Acc_CouponDiscountDetail.Guid=Acc_CouponDiscountEntityRelation.CouponDetailGuid
		inner join Acc_CouponDiscount on Acc_CouponDiscount.Guid=Acc_CouponDiscountDetail.MasterGuid
		where Acc_CouponDiscountDetail.CustomerContains='false' and
			  Acc_CouponDiscountEntityRelation.EntityType = 4 --Customer

		union all

		select	Acc_CouponDiscount.Guid As CouponGuid
		from	Acc_CouponDiscountEntityRelation
		inner join Acc_CouponDiscountDetail on Acc_CouponDiscountDetail.Guid=Acc_CouponDiscountEntityRelation.CouponDetailGuid
		inner join Acc_CouponDiscount on Acc_CouponDiscount.Guid=Acc_CouponDiscountDetail.MasterGuid
		where Acc_CouponDiscountDetail.CustomerContains='false' and
			  Acc_CouponDiscountEntityRelation.EntityType = 5 --CustomerGroup
	)

	---------------------------------------------
	--------اعمال محدوددیت کالاهای مشمول کوپن--
	---------------------------------------------
	--نتیجه اشتراک دو مجموعه
	declare @orderCouponIntersected Table
	(
		CouponGuid uniqueidentifier,
		ProductGuid uniqueidentifier
	)

	declare @orderCoupon Table
	(
		CouponGuid uniqueidentifier,
		ProductGuid uniqueidentifier
	)

	--ضرب دکارتی اقلام فاکتور و کوپن
	insert into @orderCoupon
	select distinct validCoupons.CouponGuid,ProductGuid
	from Acc_OrderDetailCouponCalculation 
	cross join @validCoupons as validCoupons
	where RefrencedId=@RefrenceId

	--اشتراک دو مجموعه گرفته میشه اگر نتیجه اشتراک با مجموعه ضرب دکارتی اقلام فاکتور با کوپن یکسان بود آنگاه این فاکتور مشمول کوپن نمیشود
	--یعنی کلیه اقلام این فاکتور در لیست اقلام غیر مشمول کوپن میباشد و ممکن است کوپن اقلام غیر مشمول دیگری نیز داشته باشد که برای ما بی اهمیت است

	insert into @orderCouponIntersected
	select * 
	from @orderCoupon
	Except
	(
		select	Acc_CouponDiscountDetail.MasterGuid as CouponGuid,
				Acc_CouponDiscountEntityRelation.EntityGuid
		from	Acc_CouponDiscountEntityRelation 
		inner join Acc_CouponDiscountDetail on Acc_CouponDiscountDetail.Guid=Acc_CouponDiscountEntityRelation.CouponDetailGuid
		where Acc_CouponDiscountDetail.ProductContains='false' and
			  Acc_CouponDiscountEntityRelation.EntityType=1  --Product

		union All

		select	Acc_CouponDiscountDetail.MasterGuid as CouponGuid,
				Acc_Product.Guid As EntityGuid
		from	Acc_CouponDiscountEntityRelation 
		inner join Acc_CouponDiscountDetail on Acc_CouponDiscountDetail.Guid=Acc_CouponDiscountEntityRelation.CouponDetailGuid
		inner join Acc_ProductGroupRelation on Acc_ProductGroupRelation.GroupGuid=Acc_CouponDiscountEntityRelation.EntityGuid and Acc_CouponDiscountEntityRelation.EntityType=2
		inner join Acc_Product  on Acc_Product.Guid=Acc_ProductGroupRelation.ProductGuid
		where Acc_CouponDiscountDetail.ProductContains='false' and
			  Acc_CouponDiscountEntityRelation.EntityType=2 --ProductGroup

		union

		(
			select CouponGuid,ProductGuid
			from @orderCoupon

			except

			(
				select	Acc_CouponDiscountDetail.MasterGuid as CouponGuid,
						Acc_Product.Guid As EntityGuid
				from	Acc_CouponDiscountEntityRelation 
				inner join Acc_CouponDiscountDetail on Acc_CouponDiscountDetail.Guid=Acc_CouponDiscountEntityRelation.CouponDetailGuid
				inner join Acc_ProductGroupRelation on Acc_ProductGroupRelation.GroupGuid=Acc_CouponDiscountEntityRelation.EntityGuid and Acc_CouponDiscountEntityRelation.EntityType=2
				inner join Acc_Product  on Acc_Product.Guid=Acc_ProductGroupRelation.ProductGuid
				where Acc_CouponDiscountDetail.ProductContains='true' and
					  Acc_CouponDiscountEntityRelation.EntityType=2 and --ProductGroup
					  Acc_CouponDiscountEntityRelation.EntityGuid=Acc_ProductGroupRelation.GroupGuid

				union

				select	Acc_CouponDiscountDetail.MasterGuid as CouponGuid,
						Acc_CouponDiscountEntityRelation.EntityGuid
				from	Acc_CouponDiscountEntityRelation 
				inner join Acc_CouponDiscountDetail on Acc_CouponDiscountDetail.Guid=Acc_CouponDiscountEntityRelation.CouponDetailGuid
				where Acc_CouponDiscountDetail.ProductContains='true' and
					  Acc_CouponDiscountEntityRelation.EntityType=1

				union

				select    Acc_CouponDiscountDetail.MasterGuid
						, Acc_Product.Guid
				from Acc_CouponDiscountDetail
				left outer join Acc_CouponDiscountEntityRelation on Acc_CouponDiscountEntityRelation.CouponDetailGuid=Acc_CouponDiscountDetail.Guid and Acc_CouponDiscountEntityRelation.EntityType in (1,2)
				cross join Acc_Product
				where Acc_CouponDiscountEntityRelation.EntityGuid is null
			)
		)
	)

	--هر کوپن شامل بیش از یک سطر در عملیات مجموعه ای هست
	--سطر هایی که در دو مجموعه فوق برای یک کپن بخصوص یکسان هستند باید کوپن هاشون رو از جدول کوپن های مجاز حذف کنیم
	--حتی اگر یک سطر از جدول راست و چپ با هم تطابق نداشته باشند این کوپن مجاز خواهد بود
	--delete from @validCoupons
	--where CouponGuid in 
	--(
	--	select	case when orderCouponIntersected.CouponGuid is null then orderCoupon.CouponGuid else orderCouponIntersected.CouponGuid end as CouponGuid
	--	from	@orderCoupon as orderCoupon full 
	--	outer join @orderCouponIntersected as orderCouponIntersected on orderCoupon.CouponGuid =orderCouponIntersected.CouponGuid
	--	where	orderCouponIntersected.CouponGuid is null or orderCoupon.CouponGuid is null
	--)

	delete @validCoupons
	from @validCoupons As ValidCoupons
	inner join
	(
		select CouponGuid 
		from @validCoupons

		except

		select CouponGuid
		from @orderCouponIntersected
	) As InvalidCoupons on ValidCoupons.CouponGuid=InvalidCoupons.CouponGuid
	

	if(@SanadPrintNumber>0 and ISNULL(@SpecificCouponGuid,dbo.GuidEmpty())=dbo.GuidEmpty())
	begin
		delete @validCoupons
		where CouponGuid not in
		(
			select    Acc_Order.CashBackCouponGuid
			from Acc_SanadPrint
			inner join Acc_Order on Acc_Order.Guid=Acc_SanadPrint.AsGuid and Acc_Order.FactorType=AsType
			where MobileLong=@SanadPrintNumber and
				  ISNULL(Acc_Order.CashBackCouponGuid,dbo.GuidEmpty())<>dbo.GuidEmpty()
		)
	end

	select *
	from @validCoupons
END
GO

IF Not EXISTS (SELECT * FROM dbo.sysobjects WHERE id = OBJECT_ID(N'[sp_Kardex_LinkSellToBackSell]') AND OBJECTPROPERTY(id, N'IsProcedure') = 1)
	exec('Create PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_Kardex_LinkSellToBackSell] AS BEGIN	print 111 	end')
GO

Alter PROCEDURE sp_Kardex_LinkSellToBackSell
	@TafsilGuid uniqueidentifier,
	@ProductGuid uniqueidentifier,
	@DateM datetime
AS
BEGIN
	select    top(20) Acc_Kardex.ID2
			, DateM
			, Description
			, KhorojQT
			, UnitPrice
			, RemTotalPrice
			, DetailGuid
			, SanadGuid
			, SanadType
			, Acc_Kardex.ProductGuid
			, ISNULL(Sum(RefrencedDetail.Quantity),0) as BackSellQuantity
			, KhorojQT-ISNULL(Sum(RefrencedDetail.Quantity),0) as RemainQuantity
			, V2Guid
			, V2Qt
			, Avarez
			, Discount
			, Tax
	from Acc_Kardex
	left outer join
	(
		select	  Acc_OrderDetail.Guid
				, Quantity
				, ProductGuid
				, ReferenceGuid
		from Acc_OrderDetail
		inner join Acc_Order on Acc_Order.Guid=Acc_OrderDetail.MasterGuid
		where Acc_Order.FactorType=8
	) as RefrencedDetail on RefrencedDetail.ProductGuid=Acc_Kardex.ProductGuid and RefrencedDetail.ReferenceGuid=Acc_Kardex.DetailGuid
	where   Acc_Kardex.TafsilGuid=@TafsilGuid and
			Acc_Kardex.ProductGuid=@ProductGuid and
			SanadType=3 and
			DateM<=@DateM
	group by  Acc_Kardex.ID2
			, DateM
			, Acc_Kardex.Description
			, KhorojQT
			, UnitPrice
			, RemTotalPrice
			, DetailGuid
			, SanadGuid
			, SanadType
			, Acc_Kardex.ProductGuid
			, V2Guid
			, V2Qt
			, Avarez
			, Discount
			, Tax
	order by DateM desc
END
GO

IF Not EXISTS (SELECT * FROM dbo.sysobjects WHERE id = OBJECT_ID(N'[sp_Global_Ticket_SelectAll]') AND OBJECTPROPERTY(id, N'IsProcedure') = 1)

ALTER PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_Global_Ticket_SelectAll]
	@TargetType smallint,
	@TargetGuid uniqueidentifier = null,
	@DateTimeFrom datetime = null,
	@DateTimeTo datetime = null,
	@RefIdStatus int = null,
	@RefIdPriority int = null
AS
BEGIN
	declare @ValidTickets Table
	(
		Guid uniqueidentifier
	)

	-- 1==>Tafsil
	-- 2==>Cashier
	-- 3==>Department
	if(@TargetType in (1,3))
	begin
		insert into @ValidTickets
		select distinct Global_Ticket.Guid
		from Global_Ticket
		Left join Global_TicketDetail on Global_TicketDetail.MasterGuid = Global_Ticket.Guid
		Left join Global_TicketPart on (Global_TicketPart.Guid = Global_Ticket.SourceGuid and Global_TicketPart.Guid = Global_Ticket.TargetGuid)
		left join Acc_Cashier on Acc_Cashier.Guid= Global_Ticket.SourceGuid
		left join Acc_Cashier cash on cash.Guid= Global_Ticket.TargetGuid
		
		where @TargetGuid is null 
			  or Global_TicketDetail.SourceGuid = @TargetGuid
			  or Global_Ticket.TargetGuid = @TargetGuid
			  or Global_Ticket.SourceGuid = @TargetGuid
			  or Global_TicketPart.Guid = @TargetGuid
	end
	else -- Cashier
	begin
		insert into @ValidTickets
		select distinct Global_Ticket.Guid
		from Global_Ticket
		Left join Global_TicketDetail on Global_TicketDetail.MasterGuid = Global_Ticket.Guid
		left join Acc_Cashier on Acc_Cashier.Guid= Global_Ticket.SourceGuid
		left join Acc_Cashier cash on cash.Guid= Global_Ticket.TargetGuid
		left join Global_TicketPart on Global_TicketPart.Guid = Global_Ticket.TargetGuid 
		where @TargetGuid is null 
			  or Global_Ticket.TargetGuid = @TargetGuid
			  or Global_Ticket.SourceGuid = @TargetGuid
			  or Global_TicketDetail.SourceGuid = @TargetGuid
			  or Global_Ticket.TargetType = 3
	end

	SELECT  Global_Ticket.*,
        CASE 
            WHEN Global_Ticket.SourceType = 1 
                THEN ISNULL(Acc_Tafsil.Name, '')      
            WHEN Global_Ticket.SourceType= 2 
                THEN ISNULL(Acc_Cashier.Name, '')     
            ELSE ''                                
        END AS SourceName,
		 CASE 
            WHEN Global_Ticket.TargetType= 2 
                THEN ISNULL(cash.Name, '')     
            WHEN Global_Ticket.TargetType = 3 
                THEN ISNULL(Global_TicketPart.Name, '')     
            ELSE ''                                
        END AS PartName,
       

        ISNULL(Acc_SanadStatusReport.Name, '') AS StatusName
	from @ValidTickets as V

	inner join Global_Ticket on Global_Ticket.Guid = V.Guid

	left join Acc_Tafsil on Acc_Tafsil.Guid = Global_Ticket.SourceGuid 
	left join Acc_Cashier on Acc_Cashier.Guid = Global_Ticket.SourceGuid
	left join Acc_Cashier cash on cash.Guid = Global_Ticket.TargetGuid
	left join Global_TicketPart on Global_TicketPart.Guid = Global_Ticket.TargetGuid 

	left join Acc_SanadStatusReport on Acc_SanadStatusReport.Id = Global_Ticket.StatusId
	left join StimulQuery as status on Global_Ticket.StatusId = status.Id and status.RefrencedId = @RefIdStatus
	left join StimulQuery as priority on Global_Ticket.Priority = priority.Id and priority.RefrencedId = @RefIdPriority
	where     (@DateTimeTo is null or @DateTimeTo >= Global_Ticket.DateM)
		  and (@DateTimeFrom is null or @DateTimeFrom <= Global_Ticket.DateM)
		  and (@RefIdPriority is null or @RefIdPriority = priority.RefrencedId)
		  and (@RefIdStatus is null or @RefIdStatus = status.RefrencedId)
END


GO

-- ==========================================================================================
IF not EXISTS (SELECT * FROM sys.views WHERE object_id = OBJECT_ID(N'[dbo].[ViewProductGroupRelation]'))
	exec('create VIEW [dbo].[ViewProductGroupRelation] as select * from Acc_Product')
go

ALTER VIEW [dbo].[ViewProductGroupRelation]
AS
	select    Guid
			, PrdName
			, case when MaxOrdinal=Rank then GroupName else GroupName+'*' end as GroupName
			, GroupGuid
			, Rank
	from
	(
		select    Acc_Product.Guid
				, Acc_Product.Name As PrdName
				, Acc_ProductGroup.Name As GroupName
				, Acc_ProductGroupRelation.GroupGuid
				, RANK() over (partition by acc_product.Guid order by Ordinal desc) As Rank
				, max(Ordinal) as MaxOrdinal
		from Acc_Product
		inner join Acc_ProductGroupRelation on Acc_ProductGroupRelation.ProductGuid=Acc_Product.Guid
		inner join Acc_ProductGroup on Acc_ProductGroup.Guid=Acc_ProductGroupRelation.GroupGuid
		group by  Acc_Product.Guid
				, Acc_Product.Name 
				, Acc_ProductGroup.Name 
				, Acc_ProductGroupRelation.GroupGuid
				, Acc_ProductGroupRelation.Ordinal
	) as temp 
	where temp.Rank<=1
GO

IF Not EXISTS (SELECT * FROM dbo.sysobjects WHERE id = OBJECT_ID(N'[Acc_Pardakht_UpdateDateMoaser]') AND OBJECTPROPERTY(id, N'IsProcedure') = 1)
	exec('Create PROCEDURE [dbo].[Acc_Pardakht_UpdateDateMoaser] AS BEGIN	print 111 	end')
GO

ALTER PROCEDURE [dbo].[Acc_Pardakht_UpdateDateMoaser]
	@Guid uniqueidentifier=null
AS
BEGIN
	update Acc_Pardakht
	set DateMoaser=temp.AffectiveDay
	from 
	(
		select		dateadd(day,round(
									SUM(Price*DATEDIFF(DAY, PardakhtDate,AffectiveDate))/SUM(Price)
									,0
								 ),PardakhtDate) AS AffectiveDay
				,PardakhtDate
				,PardakhtGuid
		from 
		(
			select	  Acc_Pardakht.Guid as PardakhtGuid
					, cast(Acc_PardakhtCheckSh.DateSaresid as date) as AffectiveDate
					, cast(Acc_Pardakht.Date as date) as PardakhtDate
					, Acc_PardakhtCheckSh.Price
			from Acc_Pardakht
			inner join Acc_PardakhtCheckSh on Acc_PardakhtCheckSh.MasterGuid=Acc_Pardakht.Guid
			where ((ISNULL(@Guid,'00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000')='00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000') or Acc_Pardakht.Guid=@Guid)

			union all

			select	  Acc_Pardakht.Guid as PardakhtGuid
					, cast(Acc_ReceptionCheck.Date as date) as AffectiveDate
					, cast(Acc_Pardakht.Date as date) as PardakhtDate
					, Acc_ReceptionCheck.Price
			from Acc_Pardakht
			inner join Acc_PardakhtCheckM on Acc_PardakhtCheckM.MasterGuid=Acc_Pardakht.Guid
			inner join Acc_ReceptionCheck on Acc_ReceptionCheck.Guid=Acc_PardakhtCheckM.GuidCheck
			where ((ISNULL(@Guid,'00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000')='00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000') or Acc_Pardakht.Guid=@Guid)

			union all

			select	  Acc_Pardakht.Guid as PardakhtGuid
					, cast(Acc_PardakhtBank.DateM as date) as AffectiveDate
					, cast(Acc_Pardakht.Date as date) as PardakhtDate
					, Acc_PardakhtBank.Price
			from Acc_Pardakht
			inner join Acc_PardakhtBank on Acc_PardakhtBank.MasterGuid=Acc_Pardakht.Guid
			where ((ISNULL(@Guid,'00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000')='00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000') or Acc_Pardakht.Guid=@Guid)

			union all

			select	  Acc_Pardakht.Guid as PardakhtGuid
					, cast(Acc_PardakhtNaghd.DateM as date) as AffectiveDate
					, cast(Acc_Pardakht.Date as date) as PardakhtDate
					, Acc_PardakhtNaghd.Price
			from Acc_Pardakht
			inner join Acc_PardakhtNaghd on Acc_PardakhtNaghd.MasterGuid=Acc_Pardakht.Guid
			where ((ISNULL(@Guid,'00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000')='00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000') or Acc_Pardakht.Guid=@Guid)

			union all

			select    Acc_SanadDet.AsGuid as PardakhtGuid
					, cast(Acc_Pardakht.Date as date) as AffectiveDate
					, cast(Acc_Pardakht.Date as date) as PardakhtDate
					, Acc_SanadDet.Debit as Price
			from Acc_Pardakht
			inner join Acc_SanadDet on Acc_Pardakht.Guid=Acc_SanadDet.AsGuid and Acc_SanadDet.AsType=2
			where Acc_SanadDet.Debit>0 and Acc_Pardakht.TafsilGuid<>Acc_SanadDet.TafsilGuid
					and ((ISNULL(@Guid,'00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000')='00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000') or Acc_Pardakht.Guid=@Guid)
			union all

			select    Acc_SanadDet.AsGuid as PardakhtGuid
					, cast(Acc_Pardakht.Date as date) as AffectiveDate
					, cast(Acc_Pardakht.Date as date) as PardakhtDate
					, -Acc_SanadDet.Credit as Price
			from Acc_Pardakht
			inner join Acc_SanadDet on Acc_Pardakht.Guid=Acc_SanadDet.AsGuid and Acc_SanadDet.AsType=2
			where Acc_SanadDet.Credit>0 and Acc_Pardakht.TafsilGuid<>Acc_SanadDet.TafsilGuid 
					and ((ISNULL(@Guid,'00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000')='00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000') or Acc_Pardakht.Guid=@Guid)
		) as ReceptionAffectiveDate
		group by PardakhtGuid,PardakhtDate
		having SUM(Price)>0
	) as temp 
	inner join Acc_Pardakht on temp.PardakhtGuid = Acc_Pardakht.Guid
	where (ISNULL(@Guid,'00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000')='00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000' and (DateMoaser is null or DateMoaser<>temp.AffectiveDay))
		  or Guid=@Guid
END
Go

Alter PROCEDURE sp_DepartmentEntitySummery
AS
BEGIN
	declare @AghsatMCount int

	declare @TafsilGroupCount int

	declare @UserCount int

	declare @ProductGroupCount int
	declare @ProductVahedCount int
	declare @FileInfoCount int	
	declare @IntroductionMethodCount int

	declare @AghsatMMinModified datetime
	declare @AghsatShMinModified datetime
	declare @ChequeMMinModified datetime
	declare @ChequeShMinModified datetime
	declare @CustomerMinModified datetime
	declare @CustomerGroupMinModified datetime
	declare @CustomerTitleMinModified datetime
	declare @KolMinModified datetime
	declare @MoeinMinModified datetime
	declare @TafsilMinModified datetime
	declare @TafsilGroupMinModified datetime
	declare @LastPriceMinModified datetime
	declare @UserMinModified datetime
	declare @ProductMinModified datetime
	declare @ProductGroupMinModified datetime
	declare @ProductVahedMinModified datetime
	declare @FileInfoMinModified datetime
	declare @IntroductionMethodMinModified datetime

	select @AghsatMCount = Count(*) 
		 , @AghsatMMinModified = MIN(Modified)
	from Acc_AghsatAshkhas

	from Acc_AghsatShakhsi

	from Acc_ReceptionCheck

	from Acc_PardakhtCheckSh

		 , @CustomerMinModified = MIN(Modified)
	from Acc_Customer

	from Acc_CustomerGroup

	from Acc_ProductVahed

	select @FileInfoCount = Count(*)
		 , @FileInfoMinModified = MIN(Modified)
	from Acc_FileInfo
	
	select	@IntroductionMethodCount = Count(*),
			@IntroductionMethodMinModified = MIN(Modified)
	from	Global_IntroductionMethod

	select    @AghsatMCount As AghsatMCount
			, @AghsatMMinModified As AghsatMMinModified

			, @ChequeShCount As ChequeShCount
			, @ChequeShMinModified As ChequeShMinModified
			, @CustomerCount As CustomerCount
			, @CustomerMinModified As CustomerMinModified
			, @CustomerGroupCount As CustomerGroupCount
			, @CustomerGroupMinModified As CustomerGroupMinModified
			, @CustomerTitleCount As CustomerTitleCount
			, @CustomerTitleMinModified As CustomerTitleMinModified
			, @KolCount As KolCount
			, @KolMinModified As KolMinModified
			, @MoeinCount As MoeinCount
			, @MoeinMinModified As MoeinMinModified
			, @TafsilCount As TafsilCount
			, @TafsilMinModified As TafsilMinModified
			, @TafsilGroupCount As TafsilGroupCount
			, @TafsilGroupMinModified As TafsilGroupMinModified
			, @LastPriceCount As LastPriceCount
			, @LastPriceMinModified As LastPriceMinModified
			, @UserCount As UserCount
			, @UserMinModified As UserMinModified

ALTER PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_Global_Sarnakh_GetAll]
	@Status bit=null,
	@CashierGuid uniqueidentifier=null,
	@StatusId int=null,
	@IntroductionGuid uniqueidentifier=null,
	@DateFrom Datetime=null,
	@DateTo Datetime=null
AS
BEGIN
	select    Global_SarNakh.*
			, ISNULL(Acc_Cashier.Name,'') As CashierName
			, ISNULL(Acc_SanadStatusReport.Name,'') As StatusName
			, ISNULL(Global_IntroductionMethod.Name,'') As IntroName
			, Case when ISNULL(Acc_Tafsil.Guid,'00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000') = '00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000' then 'false' else 'true' end as IsLinked
	from Global_SarNakh
	inner join Acc_Cashier on Acc_Cashier.Guid=Global_SarNakh.CashierGuid
	left outer join Acc_SanadStatusReport on Acc_SanadStatusReport.Id=Global_SarNakh.StatusId
	left outer join Global_IntroductionMethod on Global_IntroductionMethod.Guid=Global_SarNakh.IntroductionGuid
	left outer join Acc_Tafsil on Acc_Tafsil.Guid=Global_SarNakh.Guid
	where (@Status is null or Global_SarNakh.Status=@Status) and
		  (ISNULL(@CashierGuid,'00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000')='00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000' or Global_SarNakh.CashierGuid=@CashierGuid) and
		  (@StatusId is null or Global_SarNakh.StatusId=@StatusId) and
		  (ISNULL(@IntroductionGuid,'00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000')='00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000' or Global_SarNakh.IntroductionGuid=@IntroductionGuid) and
		  (@DateFrom is null or Global_SarNakh.CreationDate>=@DateFrom) and
		  (@DateTo is null or Global_SarNakh.CreationDate<=@DateTo)
END
GO

AS

	from Acc_Moein

	inner join Acc_HesabGroup on Acc_HesabGroup.Guid=Acc_Kol.GroupGuid
	where StimulQuery.RefrencedId=@RefrenceId and Acc_HesabGroup.TypeGuid='ed6566ab-12a0-46ec-b1f4-409446798b8f'
END

IF Not EXISTS (SELECT * FROM dbo.sysobjects WHERE id = OBJECT_ID(N'[sp_Acc_AnbarGardani_GetEkhtetamRecords]') AND OBJECTPROPERTY(id, N'IsProcedure') = 1)

ALTER PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_Acc_AnbarGardani_GetEkhtetamRecords]
	@DigitAfterPoint smallint
AS
BEGIN
	select    Acc_PrdAnbarRelation.Amount
			, Acc_PrdAnbarRelation.AnbarGuid As DepotGuid
			, Acc_Product.IdProduct
			, Acc_Depot.Name As DepotName
			, Acc_PrdAnbarRelation.PrdGuid As ProductGuid
			, Acc_Product.Name As ProductName
			, Acc_ProductVahed.Name As UnitName
			, ISNULL(ROUND(Kardex.RemUnitPrice,@DigitAfterPoint),0) As UnitPrice
			, ISNULL(Acc_ProductGroupRelation.GroupGuid,'00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000') as GuidGroup
	from Acc_PrdAnbarRelation
	inner join Acc_Product on Acc_Product.Guid=Acc_PrdAnbarRelation.PrdGuid
	inner join Acc_Depot on Acc_Depot.Guid=Acc_PrdAnbarRelation.AnbarGuid
	inner join Acc_ProductVahed on Acc_ProductVahed.Guid=Acc_Product.GuidVahed
	left outer join Acc_ProductGroupRelation on Acc_Product.Guid=Acc_ProductGroupRelation.ProductGuid and Acc_ProductGroupRelation.Ordinal=1
	inner join
	(
		select    *
				, RANK() over (partition by ProductGuid,DepotGuid order by DateM desc, Ordinal, Guid) As Rank
		from Acc_Kardex
	) As Kardex on Kardex.ProductGuid=Acc_PrdAnbarRelation.PrdGuid and Kardex.DepotGuid=Acc_PrdAnbarRelation.AnbarGuid
	where Acc_Product.Status='true' and
		  (Kardex.Guid is null or Kardex.Rank=1) and 
		  Acc_PrdAnbarRelation.Amount<>0 and
		  Acc_Product.Kind<>3
END
GO

ALTER PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_Acc_ViewSanadDetail_GetAllExistRecord]
AS
BEGIN
	select distinct AsGuid,AsType
	from Acc_ViewSanadDetail
	where AsType not in (1,2,50,60)

	union all

	select distinct   Acc_DepotReceipt.Guid As AsGuid
					, Case when Acc_DepotReceipt.Type=1 then 209
						   else 210
					  end as AsType
	from Acc_ViewSanadDetail
	inner join Acc_DepotReceiptDetail on Acc_DepotReceiptDetail.ReferenceGuid=Acc_ViewSanadDetail.Guid
	inner join Acc_DepotReceipt on Acc_DepotReceipt.Guid=Acc_DepotReceiptDetail.MasterGuid

	union all

	select    Guid as AsGuid
			, 24 as AsType
	from Acc_PrdAnbarGardani
	where Type=1
END
GO

ALTER VIEW [dbo].[View_PrdTechnical]

                             (SELECT        ProductGuid, Name AS t2Name
                               FROM            dbo.Acc_PrdTechnical AS Acc_PrdTechnical_7
                               WHERE        (TechnicalType = 2)) AS t2 ON t2.ProductGuid = dbo.Acc_Product.Guid LEFT OUTER JOIN
                             (SELECT        ProductGuid, Name AS t3Name
                               FROM            dbo.Acc_PrdTechnical AS Acc_PrdTechnical_6
                               WHERE        (TechnicalType = 3)) AS t3 ON t3.ProductGuid = dbo.Acc_Product.Guid LEFT OUTER JOIN
                             (SELECT        ProductGuid, Name AS t4Name
                               FROM            dbo.Acc_PrdTechnical AS Acc_PrdTechnical_5
                               WHERE        (TechnicalType = 4)) AS t4 ON t4.ProductGuid = dbo.Acc_Product.Guid LEFT OUTER JOIN
                             (SELECT        ProductGuid, Name AS t5Name
                               FROM            dbo.Acc_PrdTechnical AS Acc_PrdTechnical_4
                               WHERE        (TechnicalType = 5)) AS t5 ON t5.ProductGuid = dbo.Acc_Product.Guid LEFT OUTER JOIN
                             (SELECT        ProductGuid, Name AS t6Name
                               FROM            dbo.Acc_PrdTechnical AS Acc_PrdTechnical_3
                               WHERE        (TechnicalType = 6)) AS t6 ON t6.ProductGuid = dbo.Acc_Product.Guid LEFT OUTER JOIN
                             (SELECT        ProductGuid, Name AS t7Name
                               FROM            dbo.Acc_PrdTechnical AS Acc_PrdTechnical_2
                               WHERE        (TechnicalType = 7)) AS t7 ON t7.ProductGuid = dbo.Acc_Product.Guid LEFT OUTER JOIN
                             (SELECT        ProductGuid, Name AS t8Name
                               FROM            dbo.Acc_PrdTechnical AS Acc_PrdTechnical_1
                               WHERE        (TechnicalType = 8)) AS t8 ON t8.ProductGuid = dbo.Acc_Product.Guid
go

	exec('Create PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_Acc_AghsatM_DelayedInstallments] AS BEGIN	print 111 	end')
GO

ALTER PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_Acc_AghsatM_DelayedInstallments]
	@MaxDelayDayCount int,
	@dateM dateTime,
	@PenaltyPercent smallint
AS
BEGIN
	select    Temp.*
			, CAST(ROUND((((Price*@PenaltyPercent)/100)*Temp.DelayDayCount)/30,3) as decimal(18,3)) as PenaltyPrice
			, Acc_Tafsil.Code
			, Acc_Tafsil.Name
	from
	(
		select    Acc_AghsatAshkhas.Guid
				, VajhEltezam
				, DateSarResid
				, DATEADD(DAY, @MaxDelayDayCount+1, CAST(DateSarResid as Date)) as EltezamStartDate
				, DATEDIFF(DAY, DATEADD(DAY, @MaxDelayDayCount, CAST(DateSarResid as Date)),GETDATE()) DelayDayCount
				, TafsilGuid
				, MoeinGuid
				, Id2
				, Description
				, Price
				, Acc_AghsatAshkhas.SanadNo
				, VajhEltezamModified
		from Acc_AghsatAshkhas
		inner join Acc_AghsatM_Group on Acc_AghsatM_Group.Guid=Acc_AghsatAshkhas.GuidGroup
		where (VajhEltezamModified is null or VajhEltezamModified<=@dateM) and
			  DateSarResid<=@dateM and
			  ISNULL(ReceptionGuid,'00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000')='00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000'
	) as Temp
	inner join Acc_Tafsil on Acc_Tafsil.Guid=Temp.TafsilGuid
	where DelayDayCount>0 and
		  CAST(ROUND((((Price*@PenaltyPercent)/100)*Temp.DelayDayCount)/30,3) as decimal(18,3)) > 0 
END
Go

ALTER PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_Reservation_GetAll]
	@Status bit=null,
	@DateFrom datetime=null,
	@DateTo datetime=null,
	@TafsilGuid uniqueidentifier=null,
	@CashierGuid uniqueidentifier=null,
	@NumberFrom int=0,
	@NumberTo int=0
AS
BEGIN
	select    Acc_Reservation.Guid
			, Acc_Reservation.CashierGuid
			, Acc_Cashier.Name As CashierName
			, Acc_Reservation.DateM
			, Acc_Reservation.Description
			, Acc_Reservation.OrderGuid
			, Acc_Reservation.ReceptionGuid
			, ISNULL(MasterOrder.TotalSum,0) + Sum(ISNULL(DetailOrder.TotalSum,0)) As OrderSum
			, ISNULL(Acc_Reception.Sum,0) As ReceptionSum
			, Acc_Reservation.TafsilGuid
			, Acc_Tafsil.Name As TafsilName
			, Acc_Reservation.Number
	from Acc_Reservation
	inner join Acc_ReservationDetail on Acc_Reservation.Guid=Acc_ReservationDetail.MasterGuid
	inner join Acc_Cashier on Acc_Cashier.Guid=Acc_Reservation.CashierGuid
	inner join Acc_Tafsil on Acc_Tafsil.Guid=Acc_Reservation.TafsilGuid
	left outer join Acc_Order As MasterOrder on MasterOrder.Guid=Acc_Reservation.OrderGuid
	left outer join Acc_Order As DetailOrder on DetailOrder.Guid=Acc_ReservationDetail.OrderGuid
	left outer join Acc_Reception on Acc_Reception.Guid=Acc_Reservation.ReceptionGuid
	where (@Status is null or Acc_Reservation.Status=@Status) and
		  (@DateFrom is null or Acc_Reservation.DateM>=@DateFrom) and
		  (@DateTo is null or Acc_Reservation.DateM<=@DateTo) and
		  (ISNULL(@TafsilGuid,'00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000')='00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000' or Acc_Reservation.TafsilGuid=@TafsilGuid) and
		  (ISNULL(@CashierGuid,'00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000')='00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000' or Acc_Reservation.CashierGuid=@CashierGuid) and
		  (@NumberFrom=0 or Acc_Reservation.Number>=@NumberFrom) and
		  (@NumberTo=0 or Acc_Reservation.Number<=@NumberTo)
	group by  Acc_Reservation.Guid
			, Acc_Reservation.CashierGuid
			, Acc_Cashier.Name
			, Acc_Reservation.DateM
			, Acc_Reservation.Description
			, Acc_Reservation.OrderGuid
			, Acc_Reservation.ReceptionGuid
			, Acc_Reservation.TafsilGuid
			, Acc_Tafsil.Name
			, MasterOrder.TotalSum
			, Acc_Reception.Sum
			, Acc_Reservation.Number
END
GO

GO

ALTER PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_Acc_Placement_Report] 
	@PlacementGuid uniqueidentifier,
	@TafsilGuid uniqueidentifier=null,
	@CashierGuid uniqueidentifier=null,
	@NumberFrom int=0,
	@NumberTo int=0,
	@DateFrom datetime=null,
	@DateTo datetime=null,
	@PeopleCountFrom int=0,
	@PeopleCountTo int=0,
	@SumFrom decimal(18,3)=0,
	@SumTo decimal(18,3)=0
AS
BEGIN
	select *
	from
	(
		select    Acc_OpenInvoice.Guid As MasterGuid
				, Acc_OpenInvoice.DateM As StartDate
				, Acc_OpenInvoice.TafsilGuid
				, Acc_OpenInvoice.CashierGuid
				, Acc_OpenInvoice.Description
				, Acc_OpenInvoice.Sum
				, Acc_OpenInvoice.PeopleCount
				, Acc_OpenInvoice.Number
				, Acc_OpenInvoice.SettlementDate As EndDate
				, Acc_OpenInvoice.ReceptionGuid
				, Acc_Tafsil.Name As TafsilName
				, Acc_Cashier.Name As CashierName
				, ISNULL(Acc_Reception.Sum,0) As ReceptionSum
				, 0 As State
		from Acc_OpenInvoicePlacementRelation
		inner join Acc_OpenInvoice on Acc_OpenInvoice.Guid=Acc_OpenInvoicePlacementRelation.InvoiceGuid
		inner join Acc_Tafsil on Acc_Tafsil.Guid=Acc_OpenInvoice.TafsilGuid
		inner join Acc_Cashier on Acc_Cashier.Guid=Acc_OpenInvoice.CashierGuid
		left outer join Acc_Reception on Acc_Reception.Guid=Acc_OpenInvoice.ReceptionGuid and Acc_Reception.Sum>0
		where PlacementGuid=@PlacementGuid

		union All

		select    Acc_Reservation.Guid As MasterGuid
				, Acc_ReservationDetail.StartDate
				, Acc_Reservation.TafsilGuid
				, Acc_Reservation.CashierGuid
				, Acc_Reservation.Description
				, 0 As Sum
				, Acc_ReservationDetail.PeopleCount As PeopleCount
				, Acc_Reservation.Number As Number
				, Acc_ReservationDetail.EndDate
				, Acc_Reservation.ReceptionGuid
				, Acc_Tafsil.Name As TafsilName
				, Acc_Cashier.Name As CashierName
				, ISNULL(Acc_Reception.Sum,0) As ReceptionSum
				, 2 As State
		from Acc_ReservationDetail
		inner join Acc_Reservation on Acc_Reservation.Guid=Acc_ReservationDetail.MasterGuid
		inner join Acc_Tafsil on Acc_Tafsil.Guid=Acc_Reservation.TafsilGuid
		inner join Acc_Cashier on Acc_Cashier.Guid=Acc_Reservation.CashierGuid
		left outer join Acc_Reception on Acc_Reception.Guid=Acc_Reservation.ReceptionGuid and Acc_Reception.Sum>0
		where PlacementGuid=@PlacementGuid
	) As Temp
	where (ISNULL(@TafsilGuid,'00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000')='00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000' or Temp.TafsilGuid=@TafsilGuid) and
		  (ISNULL(@CashierGuid,'00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000')='00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000' or Temp.CashierGuid=@CashierGuid) and
		  (ISNULL(@NumberFrom,0)=0 or Temp.Number>=@NumberFrom) and
		  (ISNULL(@NumberTo,0)=0 or Temp.Number<=@NumberTo) and
		  (@DateFrom is null or Temp.StartDate>=@DateFrom) and
		  (@DateTo is null or Temp.StartDate<=@DateTo) and
		  (ISNULL(@PeopleCountFrom,0)=0 or Temp.PeopleCount>=@PeopleCountFrom) and
		  (ISNULL(@PeopleCountTo,0)=0 or Temp.PeopleCount<=@PeopleCountTo) and
		  (ISNULL(@SumFrom,0)=0 or Temp.Sum>=@SumFrom) and
		  (ISNULL(@SumTo,0)=0 or Temp.Sum<=@SumTo)
END
GO

GO

Alter PROCEDURE sp_Acc_ProductPriceRelation_UpdateRegularPrice
	@PriceLevelGuid uniqueIdentifier,
	@RegularPriceGuid uniqueIdentifier
AS
BEGIN
	--هم 8 داره و هم 9
	update Acc_ProductPriceRelation
	set Price=Temp.Price
	from Acc_ProductPriceRelation
	inner join 
	(
		select Price,ProductGuid
		from Acc_ProductPriceRelation
		where PriceLevelGuid=@PriceLevelGuid
	) as Temp on Temp.ProductGuid=Acc_ProductPriceRelation.ProductGuid
	where PriceLevelGuid=@RegularPriceGuid and
		  Acc_ProductPriceRelation.Price<>Temp.Price

	--سطح 8 داره ولی 9 نداره
	INSERT INTO [dbo].[Acc_ProductPriceRelation]
			   ([Guid]
			   ,[Modified]
			   ,[ProductGuid]
			   ,[Price]
			   ,[Quantity]
			   ,[Profit]
			   ,[KasrRial]
			   ,[PriceLevelGuid]
			   ,[UnitGuid]
			   ,[ServerStatus]
			   ,[DeliveryDate]
			   ,[DeliveryId]
			   ,[DeliveryMessage]
			   ,[DeliveryShortMessage]
			   ,[HostRefrenced]
			   ,[Type])
	select    NEWID()
			, GETDATE()
			, Acc_ProductPriceRelation.ProductGuid
			, Acc_ProductPriceRelation.Price
			, 0
			, 0
			, 0
			, @RegularPriceGuid
			, null
			, 0
			, null
			, 0
			, ''
			, ''
			, ''
			, 0
	from Acc_ProductPriceRelation
	left outer join 
	(
		select Price,ProductGuid
		from Acc_ProductPriceRelation
		where PriceLevelGuid=@RegularPriceGuid
	) as Regular on Regular.ProductGuid=Acc_ProductPriceRelation.ProductGuid
	where PriceLevelGuid=@PriceLevelGuid and
		  Regular.ProductGuid is null
END
GO

Alter PROCEDURE sp_Acc_Product_GetDiscountModel
AS
BEGIN
	select    Acc_Product.Guid
			, IdProduct
			, Acc_Product.Name
			, DiscountPercent
			, KardexPrice
			, Acc_ProductVahed.Name As UnitName
			, ISNULL(Acc_ProductPriceRelation.Price,0) As Price

	exec('Create PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_Acc_Barcode_FillBarcode] AS BEGIN	print 111 	end')
GO

ALTER PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_Acc_Barcode_FillBarcode]
AS
BEGIN
	DECLARE @Guid uniqueidentifier
	DECLARE @IdProduct NVARCHAR(500)  
	DECLARE @Code bigint
  
	DECLARE CR CURSOR 
	FOR select Guid,Code,IdProduct
	  from Acc_Product
	  where IdProduct<>CAST(Code as nvarchar(200))
	OPEN CR
	FETCH NEXT FROM CR INTO @Guid,@Code,@IdProduct
	WHILE @@FETCH_STATUS=0
	BEGIN
	  declare @OldBarcode nvarchar(500)
	  set @OldBarcode =(select BarCode from Acc_Barcode where BarCode=@IdProduct)

	  if(ISNULL(@OldBarcode,'')='')
	  begin
		declare @MaxOrdinal int
		select @MaxOrdinal=MAX(Ordinal)
		from Acc_Barcode
		where PrdGuid=@Guid

		set @MaxOrdinal=ISNULL(@MaxOrdinal,0)
		set @MaxOrdinal=@MaxOrdinal+1

		INSERT INTO [dbo].[Acc_Barcode]
			   ([BarCode]
			   ,[PrdGuid]
			   ,[CreateDate]
			   ,[DepotGuid]
			   ,[Description]
			   ,[Name]
			   ,[Price]
			   ,[Quantity]
			   ,[Type]
			   ,[MetaData]
			   ,[Ordinal]
			   ,[PriceTypeGuid])
		 VALUES
			   (@IdProduct
			   ,@Guid
			   ,GETDATE()
			   ,null
			   ,''
			   ,''
			   ,0
			   ,1
			   ,1
			   ,''
			   ,@MaxOrdinal
			   ,null)
	  end
	  FETCH NEXT FROM CR INTO @Guid,@Code,@IdProduct
	END
	CLOSE CR
	DEALLOCATE CR
END
GO

IF not EXISTS (SELECT * FROM sys.views WHERE object_id = OBJECT_ID(N'[dbo].[View_ProductPrice]'))
	exec('create VIEW [dbo].[View_ProductPrice] as select * from Acc_Product')
go

ALTER VIEW [dbo].[View_ProductPrice]
AS 
	SELECT    pivot_table.Guid
			, pivot_table.[0_1] As Sell1
			, pivot_table.[0_2] As Sell2
			, pivot_table.[0_3] As Sell3
			, pivot_table.[0_4] As Sell4
			, pivot_table.[0_5] As Sell5
			, pivot_table.[0_6] As Sell6
			, pivot_table.[0_7] As Sell7
			, pivot_table.[0_8] As Sell8
			, pivot_table.[0_9] As RegularPrice
			, pivot_table.[1_10] As Buy1
			, pivot_table.[1_11] As Buy2
			, pivot_table.[1_12] As Buy3
			, pivot_table.[1_13] As LastBuy
	FROM   
	(
		SELECT	  Acc_Product.Guid			 
				, ISNULL(Acc_ProductPriceRelation.Price,0) As Price
				, CAST(ISNULL(Acc_ProductPriceLevel.Type,-1) as nvarchar(10)) + '_' + CAST(ISNULL(Acc_ProductPriceLevel.Ordinal,-1) as nvarchar(10)) As PriceType
		FROM [Dbo].[Acc_Product] 
		left outer join Acc_ProductPriceRelation on Acc_ProductPriceRelation.ProductGuid=Acc_Product.Guid
		left outer join Acc_ProductPriceLevel on Acc_ProductPriceLevel.Guid=Acc_ProductPriceRelation.PriceLevelGuid and Acc_ProductPriceLevel.IsDefault='false'
	) p 
	PIVOT(
		Max(Price) 
		FOR PriceType IN ([0_1],[0_2],[0_3],[0_4],[0_5],[0_6],[0_7],[0_8],[0_9],[1_10],[1_11],[1_12],[1_13])
	) AS pivot_table
GO

IF Not EXISTS (SELECT * FROM dbo.sysobjects WHERE id = OBJECT_ID(N'[sp_Acc_Evaluation_GetDetails]') AND OBJECTPROPERTY(id, N'IsProcedure') = 1)
	exec('Create PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_Acc_Evaluation_GetDetails] AS BEGIN	print 111 	end')
GO

ALTER Procedure [dbo].[sp_Acc_Evaluation_GetDetails]
	@MasterGuid uniqueidentifier=null,
	@DateM datetime
As
Begin
	Declare @Kardex Table 
	(
		ProductGuid Uniqueidentifier,
		Quantity float,
		UnitPrice decimal(18,3),
		NewPrice decimal(18,3)
		UNIQUE CLUSTERED (ProductGuid)
	)

	insert Into @Kardex 
	select ProductGuid,
		   RemQT,
		   RemUnitPrice,
		   0
	from
	(
		select *
			, rank() over (partition By ProductGuid order by RemTotalPrice desc,newId()) as rank
		from
		(
			select  ProductGuid,
					RemQT,
					RemUnitPrice,
					RemTotalPrice
			from 
			(
				Select    ProductGuid
						, RemQT
						, RemUnitPrice
						, RemTotalPrice
						, rank() over (partition By ProductGuid,DepotGuid order by DateM desc,Ordinal desc,newId()) as rank
					From Acc_Kardex 
					where DateM<@DateM
			) as rankedItems
			where rankedItems.rank=1 And
				  RemQT>0
		) as Temp
	) As MaxTotalPrice
	Where rank=1

	if (@MasterGuid is not null)
	begin
		UPDATE @Kardex
		SET NewPrice = X.UnitPrice
		FROM 
		(
			SELECT PrdGuid,UnitPrice,QTEzafe
			from Acc_PrdAnbarGardaniDet
			inner join Acc_PrdAnbarGardani on Acc_PrdAnbarGardani.Guid=Acc_PrdAnbarGardaniDet.MasterGuid
			inner join Acc_Evaluation on Acc_Evaluation.AnbarGardaniEzafeGuid=Acc_PrdAnbarGardani.Guid
			where Acc_Evaluation.Guid=@MasterGuid
		) as X
		WHERE X.PrdGuid = [@Kardex].ProductGuid
	END

	select    Acc_Product.Guid As ProductGuid
			, Acc_Product.Name As ProductName
			, Acc_Product.IdProduct As ProductCode
			, Acc_ProductGroup.Guid As ProductGroupGuid
			, Acc_ProductGroup.ShortName As ProductGroupName
			, Acc_ProductVahed.Name As UnitName
			, LastEvaluation.DateM As LastDate
			, K.Quantity As LastQuantity
			, K.UnitPrice As LastUnitPrice
			, K.NewPrice
	from @Kardex As K
	inner join Acc_Product on Acc_Product.Guid=K.ProductGuid
	inner join Acc_ProductVahed on Acc_ProductVahed.Guid=Acc_Product.GuidVahed
	left outer join Acc_ProductGroupRelation on Acc_ProductGroupRelation.ProductGuid=Acc_Product.Guid and Acc_ProductGroupRelation.Ordinal=1
	left outer join Acc_ProductGroup on Acc_ProductGroup.Guid=Acc_ProductGroupRelation.GroupGuid
	left outer join
	(
		select    PrdGuid
				, UnitPrice
				, Acc_PrdAnbarGardani.DateM
				, rank() over (partition By PrdGuid order by Acc_PrdAnbarGardani.DateM desc) as Rank
		from Acc_PrdAnbarGardaniDet
		inner join Acc_PrdAnbarGardani on Acc_PrdAnbarGardani.Guid=Acc_PrdAnbarGardaniDet.MasterGuid
		inner join Acc_Evaluation on Acc_Evaluation.AnbarGardaniEzafeGuid=Acc_PrdAnbarGardani.Guid
	) As LastEvaluation on LastEvaluation.PrdGuid=K.ProductGuid and LastEvaluation.Rank=1
	Where Acc_Product.Status='true' and
		  K.Quantity>0 and 
		  Acc_Product.Kind<>3 
	order by Acc_Product.Name
End
GO

IF Not EXISTS (SELECT * FROM dbo.sysobjects WHERE id = OBJECT_ID(N'[sp_Acc_Coupon_SelectAll]') AND OBJECTPROPERTY(id, N'IsProcedure') = 1)
	exec('Create PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_Acc_Coupon_SelectAll] AS BEGIN	print 111 	end')
GO

ALTER PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_Acc_Coupon_SelectAll]
	@Type smallint=null,
	@Status bit=null,
	@DateFrom datetime=null,
	@DateTo datetime=null,
	@CouponCode nvarchar(500)='',
	@ServerStatus smallint=null,
	@CashierGuid uniqueidentifier=null
AS
BEGIN
	select    Acc_CouponDiscount.*
			, ISNULL(Usage.UseCount,0) as UsesCount 
			, ISNULL(Acc_Cashier.Name,'') as UserName
	from Acc_CouponDiscount
	inner join Acc_Cashier on Acc_Cashier.Guid=Acc_CouponDiscount.CashierGuid
	left outer join 
	(
		select CouponGuid,COUNT(*) As UseCount
		from dbo.VCouponUsages
		group by CouponGuid
	) As Usage on Usage.CouponGuid=Acc_CouponDiscount.Guid
	where (@Type is null or Type=@Type) and
		  (@Status is null or Acc_CouponDiscount.Status=@Status) and
		  (@DateFrom is null or DateFrom<=@DateFrom) and
		  (@DateTo is null or DateTo>=@DateTo) and
		  (ISNULL(@CouponCode,'')='' or Title=@CouponCode) and
		  (ISNULL(@ServerStatus,0)=0 or Acc_CouponDiscount.ServerStatus=@ServerStatus) and
		  (ISNULL(@CashierGuid,dbo.GuidEmpty())=dbo.GuidEmpty() or Acc_CouponDiscount.CashierGuid=@CashierGuid)
END
GO

--====================================================================================================================
IF Not EXISTS (SELECT * FROM dbo.sysobjects WHERE id = OBJECT_ID(N'[sp_Acc_Coupon_GetBySchedule]') AND OBJECTPROPERTY(id, N'IsProcedure') = 1)
	exec('Create PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_Acc_Coupon_GetBySchedule] AS BEGIN	print 111 	end')
GO

Alter PROCEDURE sp_Acc_Coupon_GetBySchedule
AS
BEGIN
	SELECT    Acc_CouponDiscount.Guid
	FROM Acc_CouponDiscount
	left outer join 
	(
		select CouponGuid,COUNT(*) As UseCount
		from dbo.VCouponUsages
		group by CouponGuid
	) As Usage on Usage.CouponGuid=Acc_CouponDiscount.Guid
	CROSS APPLY OPENJSON(metadata, '$.CouponSmsSettingsList') 
	WITH 
	(
		DayCount INT '$.DayCount'
	) AS CouponDetails
	WHERE NULLIF(MetaData, '') IS NOT NULL and
		  CouponDetails.DayCount IS NOT NULL and
		  CouponDetails.DayCount>0  and
		  DateFrom<=GETDATE() and
		  DateTo>=GETDATE() and 
		  Mode=0 and
		  Status='true' and
		  (Usage.CouponGuid is null or Usage.UseCount<Acc_CouponDiscount.CountCouponUse)
END
GO

	exec('CREATE FUNCTION [dbo].[GetAccount](@TafsilGuid uniqueidentifier) RETURNS decimal(18,3) AS BEGIN RETURN null end')
go

alter FUNCTION [dbo].[GetAccount] 
(
	@TafsilGuid uniqueidentifier
)
RETURNS Decimal(18,3)
AS
BEGIN
	declare @ret decimal(18,3)

	select	@ret=sum( Acc_Accounts.Account)
	from	Acc_Accounts inner join 
			Acc_Moein on Acc_Accounts.MoeinGuid=Acc_Moein.Guid inner join
			Acc_Kol on Acc_Moein.KolGuid=Acc_Kol.Guid inner join 
			Acc_HesabGroup on Acc_Kol.GroupGuid=Acc_HesabGroup.Guid --inner join 
			--Acc_HesabType on Acc_HesabType.Guid=Acc_HesabGroup.TypeGuid
	where	TafsilGuid=@TafsilGuid and
			Acc_HesabGroup.TypeGuid<>'048190E8-F37B-464B-A15A-4D035195B2F4'

	return isnull(@ret,0)
END
GO

ALTER FUNCTION [dbo].[GuidEmpty]() RETURNS uniqueidentifier
AS
BEGIN
	DECLARE @Result uniqueidentifier
	SELECT @Result = '00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000'
	RETURN @Result
END
Go

		, ServerStatus = 0

	inner join Acc_Reception on Acc_Reception.Guid=Acc_AghsatAshkhas.ReceptionGuid
	where ISNULL(@ReceptionGuid,dbo.GuidEmpty())=dbo.GuidEmpty() or

END

	exec('Create PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_Acc_Order_GetRelatedBackSellSum] AS BEGIN	print 111 	end')
GO

Alter PROCEDURE sp_Acc_Order_GetRelatedBackSellSum
	@Guid uniqueidentifier
AS
BEGIN
	select Sum(ISNULL(BackSellDetails.RowSum,0)) As RelatedBackSellSum
	from Acc_Order
	inner join Acc_OrderDetail on Acc_OrderDetail.MasterGuid=Acc_Order.Guid
	left outer join Acc_OrderDetail As BackSellDetails on BackSellDetails.ReferenceGuid=Acc_OrderDetail.Guid
	where Acc_Order.Guid=@Guid
	group by Acc_Order.Guid
END
GO

	exec('Create PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_Acc_Barcode_GetAllConflict] AS BEGIN	print 111 	end')
GO

Alter PROCEDURE sp_Acc_Barcode_GetAllConflict
AS
BEGIN
	select    TempBarcode.*
			, Acc_Product.Name As ProductName
	from 
	(
		select   *
				, RANK() OVER (PARTITION BY PrdGuid ORDER BY Ordinal) As Rank 
		from Acc_Barcode
	) As TempBarcode
	inner join Acc_Product on Acc_Product.Guid=TempBarcode.PrdGuid
	where Rank<>Ordinal
END
GO

Alter PROCEDURE sp_Acc_SanadStatusReport_GetAllForForceUpdate
	@DateM datetime
AS
BEGIN
	select *
	from
	(
		select    Acc_Reception.Guid As SanadGuid
				, Acc_SanadStatusReport.AsType As SanadType
				, Acc_SanadStatusReport.Id As StatusId
				, Acc_Reception.Date As SanadDate
				, Acc_SanadStatusReport.InactivityDays
				, DATEADD(DAY, Acc_SanadStatusReport.InactivityDays, Acc_Reception.Date) as ActivityDate
				, Acc_SanadStatusReport.TargetStatusId
		from Acc_Reception
		inner join Acc_SanadStatusReport on Acc_SanadStatusReport.Id=Acc_Reception.StatusId and Acc_SanadStatusReport.AsType=1
		where Acc_SanadStatusReport.InactivityDays>0

		union all

		select    Acc_Pardakht.Guid As SanadGuid
				, Acc_SanadStatusReport.AsType As SanadType
				, Acc_SanadStatusReport.Id As StatusId
				, Acc_Pardakht.Date As SanadDate
				, Acc_SanadStatusReport.InactivityDays
				, DATEADD(DAY, Acc_SanadStatusReport.InactivityDays, Acc_Pardakht.Date) as ActivityDate
				, Acc_SanadStatusReport.TargetStatusId
		from Acc_Pardakht
		inner join Acc_SanadStatusReport on Acc_SanadStatusReport.Id=Acc_Pardakht.StatusId and Acc_SanadStatusReport.AsType=2
		where Acc_SanadStatusReport.InactivityDays>0

		union all

		select    Acc_Order.Guid As SanadGuid
				, Acc_SanadStatusReport.AsType As SanadType
				, Acc_SanadStatusReport.Id As StatusId
				, Acc_Order.Date As SanadDate
				, Acc_SanadStatusReport.InactivityDays
				, DATEADD(DAY, Acc_SanadStatusReport.InactivityDays, Acc_Order.Date) as ActivityDate
				, Acc_SanadStatusReport.TargetStatusId
		from Acc_Order
		inner join Acc_SanadStatusReport on Acc_SanadStatusReport.Id=Acc_Order.StatusId and Acc_SanadStatusReport.AsType=Acc_Order.FactorType
		where Acc_SanadStatusReport.InactivityDays>0

		union all

		select    Acc_Sanad.Guid As SanadGuid
				, Acc_SanadStatusReport.AsType As SanadType
				, Acc_SanadStatusReport.Id As StatusId
				, Acc_Sanad.DateM As SanadDate
				, Acc_SanadStatusReport.InactivityDays
				, DATEADD(DAY, Acc_SanadStatusReport.InactivityDays, Acc_Sanad.DateM) as ActivityDate
				, Acc_SanadStatusReport.TargetStatusId
		from Acc_Sanad
		inner join Acc_SanadStatusReport on Acc_SanadStatusReport.Id=Acc_Sanad.StatusId and Acc_SanadStatusReport.AsType=50
		where Acc_SanadStatusReport.InactivityDays>0

		union all

		select    Acc_PrdAnbarGardani.Guid As SanadGuid
				, Acc_SanadStatusReport.AsType As SanadType
				, Acc_SanadStatusReport.Id As StatusId
				, Acc_PrdAnbarGardani.DateM As SanadDate
				, Acc_SanadStatusReport.InactivityDays
				, DATEADD(DAY, Acc_SanadStatusReport.InactivityDays, Acc_PrdAnbarGardani.DateM) as ActivityDate
				, Acc_SanadStatusReport.TargetStatusId
		from Acc_PrdAnbarGardani
		inner join Acc_SanadStatusReport on Acc_SanadStatusReport.Id=Acc_PrdAnbarGardani.StatusId and Acc_SanadStatusReport.AsType=24
		where Acc_SanadStatusReport.InactivityDays>0

		union all

		select    Acc_Resid.Guid As SanadGuid
				, Acc_SanadStatusReport.AsType As SanadType
				, Acc_SanadStatusReport.Id As StatusId
				, Acc_Resid.DateM As SanadDate
				, Acc_SanadStatusReport.InactivityDays
				, DATEADD(DAY, Acc_SanadStatusReport.InactivityDays, Acc_Resid.DateM) as ActivityDate
				, Acc_SanadStatusReport.TargetStatusId
		from Acc_Resid
		inner join Acc_SanadStatusReport on Acc_SanadStatusReport.Id=Acc_Resid.StatusId and Acc_SanadStatusReport.AsType=203
		where Acc_SanadStatusReport.InactivityDays>0

		union all

		select    Acc_Resid.Guid As SanadGuid
				, Acc_SanadStatusReport.AsType As SanadType
				, Acc_SanadStatusReport.Id As StatusId
				, Acc_Resid.DateM As SanadDate
				, Acc_SanadStatusReport.InactivityDays
				, DATEADD(DAY, Acc_SanadStatusReport.InactivityDays, Acc_Resid.DateM) as ActivityDate
				, Acc_SanadStatusReport.TargetStatusId
		from Acc_Resid
		inner join Acc_SanadStatusReport on Acc_SanadStatusReport.Id=Acc_Resid.StatusId and Acc_SanadStatusReport.AsType=203
		where Acc_SanadStatusReport.InactivityDays>0
	) As Temp
	--where Temp.ActivityDate<=@DateM
END
GO

	exec('Create PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_Acc_PrdSerial_GetAllModel] AS BEGIN	print 111 	end')
GO

Alter PROCEDURE sp_Acc_PrdSerial_GetAllModel
	  @GroupGuid uniqueidentifier = null
	, @IsMojod bit = null
	, @GarrantyStartDate datetime = null
	, @GarrantyEndDate datetime = null
AS
BEGIN
	select *
	from
	(
		Select    ViewPrdSerial.Guid
				, ViewPrdSerial.Status
				, ViewPrdSerial.ProductGuid
				, ViewPrdSerial.Serial
				, ViewPrdSerial.GarrantyCode
				, ViewPrdSerial.StartDate
				, ViewPrdSerial.EndDate
				, Acc_Product.Name As ProductName
				, Acc_Product.Code As ProductCode
				, 1 ^ count(distinct nullif(ViewPrdSerial.IsMojod, 1)) As IsMojod
				, ViewPrdSerial.ServerStatus
				, ISNULL(ViewPrdSerial.TechnicalInfo1,'') as TechnicalInfo1
				, ISNULL(ViewPrdSerial.TechnicalInfo2,'') as TechnicalInfo2
				, ISNULL(ViewPrdSerial.TechnicalInfo3,'') as TechnicalInfo3
		from ViewPrdSerial
		inner join Acc_Product on Acc_Product.Guid=ViewPrdSerial.ProductGuid
		group by  ViewPrdSerial.Guid
				, ViewPrdSerial.Status
				, ViewPrdSerial.ProductGuid
				, ViewPrdSerial.Serial
				, ViewPrdSerial.GarrantyCode
				, ViewPrdSerial.StartDate
				, ViewPrdSerial.EndDate
				, Acc_Product.Name
				, Acc_Product.Code
				, ViewPrdSerial.ServerStatus
				, ViewPrdSerial.TechnicalInfo1
				, ViewPrdSerial.TechnicalInfo2
				, ViewPrdSerial.TechnicalInfo3
	) As Serials
	left outer join Acc_ProductGroupRelation on Acc_ProductGroupRelation.ProductGuid=Serials.ProductGuid
	where (@IsMojod is null or Serials.IsMojod=@IsMojod) and
		  (@GarrantyStartDate is null or Serials.StartDate>=@GarrantyStartDate) and
		  (@GarrantyEndDate is null or Serials.EndDate<=@GarrantyEndDate) and
		  (@GroupGuid is null or Acc_ProductGroupRelation.GroupGuid=@GroupGuid)
END
GO

-- ==========================================================================================
IF Not EXISTS (SELECT * FROM dbo.sysobjects WHERE id = OBJECT_ID(N'[sp_Acc_Product_SelectAttributeAmounts]') AND OBJECTPROPERTY(id, N'IsProcedure') = 1)
	exec('Create PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_Acc_Product_SelectAttributeAmounts] AS BEGIN	print 111 	end')
GO

ALTER PROCEDURE [dbo].[sp_Acc_Product_SelectAttributeAmounts]
    @MasterGuid UNIQUEIDENTIFIER,
    @RowAttr UNIQUEIDENTIFIER,
    @ColAttr UNIQUEIDENTIFIER
AS
BEGIN
SET NOCOUNT ON;
SET ANSI_WARNINGS OFF
DECLARE @cols NVARCHAR(MAX), @sql NVARCHAR(MAX);
DECLARE @RowAttrName NVARCHAR(200), @ColAttrName NVARCHAR(200);

-- گرفتن نام ویژگی ها برای نمایش در ستون و ردیف جدول
SELECT @RowAttrName = Name FROM Acc_ProductAttribute WHERE Guid = @RowAttr;
SELECT @ColAttrName = Name FROM Acc_ProductAttribute WHERE Guid = @ColAttr;

-- جمع موجودی هر محصول فرزند کالای مادر
;WITH ProductQty AS (
    SELECT PrdGuid, SUM(ISNULL(Amount, 0)) AS TotalQty
    FROM Acc_PrdAnbarRelation
    GROUP BY PrdGuid
),
-- استخراج ویژگی‌های هر محصول فرزند (با ترکیب نام ویژگی و مقدار)
Attrs AS (
    SELECT 
        p.Guid AS ProductGuid,
        MAX(CASE WHEN par.AttributeGuid = @RowAttr THEN CONCAT(attrib.Name, ' ', pav.Name) END) AS RowAttrValue,
        MAX(CASE WHEN par.AttributeGuid = @ColAttr THEN CONCAT(attrib.Name, ' ', pav.Name) END) AS ColAttrValue
    FROM Acc_Product p
    LEFT JOIN Acc_ProductAttributeRelation par ON p.Guid = par.ProductGuid
    LEFT JOIN Acc_ProductAttributeValue pav ON par.AttributeValueGuid = pav.Guid
    LEFT JOIN Acc_ProductAttribute attrib ON par.AttributeGuid = attrib.Guid
    WHERE p.MasterGuid = @MasterGuid and
		  p.Status='true' and
		  attrib.Status='true'
    GROUP BY p.Guid
),
-- جدول اولیه برای Pivot با مقادیر و موجودی
PivotData AS (
    SELECT 
        a.ProductGuid,
        a.RowAttrValue,
        a.ColAttrValue,
        COALESCE(q.TotalQty, 0) AS TotalQty
    FROM Attrs a
    LEFT JOIN ProductQty q ON a.ProductGuid = q.PrdGuid
)
SELECT * INTO #PivotData FROM PivotData;

-- استخراج لیست مقادیر ستون‌ها (مثلاً رنگ‌ها) که فقط در کالاهای فرزند موجودند
SELECT @cols = STRING_AGG(QUOTENAME(DistinctValues.Name), ',')
FROM (
    SELECT DISTINCT CONCAT(attrib.Name, ' ', pav.Name) AS Name
    FROM Acc_ProductAttributeValue pav
    INNER JOIN Acc_ProductAttributeRelation par ON pav.Guid = par.AttributeValueGuid
    INNER JOIN Acc_ProductAttribute attrib ON par.AttributeGuid = attrib.Guid
    INNER JOIN Acc_Product p ON par.ProductGuid = p.Guid
    WHERE p.MasterGuid = @MasterGuid AND 
		  par.AttributeGuid = @ColAttr and
		  p.Status='true' and
		  attrib.Status='true'
) AS DistinctValues;

-- ایجاد کوئری Pivot داینامیک و اجرا
SET @sql = N'
SELECT 
    RowAttrValue AS [' + ISNULL(@RowAttrName, 'RowAttribute') + '],
    ' + @cols + '
FROM (
    SELECT RowAttrValue, ColAttrValue, TotalQty
    FROM #PivotData
) AS src
PIVOT (
    SUM(TotalQty)
    FOR ColAttrValue IN (' + @cols + ')
) AS pv
ORDER BY [' + ISNULL(@RowAttrName, 'RowAttribute') + '];';

EXEC sp_executesql @sql;

DROP TABLE #PivotData;
END
GO
